### [1] [Radio Monitoring of the Changing-look AGN Mrk 590 I: VLA Observations Reveal A Bow-shock Driven Radio Brightening Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26243)
*Gregory Walsh,Marianne Vestergaard,Daniel Lawther,Sandra Raimundo,Jun Yi Koay*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过VLBA/VLA多频段（1-17 GHz）观测了变脸活动星系核（CLAGN）Mrk 590在2015至2017年四个历元的射电变化，发现其射电辐射由微弱致密成分主导，且射电变率由喷流与星际介质相互作用产生的弓形激波驱动，与更高能波段由吸积驱动的再增亮事件不相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mrk 590是一个典型的变脸活动星系核（CLAGN），其光学/紫外/X射线波段的变脸事件已得到充分监测，但射电波段对变脸事件的响应完全未知。理解射电与吸积驱动发射之间是否相关，对于揭示CLAGN的物理机制和多样性至关重要。

Method: 利用Karl G. Jansky甚大阵列（VLA）A构型在1-17 GHz多频段对Mrk 590进行四个历元（2015-2017年）的观测；识别射电谱的振幅和形状变化；采用贝叶斯推理建模程序来约束驱动射电变率的物理机制。

Result: 射电源由微弱的致密成分主导，无显著延展结构；射电谱在观测期间表现出振幅和形状的可变特征；贝叶斯建模倾向于支持由射电喷流与星际介质相互作用产生的弓形激波驱动射电增亮；射电变率与导致AGN在更高能波段再增亮的事件不相关。

Conclusion: Mrk 590的射电变率与吸积驱动的变脸事件缺乏相关性，这与在其他CLAGN中观测到的射电与吸积发射相关变率不同，展示了变脸活动星系核群体的多样性和驱动其行为的机制多样性。

Abstract: Mrk 590 is a changing-look AGN (CLAGN) that slowly faded from a Seyfert Type 1 to near quiescence over a few decades before re-igniting in 2017. Monitoring in the optical/UV/X-ray wavebands has revealed accretion-driven variability signatures, but the response of the radio source to the changing-look events that drove its fading and re-ignition is unknown. We present multi-frequency (1-17~GHz), A-configuration, Karl G. Jansky Very Large Array (VLA) observations of Mrk 590 over four epochs from 2015 to 2017. The radio source is dominated by a faint, compact component, absent of any notable extended structures. We identify a variable radio spectrum over this period, both in amplitude and in shape, and implement a Bayesian inference modeling routine to constrain the mechanism driving the radio variability. Our routine favors radio variability powered by a bow shock produced through an interaction of a radio jet with the interstellar medium, which drives the radio brightening in later epochs. This demonstrates that the radio variability we observed is not correlated with the variable accretion event that led to the re-brightening of the AGN at higher energy wavebands. While correlated variability between the radio and accretion-driven emission has been observed in other CLAGN, the lack of correlated variability in Mrk 590 shows the diversity of changing-look AGN populations and the mechanisms driving their behavior.

</details>


### [2] [Using CFHT's SITELLE to Probe the Long-Sought Supernova Remnant Shell in the Crab Nebula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26280)
*Lucas V. da Conceição,Janette Suherli,Samar Safi-Harb,Carter Rhea,Ivo R. Seitenzahl,Ashley J. Ruiter*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用CFHT的成像傅里叶变换光谱仪SITELLE，对蟹状星云进行了深场宽视场积分场光谱观测，搜索其前瞻激波（forward shock）。观测针对[Fe XIV]λ5303冠状线（激波加热气体的示踪物），覆盖脉冲星以西2.4–10 pc的两个11′×11′天区。结果显示在观测区域内未发现统计学显著的[Fe XIV]发射，并给出了保守的平均表面亮度上限≲3.79×10⁻¹⁷ erg cm⁻² s⁻¹ arcsec⁻²。结果与前瞻激波在极低密度介质中膨胀、激波气体弱或欠电离、或壳层位于观测区域之外等情景一致，展示了宽带光学积分场光谱学在约束超新星遗迹中微弱大尺度结构方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找蟹状星云中长期以来未被探测到的前瞻激波（forward shock），该激波作为超新星遗迹（SNR）外壳的标志，对于理解蟹状星云的演化环境和激波物理具有重要意义。

Method: 使用CFHT的SITELLE成像傅里叶变换光谱仪，在脉冲星以西2.4–10 pc范围内获取两个11′×11′天区的深场宽视场积分场光谱，针对[Fe XIV]λ5303冠状线发射进行全视场搜索，并在三个代表性环带上估算表面亮度上限。

Result: 在观测区域内未发现统计学显著的[Fe XIV]发射，得到保守的平均表面亮度上限≲3.79×10⁻¹⁷ erg cm⁻² s⁻¹ arcsec⁻²，这是迄今对可见星云之外蟹状星云假想SNR壳层冠状铁发射最深的宽区域光学约束。

Conclusion: 观测结果支持三种可能情景：前瞻激波在极低密度介质中膨胀（激波太弱）、激波气体欠电离、或壳层位于观测视场之外。该工作展示了宽视场光学积分场光谱学在约束SNR微弱大尺度结构方面的强大能力。

Abstract: We present deep, wide-field integral field spectroscopy of the Crab nebula obtained with the imaging Fourier transform spectrometer SITELLE at the Canada--France--Hawaii Telescope (CFHT), to search for the long-sought forward shock. Our observations target the coronal line [Fe XIV]$λ$5303, a tracer of shock-heated gas, over two $11^{\prime} \times 11^{\prime}$ fields that probe projected radii of $\sim$2.4--10~pc west of the pulsar, encompassing the range in which a supernova shell is expected. After data processing and a search over the full field of view, we found no statistically significant [Fe XIV] emission in the surveyed regions. We derive a conservative average surface-brightness upper limit of $\lesssim 3.79 \times 10^{-17}~\mathrm{erg}~\mathrm{cm}^{-2}~\mathrm{s}^{-1}~\mathrm{arcsec}^{-2}$ over three representative annuli. This represents the deepest large-area optical constraint on coronal iron emission beyond the visible nebula from the Crab's putative supernova remnant (SNR) shell. Our results are consistent with scenarios in which the forward shock is expanding into a very low-density medium, the shocked gas is weak or underionized, or the shell lies outside the observed region. This work demonstrates the power of wide-field optical integral field spectroscopy for constraining faint, large-scale structures in SNRs.

</details>


### [3] [Synchrotron self-Compton Reverse Shock in Energy-Injection and Radiative Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26290)
*Fraija Nissim,Betancourt-Kamenetskaia Boris,Galván Antonio,Dainotti Maria Giovanna*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文推导了伽马射线暴（GRB）反向激波区域产生的同步自康普顿（SSC）闭合关系，涵盖厚壳与薄壳两种情况，并考虑常数密度和分层周介质环境。研究发现，反向激波的SSC辐射可以重现GRB余辉中常见的早期陡衰和平台阶段，通过与第二期Fermi-LAT伽马射线暴目录（2FLGC）中PL和BPL拟合结果的对比，发现PL光变曲线拟合更偏向有能量注入的场景，而BPL拟合更偏向无能量注入场景；在能量注入场景下，数据更支持常数密度环境而非恒星风剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRB是能量最高的河外星系暂现源，其高能光变曲线和光谱中的多样化形态（通过同步辐射和SSC闭合关系解释）可能依赖于周介质密度剖面、能量注入以及反向激波在绝热或辐射条件下的动力学演化。此前缺乏对反向激波区域SSC闭合关系的系统推导，本研究旨在填补这一空白，并通过与2FLGC观测数据对比来判别不同物理场景的优劣。

Method: 论文推导了厚壳和薄壳情况下反向激波区域的SSC闭合关系，假设常数密度和分层周介质（如恒星风剖面）两种环境，并采用电子谱指数的双幂律分布假设，同时纳入有无来自前身星能量注入的两种情形。随后将预测的光谱和时间衰减指数与2FLGC中PL和BPL拟合结果进行系统对比。

Result: 反向激波的SSC发射根据物理参数不同可以重现GRB余辉中常见的初始陡衰阶段和平台阶段。PL光变曲线拟合倾向于支持含能量注入的场景，而BPL拟合更支持无能量注入的场景。在含能量注入的场景中，数据更偏向于常数密度环境而非恒星风剖面。

Conclusion: 该研究为利用反向激波SSC辐射解释GRB余辉早期多波段形态提供了系统的理论框架，并且通过与Fermi-LAT观测数据对比，能够有效判别不同物理参数组合（能量注入与否、环境介质类型）的适用性，为理解GRB中心引擎及周介质性质提供了新的约束。

Abstract: Gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) are among the most energetic extragalactic transients, providing a unique laboratory to investigate the physical origin of the diverse morphologies observed in high-energy light curves and spectra. These morphologies, interpreted through synchrotron and synchrotron self-Compton (SSC) closure relations (CRs), may depend on the circumburst density profile, energy injection, and the dynamical evolution of the reverse shock in the adiabatic or radiative regime. We derive the SSC closure relations produced in the reverse-shock region for both thick- and thin-shell cases, considering constant-density and stratified circumburst media. The analysis assumes two power-law (PL) distributions for the electron spectral index and includes scenarios with and without energy injection from the progenitor. We show that, depending on the physical parameters, SSC emission from the reverse shock can reproduce the initial steep-decay and plateau phases commonly observed in GRB afterglows. We then compare the predicted spectral and temporal indices with those derived from PL and broken power-law (BPL) fits reported in the Second Fermi-LAT Gamma-ray Burst Catalog (2FLGC). Our results indicate that PL (BPL) light-curve fits preferentially favor scenarios with (without) energy injection. In scenarios with energy injection, the data preferentially support a constant-density environment over a stellar-wind profile.

</details>


### [4] [FRB 121102: No supernova-like ejecta or magnetar power, hinting at a binary WD merger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26567)
*Eli Waxman,Eran O. Ofek,Doron Kushnir*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用FRB 121102的时间相关色散测量及其伴随的持续射电源（PRS）的早期射电观测，推导出其“引擎”的严格限制条件：相对论性PRS等离子体的能量>10^49.5 erg，年龄约60年，对应源功率>10^40 erg/s。若源为中子星，则指向旋转能量而非磁能，与约10 ms周期、约10^12.5 G磁场的中子星一致；吸积也是可行方案。冷等离子体的速度和动能与典型超新星抛射物不符（除非大量高密度团块承载质量），膨胀速度限于数百km/s，表明其在相对论性PRS等离子体发射开始前约>10^3年喷出。这些约束可由白矮星双星并合前身系统满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了揭示重复暴FRB 121102及其伴随持续射电源（PRS）背后底层能源机制的本质，通过时间相关色散测量和早期射电观测数据，对可能的引擎模型（中子星旋转能、磁能、吸积、超新星抛射物、白矮星并合等）进行严格的物理约束和判别。

Method: 利用FRB 121102的时间相关色散测量数据和其伴随PRS的早期射电观测，计算相对论性PRS等离子体的能量、年龄和源功率下限；通过等离子体的动力学约束（速度、动能）与超新星抛射物特性进行对比，排除或限制候选模型，并评估中子星旋转能、磁能、吸积及白矮星双星并合等前身模型的可行性。

Result: 得到PRS等离子体能量>10^49.5 erg、年龄约60年、源功率>10^40 erg/s的严格约束；若为中子星则排除磁能来源，支持约10 ms周期、约10^12.5 G的中子星旋转能源；吸积也可行。冷等离子体膨胀速度限制为数百km/s，与典型超新星抛射物不一致（除非存在约10^-2.5尺寸的高密度团块），且需在相对论性发射前约10^3年喷出。白矮星双星并合前身系统可同时满足所有约束。

Conclusion: FRB 121102的引擎最可能是一个由白矮星双星并合产生的高速旋转中子星（旋转能源），其缠绕的冷等离子体在并合过程中以缓慢速度喷出，而非超新星抛射物；这一场景能够同时满足能量、年龄、功率和动力学方面的所有观测约束。

Abstract: We use measurements of the time-dependent dispersion measure of the repeating FRB 121102, together with earlier radio observations of its associated persistent radio source (PRS), to derive stringent constraints on its underlying ``engine.'' The energy held by the relativistic PRS plasma is $>10^{49.5}$ erg, and its age is $\approx60$ yr, corresponding to an underlying source power exceeding $10^{40}$ erg s$^{-1}$. If the underlying source is a neutron star, this implies a rotational rather than a magnetic energy source, consistent with a $\sim10$ ms, $\sim10^{12.5}$ G neutron star. Alternatively, accretion may also be a viable energy source. The velocity and the kinetic energy of the cold plasma confining the relativistic PRS plasma are inconsistent with it being typical supernova ejecta (unless a significant fraction of the ejecta mass is carried by high-density clumps of $\approx10^{-2.5}$ fractional size)- its expansion speed is limited to a few hundred km/s, which also implies that it was ejected from the source more than $\approx 10^3$ yr preceding the onset of relativistic PRS plasma emission. These constraints may be satisfied by a white dwarf binary merger progenitor system, where a fraction of a solar mass was ejected at a slow speed during and after the merger, and a rapidly rotating neutron star was formed after $\sim10^3$ yr thermal evolution period of the merger remnant.

</details>


### [5] [Investigating the disappearance of a type-C QPO in black hole X-ray binary Swift J151857-572147 with AstroSat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26627)
*Biki Ram,Manoneeta Chakraborty,Mayukh Pahari*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究利用AstroSat观测对黑洞低质量X射线双星Swift J151857.0--572147进行了定时与宽带能谱分析，发现其在两个时间段（S1和S2）内经历了从存在~8 Hz type-C准周期振荡（QPO）及低频凸起（LFB）到两者同时消失的演变，伴随谱态转变和日冕结构重组，揭示QPO起源于日冕（热内流）区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究黑洞X射线双星中type-C QPO的物理起源及其与谱态转变的联系，通过观测QPO和低频宽带噪声同时消失这一罕见现象，深入理解吸积内流/日冕区域在谱态转变过程中的几何重组机制。

Method: 对AstroSat时间分辨观测数据实施定时分析与宽带能谱拟合；将观测分为S1和S2两段，进行功率密度谱（PDS）分析（QPO和LFB），能量依赖的RMS幅度和时间延迟分析，硬度-强度图（HID）分析，以及轨道分辨的宽带能谱参数（光子指数、电子温度、热参数）演化追踪。

Result: S1段检测到~8.02 Hz的type-C QPO和LFB；S2段两者同时消失。QPO的RMS幅度和时间延迟随能量单调增加，预示源为低倾角系统。QPO与LFB质心频率呈强正相关（Pearson系数0.98）。HID和能谱分析揭示谱态转变与QPO/LFB消失同步发生；非热参数（光子指数、日冕电子温度）在S1到S2间显著偏离，而热参数无系统变化。

Conclusion: type-C QPO和低频宽带噪声均源自热内流（日冕），两者的同时消失表明内吸积几何在该源发生谱态转变时快速重组，强有力支持QPO起源于日冕，且这些特征的消失反映了康普顿化区域的实质性转变。

Abstract: We present a timing and broadband spectral study of the AstroSat observation of the black hole low mass X-ray binary Swift J151857.0--572147, focusing on its evolution across two temporal segments, S1 and S2. During the first segment S1 lasting $\sim$ 50.6 ks, the source shows a clear type-C QPO at $\sim8.02$ Hz and a low-frequency bump (LFB) in the power density spectrum. By the later segment S2, the strength of the QPO feature decreases significantly and is no longer consistent with a coherent QPO, along with the simultaneous disappearance of the LFB. An energy-dependent analysis reveals that both the RMS amplitude and the time lag of the QPO in S1 increase monotonically with energy, suggesting a low inclination nature of the source. The QPO and LFB centroid frequencies show a strong positive correlation across orbital segments, with a Pearson coefficient of 0.98. Our variability analysis and hardness-intensity diagram reveal a spectral state transition of the source coincident with the turn-off of the QPO and the LFB, a conclusion supported by the evolution of broadband spectral parameters. Notably, the orbit-resolved spectral analysis reveals that the non-thermal parameters, photon index, and electron temperature of the corona exhibit significant deviation as the source traverses from S1 to S2, whereas the thermal parameters scatter without any systematic variation between the two segments. The simultaneous turn-off of the type-C QPO and the low-frequency broadband noise component, both originating from the hot inner flow, indicates a rapid reorganization of the inner accretion geometry. Our results strongly suggest a coronal origin for the QPO and demonstrate that the disappearance of both features reflects a substantial transformation of the Comptonizing region across the spectral transition.

</details>


### [6] [Spectro-Polarimetric Properties of CHIME FRB Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26654)
*Dengke Zhou,Yi Feng,Jiaying Xu,Chenyuan Xu,Jianhua Fang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用CHIME射电望远镜对28个重复快速射电暴（FRB）和70个非重复FRB进行了系统性的频谱偏振分析，量化了表征低频消偏振的参数σ_RM。研究将重复FRB的σ_RM样本从14个扩展到36个（增加2.6倍），发现重复暴的核密度估计峰值位于1.3 rad m⁻²，约70%的源σ_RM≳1 rad m⁻²，表明其大多处于复杂的磁电离环境中。相比之下，70个非重复暴中没有一个表现出显著的消偏振（σ_RM≳5 rad m⁻²），约半数与随频率恒定的线性偏振分数模型一致，但CHIME有限频率覆盖可能影响这些结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB的偏振特性是揭示其起源和周围环境的钥匙，特别是低频消偏振参数σ_RM可以探测源周围复杂的磁电离介质。此前重复FRB的σ_RM样本仅有14个，非重复FRB的系统性偏振研究亦缺乏，作者旨在通过大规模观测拓展样本并比较重复与非重复FRB的环境差异。

Method: 利用CHIME观测，对28个重复FRB测量σ_RM以扩展样本；对70个非重复FRB检验四种频谱偏振模型（包括恒定线性偏振分数模型和不同消偏振模型），并通过核密度估计（KDE）分析重复暴群体的σ_RM分布。

Result: 重复FRB的σ_RM样本从14个扩展到36个（2.6倍增幅），其KDE峰值位于1.3 rad m⁻²，约70%源σ_RM≳1 rad m⁻²；非重复FRB中无任何源表现出σ_RM≳5 rad m⁻²的显著消偏振，约一半与恒定线性偏振分数一致。

Conclusion: 重复FRB大多处于复杂的磁电离环境中，而非重复FRB未表现出显著的低频消偏振，这暗示两类FRB的局域环境可能存在本质差异。然而CHIME的有限频率带宽可能引入观测偏差，未来超宽带偏振观测将克服这一限制，提供更精确的σ_RM测量，加深对FRB环境及起源的理解。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) are enigmatic millisecond-duration radio transients whose polarization properties offer crucial insights into their origins and environments. In particular, low-frequency depolarization---quantified by the parameter \(σ_{\mathrm{RM}}\)---probes the complex magneto-ionic medium surrounding the progenitor, and has been observed across a population of repeating FRBs. We present a systematic spectro-polarimetric analysis of repeating and non-repeating FRBs using observations from the Canadian Hydrogen Intensity Mapping Experiment (CHIME). For 28 repeating FRBs, we measure \(σ_{\mathrm{RM}}\), expanding the known sample from 14 to 36 sources (an increase by a factor of 2.6). Abstract: We investigate potential sources of ultrahigh-energy cosmic rays (UHECRs) and their acceleration mechanisms, focusing on astrophysical phenomena associated with massive stellar deaths and supermassive black holes. While high-luminosity GRBs (HL GRBs) are constrained as UHECR sources by high-energy neutrino observations, low-luminosity GRBs (LL GRBs) and engine-driven supernovae remain promising candidates, with intermediate-mass nuclei as the dominant components. Compact binary mergers and $r$-process nucleosynthesis in neutron-rich environments may also contribute to ultraheavy UHECRs. The composition of UHECRs from TDEs depends on the properties of the disrupted stars. AGN, particularly radio galaxies, remain promising sources, with acceleration occurring in their large-scale jets and lobes. Shear acceleration mechanisms have been proposed as a viable alternative for accelerating UHECRs, involving the re-acceleration of low-energy cosmic rays and being compatible with the observed spectrum and composition. Future multi-messenger observations, especially from upcoming observatories, are expected to provide critical data to refine our understanding of UHECR origins, test existing models, and explore new acceleration mechanisms.

Abstract: Gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) have long been considered candidate sources of ultrahigh-energy cosmic rays (UHECRs) due to their large energy release and relativistic outflows. The detection of multi-TeV $γ$-rays from GRB~221009A and its rarity have renewed interest in this connection and motivate considering an additional narrow-jet long GRB population in the local Universe. We investigate whether such a local narrow-jet population can contribute to the observed diffuse UHECR energy spectrum. We also examine the associated cosmogenic neutrino and cascade $γ$-ray emissions to assess the multimessenger viability of this scenario. We fit the observed UHECR spectrum and mass composition data using three source-evolution models: a uniform comoving source emissivity, a standard-jet GRB population tracing the star formation rate (SFR), and a standard + narrow-jet GRB population tracing SFR. We propagate a mixed-composition UHECR injection and calculate the cosmogenic neutrino and cascade $γ$-ray fluxes. The standard + narrow jet model fits the observed UHECR spectrum and composition, with the highest-energy flux dominated by the narrow-jet population confined to $z\le z_{\max,*}\simeq0.36$. This low-redshift dominance lowers the cosmogenic neutrino flux compared to the standard-jet GRB population. For the narrow-jet GRB population, the fit implies a baryon loading factor $ξ_{\rm CR}^{\rm nj}\simeq10$. Such a locally enhanced long-GRB population can therefore explain the highest-energy UHECR flux without violating current multimessenger constraints. Future UHE searches can further probe this scenario through the associated cosmogenic fluxes.

</details>

Abstract: How does the Bose or Fermi statistics of microscopic particles survive when confinement binds them into emergent bosonic composites? We address this question in the strong-coupling limit of a $2+1$D $\mathbb{Z}_2$ lattice gauge theory, where charges are confined into tightly bound pairs that can be described by an effective dimer model. We find that the statistics of the underlying matter is encoded entirely in a single local hopping phase $\varphi$ -$0$ for bosons, $π$ for fermions- while interactions remain statistics-independent. Treating $\varphi$ as a continuous parameter that interpolates between the two, we map the ground-state phase diagram with the help of tensor-network methods. The angle $\varphi$ itself drives a transition between a dimer-superfluid and dimer charge density wave state, while the magnetic coupling binds neighboring dimers into resonating pairs, in competition with the inter-dimer repulsion. We identify a novel gapped phase in which dimer pairs crystallize into an ordered pattern of resonating plaquettes. Finally, we propose a quench protocol under which identical dimer configurations evolve in markedly different ways depending on the statistics of their constituents. This provides a dynamical probe of the internal structure of dimers, and detects ordered phases through real time signatures, within reach of simulators that natively realize bosonic degrees of freedom.

Abstract: We study the ground-state properties and dynamical response of the Heisenberg model on the sawtooth chain in and away from the exactly solvable valence-bond-solid (VBS) point. Abstract: Thermal quenching inhibits equilibration toward the thermodynamic ground state during phase transitions, revealing metastable phases such as structural glasses and quenched alloys. A $c$-axis field suppresses this order, driving the system into a gapless quantum spin liquid with a spinon Fermi surface above $μ_0 H_\mathrm{c} \approx 3\,\mathrm{T}$, evidenced by a finite residual linear term in thermal conductivity, a Pauli-like susceptibility, and a temperature-independent NMR Knight shift. Approaching $H_\mathrm{c}$ from above, the scattering rate of itinerant excitations is strongly enhanced while their density of states shows no critical enhancement, atypical of conventional magnetic quantum criticality. These results point to a continuous confinement--deconfinement transition governed by fractionalized excitations beyond the Ginzburg--Landau paradigm.

Abstract: Exactly solvable quantum many-body models are rare, and even when their spectra are algebraically organized, dynamical responses generally remain difficult to obtain because they probe an extensive number of excited states. Here we show that quantum geometric nesting (QGN) models admit an unusually strong form of solvability rooted in \emph{Fock-space fragmentation}: excitations on top of the exact frustration-free ground states decouple into Krylov subspaces with a fixed number of particle and hole operators, and hence remain dynamically invariant. Exploiting this structure, we prove that the stiffness of the spontaneously broken continuous symmetry in QGN models is exactly equal to its variational value in the Gaussian manifold, confirming a conjecture from quantum many-body bootstrap~\cite{GaoHanKhalaf2026}. The proof shows that an infinitesimal phase twist couples the ground state only to the one-particle, one-hole fragment, which coincides with the tangent space of the ground state within the variational manifold, thereby making the variational curvature exact. More generally, perturbations whose action remains within a fixed Fock-space fragment have response functions determined exactly by the corresponding few-body sector, enabling exact access to quantities including static susceptibility, optical conductivity, dynamical structure factors, and single-particle Green's functions.

</details>

Abstract: Continuous quantum phase transitions in interacting many-body systems exhibit universal phenomena that are largely independent of microscopic details. An organizing principle distilled from canonical examples such as the magnetic transition in Ising models is that the low-energy physics of transitions between gapped phases is governed by scale-invariant quantum field theories with gapless degrees of freedom at isolated points in momentum space. In this paper, we show that this principle can fail in a radical way: an entire gapless Fermi surface can emerge at a quantum critical point separating two fully gapped insulators. These exotic phase transitions are dubbed \textit{metallogenic quantum critical points} (MGQCP). We construct a controlled family of effective field theories for MGQCPs between distinct fractional Chern insulators and propose a periodically modulated quantum Hall bilayer as a minimal microscopic setting. The critical theory exhibits an unusual mixture of physical signatures: while transport and compressibility follow scaling laws compatible with a conformal field theory, the electron spectral function develops a power-law non-analyticity at the Fermi momentum, characteristic of non-Fermi-liquid metals. We discuss how the concept of MGQCP may shed new light on critical phenomena in moire materials with tunable Chern bands. More generally, our results point to a broader class of quantum critical phenomena in which extended manifolds of gapless excitations emerge in momentum space despite being absent in both neighboring phases.

</details>

### [26] [WALL-SS: Scaling Long-horizon World Models via Next-Scale Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26239)
*Maeve Zhang,Rain Sun,Xiang Wang,Cyril Zhang,Shalfun Li,Meng Cao,Howard Lu,Ethan Chen,Harry Jhou,KZ Zheng,Lights Shi,Regis Cheng,Lorenzin,Robert Wang,Victor Yao,Gody Li,Elise Mon,Yohann Tang,Ryan Yu,PS Zhang,Vincent Chen,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WALL-SS，一种基于尺度级自回归缩放的生成式世界模型，通过将具身轨迹建模为观察与动作交错的时间因果序列，支持动作可控、长时域的机器人视觉仿真，并引入动作条件化下一尺度预测、尺度压缩长时记忆与在线策略对齐三个组件来提升长时程生成的稳定性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型为机器人提供环境演化的预测模型，有望用于仿真、规划、策略评估和机器人学习。然而，现有方法多停留在片段级别的未来预测，缺乏将动作与后果统一关联的生成式框架，难以支持灵活的时间跨度、连续交互以及基于奖励的优化。因此需要一种统一的生成式表述，能明确建模动作-状态依赖关系并支持长时程的交互式仿真。

Method: WALL-SS采用尺度级自回归缩放（Scale-wise autoregressive Scaling）的生成方式。具体而言：1）将具身轨迹表示为观察与动作交错的时间因果序列，使动作依赖的状态转换显式化，自然支持变长生成和流式扩展；2）以从粗到细的方式生成每个未来观察，并在同一层级内设计三个互补组件：a) 动作条件化下一尺度预测，注入尺度对齐的动作表示以增强动作-未来耦合；b) 尺度压缩长时记忆，保留近期交互的精细分辨率同时压缩远端观察与动作，配合尺度级梦境强韧化以增强对自生成上下文的鲁棒性；c) 在线策略对齐，通过动作跟随与长期一致性奖励优化自回归视觉动力学，同时保持预训练视觉分布。

Result: 实验表明WALL-SS在动作跟随和轨迹精度方面均有提升；在有限记忆条件下支持连贯的分钟级流式滚动生成；在线策略对齐能持续减少动作漂移和长时程不一致性问题。

Conclusion: WALL-SS通过尺度级自回归缩放的统一生成式框架，实现了动作可控且长时程的机器人世界模型仿真，验证了尺度层级架构与在线策略对齐在多方面的有效性，为生成式世界模型在机器人领域的应用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Generative world models provide robots with predictive models of how the world evolves under interaction, with growing potential for simulation, planning, policy evaluation, and robot learning. Beyond clip-level future prediction, a unified generative formulation should relate actions to consequences, support flexible horizons and continuous interaction, and enable reward-driven optimization. We introduce WALL-SS, a world model that generates visual futures through Scale-wise autoregressive Scaling, enabling action-controllable and long-horizon robotic simulation. WALL-SS represents embodied trajectories as causal sequences of temporally interleaved observations and actions, making action-dependent state transitions explicit while naturally supporting variable-length generation, streaming extension through reusable causal states, and direct optimization through sequence probabilities. To make this formulation effective over long horizons, we generate each future observation in a coarse-to-fine manner and develop three complementary components within the same hierarchy. Action-conditioned next-scale prediction injects scale-aligned action representations to improve action-future coupling and model both successful and failed behaviors. Scale-compressed long-horizon memory retains recent interactions at fine resolution while compressing distant observations and actions, with scale-wise dream forcing enhancing robustness to self-generated context. Finally, on-policy alignment optimizes autoregressive visual dynamics with action-following and long-term consistency rewards while preserving the pretrained visual distribution. Experiments show that WALL-SS improves action following and trajectory accuracy, supports coherent minute-long streaming rollout under bounded memory, and consistently benefits from on-policy alignment in reducing action drift and long-horizon inconsistency.

</details>

*Rana Danesh,Pari Qarehdaghi,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约束感知的物理信息神经网络（PINN），用于腱驱动连续体机器人（CCR）与被操纵柔性物体形成闭链系统时的静态形状估计。该方法利用投影静态平衡残差和构型级几何残差，在有限且含噪的训练数据下显著优于纯数据驱动的人工神经网络，并兼具物理一致性、高精度和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同操纵连续体机器人（CCR）的静态形状估计具有挑战性，因为连续体臂与被操纵的柔性物体构成闭环，必须同时满足静态平衡和几何闭环约束。传统迭代非线性求解器计算昂贵，而纯数据驱动方法在有限或含噪数据下精度有限且缺乏物理一致性。

Method: 该方法基于几何变量应变公式（geometric variable strain formulation）对腱驱动CCR建模，构建了一个约束感知的物理信息神经网络（PINN），将投影静态平衡残差和构型级几何残差嵌入损失函数中，以强制执行机构力学和闭环几何约束。模型先在仿真数据上训练，再通过实验数据进行微调。

Result: 在140个样本和50%标签噪声条件下，PINN相比纯数据驱动ANN在相对构型误差、平衡残差和闭环残差上分别降低67.88%、67.35%和88.06%。使用完整数据集时，相对构型误差为0.1597%，推理时间为0.1773ms（而迭代非线性求解器需17.97s）。实验微调将标记RMSE从2.657mm降至0.497mm，R2从-0.788提升至0.937。

Conclusion: 所提出的约束感知PINN能够实现闭环CCR系统准确、物理一致且计算高效的静态形状估计，在数据稀缺和含噪场景下相比纯数据驱动方法具有显著优势，同时推理速度远超传统迭代求解器。

Abstract: Static shape estimation of co-manipulative continuum robots (CCRs) is challenging because the continuum arms and manipulated flexible object form a closed chain that must satisfy both static equilibrium and geometric loop-closure constraints. This paper presents a constraint-aware physics-informed neural network (PINN) for static shape estimation of a tendon-driven CCR modeled using the geometric variable strain formulation. The proposed method incorporates a projected static equilibrium residual and a configuration-level geometric residual to enforce the governing mechanics and closed-chain geometry. In simulation, the PINN is compared with a purely data-driven artificial neural network (ANN) under limited and noisy training data. With 140 samples and 50% label noise, the PINN reduces the relative configuration error, equilibrium residual, and closed-chain residual by 67.88%, 67.35%, and 88.06%, respectively. Using the full dataset, the PINN achieves 0.1597% relative configuration error with an inference time of 0.1773 ms, compared with 17.97 s for an iterative nonlinear solver. Experimental fine-tuning reduces the marker RMSE from 2.657 mm to 0.497 mm and increases R2 from -0.788 to 0.937. These results demonstrate accurate, physically consistent, and computationally efficient static shape estimation of closed-chain CCRs.

</details>


### [28] [Dispersive Forward Tree Search for Optimal Control: Coverage, Complexity, and Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26314)
*Shashank A. Deshpande,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前向传播的动力学规划器DFT*（Dispersive Forward Tree search），针对微分平坦非线性系统，具备确定性的有限样本近最优性保证。通过构造局部分散性控制命令集构建前向树，结合成本条件的主宰剪枝策略，将树规模从指数级降至多项式级，并在嵌入式处理器上展现出优于现有最先进动力学规划器的解质量和实时规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于转向（steering）的规划器需要求解状态到状态的边值问题，对于非线性平台而言往往难以实现。虽然前向传播（forward propagation）可以规避转向要求，但其关联规划器的有限样本行为从未被刻画，且实际实现性能欠佳。因此，需要一种既具备理论保证、又能在实践中高效实现的传播式动力学规划方法。

Method: 1) 在微分平坦非线性系统类中证明：由局部分散性（locally dispersive）控制命令构成的前向树，在认证的树规模下必包含近最优轨迹；2) 提出面向控制仿射系统构造分散性命令集的一般机制；3) 证明覆盖认证轨迹类必然需要指数级规模的前向树，并提出成本条件化的主宰剪枝（cost-conditioned dominance pruning）程序，在保持近最优性的同时将树规模降至关于时间范围的多项式量级；4) 实现DFT*算法，以广度优先方式扩展前向树，天然适配并行硬件；5) 为单轮车、拖挂车和四旋翼设计了高效的分散性采样器，并在嵌入式处理器及滚动时域实时规划场景中进行了评估。

Result: DFT*在嵌入式级处理器上，以相当的求解时间内，持续提供与最先进动力学规划器相当甚至显著更优的解决方案质量，且随并行计算扩展可获得进一步加速。在动态环境中的滚动时域实现展示了嵌入式计算预算下的实时规划能力。

Conclusion: 本文为传播式动力学规划提供了首个确定性有限样本近最优性保证的理论框架，并验证了其在多种非线性平台上的实际有效性和硬件可扩展性，弥合了理论保证与工程实践之间的差距。

Abstract: Steering-based planners require solutions to state-to-state boundary value problems, which can be inaccessible for nonlinear platforms. Forward propagation evades the steering requirement, but the finite-sample behavior of the associated planners remains uncharacterized and their implementations underperform in practice. This paper develops a propagation-based kinodynamic planner with deterministic finite-sample near-optimality guarantees. We work within the large class of differentially flat nonlinear systems and show that a forward tree of locally dispersive control commands contains a near-optimal trajectory at a certified tree size. We provide a general mechanism to construct dispersive command sets for control-affine systems, which are necessary to implement the search algorithm prescribed by the theory. We show that covering the certified trajectory class irrespective of cost provably demands a tree exponentially sized in the problem horizon, and present a cost-conditioned dominance pruning procedure that retains near-optimality at a tree size polynomial in the horizon. We implement the resulting search algorithm, Dispersive Forward Tree search (DFT*), as breadth-first expansion of the forward tree, which maps naturally onto parallel hardware. We design efficient dispersive samplers for the unicycle, the trailer car, and the quadrotor and evaluate challenging planning tasks for these platforms. DFT* delivers consistently competitive and often substantially better solution quality than state-of-the-art kinodynamic planners at comparable solution times on embedded-tier processors, accelerating further as parallel compute is scaled. We also implement DFT* in a receding-horizon loop to demonstrate real-time planning in dynamic environments at embedded-tier compute budgets.

</details>


### [29] [CLIPPER: Replayable Shortlisted Optimization for Repeated Spatial Coverage Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26819)
*Julian Teusch,Jörg Philipp Müller,Monika Sester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLIPPER是一种面向城市微出行规划的约束精确低延迟迭代规划框架，通过与不伦瑞克市合作开发，在保持与全集贪心算法几乎相同的覆盖率（平均差距不超过0.245个百分点）的同时，将规划时间降低13.6至28.9倍。CLIPPER-F和CLIPPER-A两种变体分别在不同城市尺度上实现了快速、可回放的城市规划状态比较，同时强制执行所有编码的模型约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市微出行规划面临频繁的政策变更需求，每次政策修改都需要重新生成可行的规划方案。现有全集贪心算法在城市规模下每次替代方案需要数十秒的计算时间，无法满足快速迭代和实时比较规划状态的需求。

Method: CLIPPER采用候选池机制（形成有界候选池）但精确重算当前增益并在选择前检查所有活跃约束。覆盖率单独决定初始排序顺序，离线全集扫描测量候选池遗漏的增益，在线通过保守边界触发扩展或审计。CLIPPER-F为每个提案组分配相同数量的候选槽位，而CLIPPER-A在组间共享一个候选预算。

Result: 在不伦瑞克、慕尼黑和柏林三个城市上，CLIPPER-F的完整链平均覆盖率与全集贪心算法差距不超过0.245个百分点，平均规划时间降低13.6-28.9倍。CLIPPER-A在覆盖率优先策略下仅使用全集贪心算法规划时间的9-15%，不伦瑞克、慕尼黑和柏林的平均覆盖率差距分别为1.82、0.12和0.27个百分点。

Conclusion: CLIPPER能够在不牺牲约束满足性的前提下，实现快速、可回放的城市级规划状态比较，为城市微出行规划提供了高效的迭代方案支持工具。

Abstract: Operational requirements developed with the City of Braunschweig frame municipal micromobility planning under geofenced exclusions, mandatory retained sites, spacing rules, and area-level caps. Each policy edit requires a new feasible plan; full-set greedy takes tens of seconds per alternative at city scale. We present CLIPPER (Constraint-exact Low-latency Iterative Planning with Pooled Evaluation and Replay). It forms bounded candidate pools but recomputes exact current gains and checks every active constraint before selection. Coverage from each candidate alone sets the initial order. Offline full-set scans measure gains omitted by the pool; online, a conservative bound triggers expansion or audit. CLIPPER-F gives each proposal group the same number of candidate slots. Across Braunschweig, Munich, and Berlin, its mean coverage over complete chains stays within 0.245 percentage points of full-set greedy under the same policy, with 13.6--28.9 times lower mean rollout time. CLIPPER-A instead distributes one shared candidate budget across the groups. Under its coverage-prioritized policy, it uses 9--15% of full-set greedy's rollout time under the same policy, with mean gaps of 1.82 percentage points in Braunschweig, 0.12 in Munich, and 0.27 in Berlin. Together, CLIPPER enables rapid, replayable comparison of recorded city-scale planning states while enforcing every encoded model constraint.

</details>


### [30] [Cross-Platform Benchmark of Neural 3D Reconstruction for Autonomous Laboratory Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26383)
*Yongho Kim,Mengjiao Han,Victor Mateevitsi,Silvio Rizzi,Michael E. Papka,Nicola Ferrier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统性地评估了NeRF和3D Gaussian Splatting等神经3D重建方法在从单板计算机到服务器级节点等GPU计算平台上的训练和渲染性能，并将Meta的SAM3D单图重建纳入同一评价指标，量化其延迟与保真度差距。结果显示Gaussian Splatting在更高GPU成本下取得优于NeRF的渲染质量，板载计算无法支撑交互速率下的完整逐场景优化，而SAM3D虽能在数秒内生成合理几何但细节失配可能影响下游操作，因此提出了分层管线方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人执行实验室任务依赖于能在物理控制回路延迟预算内将原始相机流转化为可操作物体表示的3D重建管线。虽然神经3D重建方法已展现高质量视图合成能力，但它们在实验室机器人实际运行的计算平台上的实时可行性缺乏系统性的特征刻画，亟需跨平台基准测试来填补这一空白。

Method: 作者构建了一个系统性的计算平台基准测试，在从单板计算机到服务器级节点的多种GPU计算设备上，对NeRF和3D Gaussian Splatting两种神经3D重建方法的训练与渲染进行了对比评估，并在相同的评价维度上引入了Meta的SAM3D单图像重建方法，以量化其与逐场景优化方法在延迟和保真度上的差距。

Result: 实验结果表明：Gaussian Splatting在GPU成本更高的前提下获得了比NeRF更高的渲染质量；板载计算资源不足以支撑以交互速率进行完整的逐场景优化；对SAM3D的初步评估显示其能在数秒内给出合理的物体几何，但存在细节失配问题，可能影响下游的操控任务。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持采用分层管线的方案：用轻量级前馈重建来维持机器人实时感知与跟踪回路，同时将更重的神经重建方法选择性地调度到合适的计算资源上执行。

Abstract: Autonomous robots performing laboratory tasks depend on 3D reconstruction pipelines that can turn raw camera streams into actionable object representations within the latency budget of a physical control loop. Neural 3D reconstruction methods have demonstrated high-quality view synthesis, but their real-time viability across the compute platforms on which laboratory robots actually run remains poorly characterized. In this work, we present a systematic compute-platform benchmark of neural 3D reconstruction methods, evaluating NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting training and rendering on GPU-enabled computing devices ranging from single-board computers to server-class nodes, and place Meta's SAM3D single-image reconstruction on the same axes to quantify its latency and fidelity gap relative to per-scene optimization. Our results show that Gaussian Splatting yields higher rendering quality than NeRF at greater GPU cost, and that onboard compute is insufficient for full per-scene optimization at interactive rates. Our preliminary assessment on SAM3D indicates that it delivers plausible object geometry within seconds, but with detail mismatches that can compromise downstream manipulation. Together, these findings motivate tiered pipelines in which lightweight feed-forward reconstruction sustains the real-time perception-and-tracking loop for laboratory robots, while heavier neural reconstruction is scheduled selectively on suitable compute.

</details>


### [31] [RTNav: Towards Real-Time Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26496)
*Easop Lee,Lingyu Zhang,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RTNav，一种针对现实实时执行条件设计的零样本目标导航架构，显式考虑推理延迟、异步环境步进和有限计算资源。在HM3D实时变体上，RTNav比现有方法成功率提升最高11%，SCT提升最高5.1个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的目标导航方法大多在同步模拟器中开发，环境等待智能体行动，推理时间被认为免费。然而在实际应用中，视觉语言基础模型引入的推理延迟不可忽视，在实时执行（墙钟时间计入任务预算）条件下，传统顺序执行"感知-推理-动作"的架构效率低下，导致零样本导航方法性能严重下降。作者由此提出将延迟和异步作为显式设计考虑的必要性。

Method: RTNav是一个简单但有效的架构，将推理延迟、异步环境步进和有界计算作为显式设计考量。具体包括：针对实时执行的三方面设计——处理推理延迟（避免顺序阻塞）、支持异步环境步进（环境不需等待智能体推理完成）、在有限计算预算内平衡感知与行动频率。

Result: 在HM3D-v1、HM3D-v2和HM3D-OVON的实时变体上评估，RTNav相比先前工作将成功率（Success Rate）提升最高11%，成功加权完成时间（Success weighted by Completion Time, SCT）提升最高5.1个点。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一个此前被忽视的重要问题：同步模拟器中的性能并不能代表真实世界实时运行时的表现。RTNav通过将时序与计算约束纳入架构设计，在实时条件下显著优于现有方法，为在现实世界持续运行的导航代理设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Navigation in unknown environments to find unforeseen objects has become increasingly feasible with capable vision and language foundation models. However, these models also introduce non-negligible inference latency, which becomes an important concern when agents must operate continuously in the real world. Most state-of-the-art methods are still developed in synchronous simulators, where the environment waits for the agent to act and inference time is effectively free. As a result, agents are often designed around the sequential execution of perception, reasoning, and action, with little regard for time constraints. Under real-time execution, where wall-clock time counts towards the task budget, the inefficiencies of these architectures become clear. We show that recent zero-shot object navigation methods suffer consistent performance degradation under such realistic timing conditions. Motivated by this observation, we propose RTNav, a simple but effective architecture that treats inference latency, asynchronous environment stepping, and bounded compute as explicit design considerations. Evaluated on real-time variants of HM3D-v1, HM3D-v2, and HM3D-OVON, RTNav improves the success rate by up to 11% and the Success weighted by Completion Time by up to 5.1 points over prior work.

</details>

*Xulin Chen,Borui He,Ruipeng Liu,Naveed Tahir,Zhenyu Gan,Garrett E. Katz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文为Poppy人形机器人提出了一种基于线性二次调节器（LQR）框架的闭环行走控制器，通过从开环轨迹播放数据中学习二次代价函数，实现了可靠且无需人工辅助的双足行走，并在实验中证明了比开环路径播放显著更优的行走性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Poppy人形机器人是一个开源、低成本、适合AI研究和教育的机器人平台，但现有文献中尚无基于标准Poppy硬件实现可靠、无辅助双足行走的方法，这成为该平台在研究中应用的一大障碍。

Method: 首先通过开环播放名义行走轨迹收集数据，然后从这些数据中学习一个用于LQR控制的二次代价函数，最终构建一个闭环行走控制器用于轨迹跟踪。

Result: 闭环控制器经过经验验证，与开环轨迹播放相比，在行走性能上取得了统计学上显著的改善。

Conclusion: 该方法成功为Poppy人形机器人提供了功能性的闭环行走控制器，填补了该平台在可靠双足运动控制方面的空白，为研究和教育应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Poppy Humanoid is an open-source, low-cost robot suitable for research and education in artificial intelligence. However, we are unaware of any published methodology that achieves reliable, unassisted bipedal locomotion on the standard Poppy hardware. This paper contributes a functional closed-loop walking controller for Poppy, based on the linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) framework for trajectory tracking. Starting with data collected from open-loop playback of a nominal walking trajectory, our proposed method learns a quadratic cost function for an LQR controller that substantially improves the reliability of the motion. The closed-loop controller is validated empirically, demonstrating statistically significant improvements in walking performance compared to open-loop trajectory playback.

</details>


### [33] [Memory Anchors for Continual Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26545)
*Maximilian Du,Zhanyi Sun,Chen Xu,Paarth Shah,Masha Itkina,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"记忆锚点"（Memory Anchors）概念，指出回放缓冲区中少量新旧任务表征重叠区域的样本对防止灾难性遗忘起关键作用，仅排除10%的记忆锚点就导致遗忘增加4.5倍，而增强记忆锚点可使高冲突任务遗忘降低63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然在野外部署的机器人策略需要持续学习新任务且不遗忘已有行为，常用的回放缓冲区方法虽从所有先前经验中随机采样，但作者发现其中仅少量经验对锚定过往性能贡献极大，这些被称为"记忆锚点"的样本未被识别和有效利用。

Method: 识别出新旧任务观测表征相互坍塌但任务需求冲突的区域中的经验作为记忆锚点；通过排除或富集这些锚点样本，在LIBERO基准套件和真实机器人上验证其对灾难性遗忘的影响。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，仅排除10%的记忆锚点再采样缓冲区，灾难性遗忘增加超过4.5倍；反之，用记忆锚点富集回放缓冲区可将高冲突任务的遗忘降低63%，并实现真实机器人上两个任务序列的成功持续学习。

Conclusion: 记忆锚点作为防止旧任务知识被破坏性覆盖的关键样本，对回放缓冲区采样策略至关重要，识别并富集记忆锚点能显著提升机器人策略的持续学习能力。

Abstract: Robot policies deployed in the wild should have the capability to continually learn new tasks without forgetting existing behaviors. A common approach to combat such catastrophic forgetting is to train on new task data with a replay buffer of previously learned task data. Although this buffer is commonly sampled randomly from all prior experiences, we show that a small set of these experiences contributes greatly in anchoring past performance. We call these experiences Memory Anchors. We identify Memory Anchors in regions where representations of new-task observations collapse onto those of old-task observations even though the tasks require conflicting actions, like when a familiar object must be manipulated in a new way. Rehearsing old data in this region plays a key role in preventing destructive overwriting of past task knowledge, serving as this critical Memory Anchor role. Excluding only 10% Memory Anchors before sampling the buffer leads to more than a 4.5x increase in catastrophic forgetting on the LIBERO benchmark suites. Conversely, enriching the replay buffer with Memory Anchors can decrease high-conflict task forgetting by 63% and enables successful continual learning of two task sequences on a real robot. Videos and additional visualizations can be found at https://robot-adaptation.github.io/MemoryAnchors

</details>


### [34] [TrapVLA: Trapping Vision-Language-Action Models in Configured Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26578)
*Jun-Hui Liu,Kun-Yu Lin,Yi-Lin Wei,Xu-Han Chen,Yinghao Li,Zhuohao Li,Yuan-Ming Li,Qing Zhang,Xiaoyi Fan,Dongmei Jiang,Yan Li,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的后门攻击任务——Configured Failure Trapping，针对视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型，通过隐蔽的文本触发器诱导机器人执行特定配置的失败模式（如按指定位置偏移抓取），而非仅造成任意失败。作者构建了数据引擎、评测套件及两个基准（Trap-LIBERO和Trap-RoboTwin），并提出TrapVLA方法通过学习触发诱发的动作残差来实现配置化失败攻击，实验证明其在仿真和真实机器人场景中有效注入配置化失败模式同时保持干净数据上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对VLA模型的后门攻击仅将任意任务失败视为攻击成功，无法控制机器人具体如何失败。作者旨在提升攻击的任务难度和隐蔽性，提出更严苛的配置化失败攻击，攻击者需精确控制失败行为（如指定抓取偏移量），这既更具挑战性也更难被检测。

Method: 提出(1)数据引擎用于合成高质量的配置化目标失败轨迹；(2)自动化评测套件用于衡量配置化失败保真度；(3)两个新基准Trap-LIBERO和Trap-RoboTwin覆盖四种代表性失败模式；(4)TrapVLA方法，通过显式学习触发器引入的动作残差来引导策略朝配置化失败方向偏移，应对稀疏动作偏离这一关键挑战。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实机器人设置中的大量实验表明，TrapVLA能够有效将配置化失败模式注入VLA模型，同时在大幅保持干净数据上的任务性能。

Conclusion: 本文首次定义了VLA模型下的配置化失败攻击任务并提供了完整工具链（数据合成、评测、基准与方法），验证了该攻击的可行性与隐蔽性，为VLA安全研究提供了新的评估维度。

Abstract: This work introduces Configured Failure Trapping, a novel backdoor attack task against Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, which aims to activate attacks through stealthy textual triggers and induce configured failure modes. Unlike prior backdoor attacks that treat any task failure as a successful attack, Configured Failure Trapping requires the attacker to control how the robot fails (e.g., causing the robot to grasp with a specified positional offset), making it substantially more challenging and hard to detect. To support the new task, we propose an effective data engine for synthesizing high-quality target trajectories and an automated suite for measuring configured-failure fidelity. Then, based on this foundation, we construct two new benchmarks, namely Trap-LIBERO and Trap-RoboTwin, that instantiate Configured Failure Trapping across four representative failure modes. To address this task, we identify sparse action deviation as a critical challenge and accordingly propose a novel method named TrapVLA, which explicitly learns trigger-induced action residuals to steer the policy toward the configured failure behavior. Extensive experiments across simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic settings show that TrapVLA effectively injects configured failure modes into VLA models while largely preserving performance on clean data. Project page: https://john-liua.github.io/TrapVLA/

</details>


### [35] [SOLO: Stable Omni-terrain Long-Horizon Perceptive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26583)
*Pihai Sun,Gang Han,Jingkai Sun,Jiahao Ma,Zeran Su,Zelin Tao,Peiran Liu,Shuai Shi,Wei Cui,Zifan Wang,Jialin Yu,Wen Zhao,Kangning Yin,Jiaxu Wang,Jiahang Cao,Lingfeng Zhang,Hao Cheng,Jian Tang,Yijie Guo,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOLO是一个面向长时程人形机器人行走的统一框架，通过查询重建器（QR）保留尖锐地形边界，以及轨迹感知MSE蒸馏（TA-MSE）实现时序信用分配，解决了密集地形重建平滑化关键细节和逐点模仿缺乏时间信用分配两大问题。在仿真中高度图L1误差降低3.3-4.0倍，压力测试地形成功率达97.5%，并零样本部署在1.5公里户外与室内混合地形上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能在长距离复杂地形中行走而不失去平衡，而感知型人形机器人策略会因感知与控制误差的累积而变得脆弱。这种长时程脆弱性源于两个相互叠加的原因：密集地形重建会平滑掉对动作至关重要的细节，以及逐点模仿缺乏时序上的信用分配。

Method: SOLO包含两个核心组件：1) 查询重建器（QR）利用傅里叶编码的单元查询从深度-本体感觉token中检索空间特定证据，从而保留尖锐地形边界；2) 轨迹感知MSE（TA-MSE）蒸馏将下一状态师生分歧加入PPO奖励，使广义优势估计（GAE）能将未来的分歧惩罚传播到先前的动作。

Result: 在仿真中，QR使高度图L1误差降低3.3-4.0倍；TA-MSE在课程进度上超越PPO和MSE+PPO。在压力测试地形上，SOLO达到97.5%的平均穿越成功率和96%的踏脚石成功率，而密集重建变体分别为75.0-75.6%和0-3%。在仅配备胸部深度相机和本体感觉的情况下零样本部署，成功完成1.5公里户外路线和室内混合地形路线。

Conclusion: SOLO证明了通过精细地形重建与时序感知的模仿学习相结合，能够显著提升人形机器人在长距离复杂地形中的鲁棒性，实现从仿真到真实世界的零样本迁移。

Abstract: Humans traverse complex terrain over long distances without losing balance, whereas perceptive humanoid policies become fragile as perception and control errors accumulate. We present SOLO, a unified framework addressing two compounding causes of this long-horizon fragility: dense terrain reconstruction smooths action-critical details, and pointwise imitation lacks temporal credit assignment. Its Query Reconstructor (QR) uses Fourier-encoded cell queries to retrieve spatially specific evidence from depth-proprioception tokens, preserving sharp terrain boundaries. Trajectory-Aware MSE (TA-MSE) Distillation adds next-state teacher-student disagreement to the PPO reward, enabling Generalized Advantage Estimation to propagate future disagreement penalties to preceding actions. In simulation, QR reduces height-map L1 error by factors of 3.3-4.0, while TA-MSE surpasses PPO and MSE+PPO in curriculum progression. On stress-test terrains, SOLO achieves 97.5% mean traversal success and 96% stepping-stone success, versus 75.0-75.6% and 0-3% for dense-reconstructor variants. Deployed zero-shot with only a chest-mounted depth camera and proprioception, SOLO completes a continuous 1.5-km outdoor route and an indoor mixed-terrain course. Project page: https://sunpihai-up.github.io/solo/

</details>


### [36] [Relaxation-Aware Multimodal Sensing of Soft Gripper Driven by Structure-Perception-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26622)
*Yanzhe Wang,Hao Wu,Ziyi Zheng,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集成结构-感知-学习的软体机械手框架，通过变刚度设计与视觉/红外热成像感知，结合温度耦合的粘弹性力表征与物理信息学习模型，实现长时间稳定抓取，在280秒恒力抓取任务中平均绝对误差仅0.066N。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机械手在持续抓取过程中，由于软聚合物的固有粘弹性导致应力松弛和抓取力持续衰减，目前缺乏有效解决方案来维持稳定、持续的抓握力。

Method: 提出结构-感知-学习一体化框架：(1)开发变刚度软体夹爪；(2)利用机载视觉和红外热成像实时追踪变形与温度场；(3)提出温度耦合的粘弹性力表征；(4)采用物理信息学习模型重构力趋势并提供显式力补偿。

Result: 在280秒恒力抓取-保持任务中，提出的方法将期望力维持的平均绝对误差降至0.066N，相比固定孔径和仅瞬时基线分别提升80%和95%。

Conclusion: 结果支持机制-AI协同设计观点：机制塑造可行的交互，学习补偿粘弹性动力学中的残余不确定性，共同实现稳定、持续的抓取。

Abstract: Achieving stable, sustained grasping with soft robotic hands remains a fundamental challenge. Compliance enables safe and adaptive contact, yet the intrinsic viscoelasticity of soft polymers leads to stress relaxation and a continuous decay of grasping force during holding. Inspired by human grasping, which combines phase-dependent stiffness regulation with continuous sensing and feedback, this paper presents an integrated structure--perception--learning framework. We develop a variable-stiffness soft gripper that uses onboard vision and infrared thermography to track deformation and the temperature field in real time, preserving continuous tracking of the interaction state. To mitigate relaxation-induced force decay, we propose a temperature-coupled viscoelastic force representation, together with a physics-informed learning model, to reconstruct the force trend and provide explicit compensation during holding. Experiments show that, in a 280s force-controlled grasp-and-hold task, the proposed method maintains the desired force with a mean absolute error of 0.066N, outperforming fixed-aperture and instantaneous-only baselines by 80% and 95%, respectively. Overall, the results support a mechanism--AI co-design view: mechanisms shape feasible interactions, while learning compensates remaining uncertainty in viscoelastic dynamics, together enabling stable, sustained grasping.

</details>


### [37] [FLARE: A Failure-Aware Framework for Autonomous Correction and Recovery in Visual-Language Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26645)
*Ganlong Zhao,Zijia Tang,Xingping Chen,Zhanghui Kuang,Ye Tian,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLARE是一个通过"Retry"和"Reset"双机制赋予视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）错误恢复能力的框架。Retry机制通过注入扰动和桥接段使策略能自主处理执行偏差；Reset机制利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）离线分析失败视频，自动识别OOD状态并高效收集目标化的重置技能库，在在线MLLM监控器的仲裁下恢复环境至有效任务状态。实验表明该方法显著提升了接触密集操作任务的成功率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型通常仅在轨迹单调、无失败的"完美"演示上训练，导致其无法从常见执行错误（如抓取失败、物体掉落、意外碰撞）中恢复，性能脆弱且缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 1) Retry机制：通过在演示中注入扰动及解耦机器人位姿与环境状态的桥接段，使策略能够自主处理执行偏差。2) Reset机制：利用MLLM离线分析执行视频，自动识别OOD（越界）状态，高效定向收集小规模目标中心化的Reset技能库并训练以恢复环境至任务有效状态。3) 推理阶段：在线MLLM监控器仲裁任务执行与Reset技能之间的切换。

Result: 在具有挑战性的接触丰富型操作任务上的实验表明，FLARE显著提升了任务成功率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FLARE通过Retry与Reset的协同范式，有效弥补了VLA在错误恢复能力上的不足，为VLA在复杂长程操作任务中的实用化提供了系统性的鲁棒性增强方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action Models~(VLAs) have demonstrated significant promise in generalizing to complex, long-horizon robotic manipulation tasks. However, their performance remains brittle, as they are typically trained on trajectory-monotonic, failure-free demonstrations. This reliance on ``perfect" data leaves them unable to recover from common execution errors, such as a missed grasp, a dropped object, or an unexpected collision. In this paper, we propose FLARE, a novel framework that endows VLAs with robust error recovery capabilities through a ``Retry" and ``Reset" paradigm. First, we introduce a ``Retry" mechanism by injecting perturbation and bridging segments that decouple robot pose from environment state into demonstrations, enabling the policy to autonomously handle execution deviations. Second, to address critical, state-breaking (OOD) failures, we introduce a ``Reset" pipeline. We leverage an MLLM for offline failure analysis to automatically identify OOD states from execution videos. This analysis enables the efficient, targeted collection of a small library of object-centric ``Reset" skills, which are trained to restore the environment to a task-valid state. Our full framework integrates these learned policies. At inference, an online MLLM monitor arbitrates between task execution and ``Reset" skills. Experiments on challenging, contact-rich manipulation tasks show our approach significantly improves task success and robustness.

</details>


### [38] [Beyond the Proving Ground: Independent Public-Road Testing of Assisted Lane Change Systems using LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26669)
*Marcello Cellina,Akos Kriston,Antonio Migneco,Davide Maggi,Stefano Favelli,Fabrizio Re,Fabrizio Minarini,Andrea Nuovo,Riccardo Dona,Biagio Ciuffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在公共道路上独立测试高级驾驶辅助系统（ADAS）中辅助换道（ALC）功能的方法，并在法国A31高速公路上使用基于LiDAR的车辆检测与跟踪系统开展了测试活动。27个预定换道操作中，18个完成、9个被抑制，其中有6个案例系统允许了不符合UNECE R79法规最小安全距离要求的操作，3个案例在考虑测量不确定度后偏差仍具有统计显著性。这是首个评估ALC系统是否符合R79安全距离要求的公共道路测试活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用ADAS系统的测试对于型式认证和服役期间的安全与合规至关重要，但试车场场景无法反映真实道路驾驶复杂性，而地理围栏方法需要制造商配合且限制评估独立性，因此需要一种独立、真实的公共道路测试方法。

Method: 提出在公共道路上独立测试ALC系统的方法论：在法国A31高速公路上开展测试，测试车辆搭载基于LiDAR的车辆检测与跟踪系统，测试覆盖测试车辆与接管车辆之间不同的车距和车速组合，使用RTK-GNSS接收器评估检测与跟踪性能，并将记录的换道轨迹与UNECE R79法规的换道抑制要求进行比较。

Result: 在27个预定义换道操作中，18个完成、9个被抑制；6个案例中系统允许了不满足法规最小距离要求的操作，其中3个案例在考虑测量不确定度后偏差仍具有统计显著性。结果表明LiDAR感测适用于该测试目的。

Conclusion: 该方法是首个针对ALC系统R79安全距离合规性的公共道路测试活动，证明了基于LiDAR的感测方案的适用性，可为市场监督和未来法规修订提供支持，揭示型式认证程序未覆盖的真实道路行为。

Abstract: Testing of commercial Advanced Driver Assistance Systems is essential to ensure safety and compliance during type approval and in service operation. However, proving ground scenarios may not reflect real world driving complexity, while geo fencing can require manufacturer collaboration and limit assessment independence. This work presents a methodology for independently testing Assisted Lane Change systems on public roads. A campaign on the A31 French motorway used a test vehicle equipped with a LiDAR based vehicle detection and tracking system. Tests covered combinations of inter vehicle distance and speed between the test vehicle and the take over vehicle. Real time kinematic global navigation satellite system receivers assessed detection and tracking performance. Recorded lane change trajectories were compared with the lane change suppression requirements of UNECE Regulation Number 79. Of 27 predefined lane change manoeuvres, 18 were completed and 9 suppressed. In 6 cases, the system allowed manoeuvres that did not meet regulatory minimum distance requirements. In 3 cases, the deviation remained statistically significant after accounting for measurement uncertainty. To the authors knowledge, this is the first public road campaign designed to assess Assisted Lane Change compliance with Regulation Number 79 safety distance requirements. The results demonstrate the suitability of LiDAR based sensing for this purpose. The methodology can support market surveillance and future regulatory revisions by revealing real world behaviours not covered by approval procedures.

</details>


### [39] [PredVLA: A Sub-Million-Parameter Predictive-Coding Policy for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26673)
*Hiroki Sawada,Shunichi Kasahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PredVLA是一种仅含68万可训练参数、无需机器人数据预训练的语言条件预测编码策略，在LIBERO基准上取得86.9%（短时域）和75.4%（含长时域）的平均成功率，远超参数匹配的Transformer和LSTM策略（分别为3.7倍和7.4倍），并通过预测误差驱动的潜在状态推断实现可测的在线矫正机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言条件机器人操控基准由大规模预训练的视觉-语言-动作模型主导，但模型规模对语言条件控制性能的必要性尚不明确，且缺乏对轻量级替代控制架构的竞争力评估。作者希望探究在极小参数预算下，完全不同的控制架构是否仍能保持竞争力。

Method: PredVLA采用分层生成式循环动力学模型，预测视觉特征和本体感知，观测仅通过来自感觉预测误差的在线推断影响潜在状态。该架构不使用机器人数据预训练，仅含0.68百万可训练参数。通过禁用预测误差推断可实现精确的开环对照，从而直接量化观测驱动矫正的贡献。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，PredVLA在三个短时域套件上平均成功率达86.9%，包含长时域套件时为75.4%。在相同冻结前端、示范数据、动作解码器和评估协议的控制比较下，其平均成功率分别是参数匹配的Transformer和LSTM策略的3.7倍和7.4倍。

Conclusion: 亚百万参数的循环生成策略即可在现代语言条件操控基准上取得强性能，同时预测编码框架提供了预测误差驱动的在线状态矫正的显式机制，挑战了大模型在机器人操控中不可或缺的假设。

Abstract: Large pretrained vision-language-action models dominate modern robot-manipulation benchmarks, but it remains unclear how much model scale is necessary for strong language-conditioned control, or whether fundamentally different control architectures can remain competitive at much smaller parameter budgets. We present PredVLA, a language-conditioned predictive-coding policy with only 0.68 million trainable network parameters and no robot-data pretraining, whose hierarchical generative recurrent dynamics predict visual features and proprioception while observations influence latent state only through online inference from the resulting sensory prediction errors. On LIBERO, PredVLA achieves an 86.9% mean success rate across the three short-horizon suites and 75.4% when the long-horizon suite is included. Under a controlled comparison using the same frozen front end, demonstrations, action decoder, and evaluation protocol, PredVLA achieves 3.7x and 7.4x mean success rates of parameter-matched Transformer and LSTM policies, respectively. The predictive-coding formulation also makes the contribution of observation-driven correction directly measurable: because observations influence the recurrent state only through prediction-error-based latent inference, disabling this inference yields an exact open-loop control condition. Together, these results show that a sub-million-parameter recurrent generative policy can achieve strong performance on modern language-conditioned manipulation benchmarks while providing an explicit mechanism for prediction-error-driven online state correction.

</details>


### [40] [Residual Deep Reinforcement Learning-Based Computed Torque Control for a Cable-Driven Lower-Limb Rehabilitation Robot under Disturbances and Parametric Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26739)
*Mohammad-Hossein Fakouri,Ali Keymasi-Khalaji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种残差深度强化学习增强的计算力矩控制框架，用于绳索驱动下肢康复机器人的精确轨迹跟踪。该框架由计算力矩控制生成标称指令，有界DDPG策略仅提供额外的补偿力矩，在多种仿真条件下相比纯计算力矩控制显著改善了跟踪精度和扰动抑制能力，同时保留了可解释的模型基控制结构并满足可行性约束检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绳索驱动下肢康复机器人的精确轨迹跟踪面临模型不确定性、外部扰动、关节约束和绳索仅能拉动（pull-only）驱动等挑战。传统模型基控制器对模型失配敏感，而纯学习基控制会降低透明性并使约束感知操作复杂化。因此需要一种既能保持模型基控制可解释结构，又能增强鲁棒性的混合控制方法。

Method: 提出残差深度强化学习增强的计算力矩控制框架：计算力矩控制（CTC）生成标称控制指令，一个有界的DDPG（Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient）策略仅在标称指令基础上提供额外的补偿力矩。在仿真中评估了标称、不确定、扰动、组合和泛化等条件，并进行了轨迹跟踪、关节极限、绳索需求、工作空间可行性和绳索雅可比等诊断分析。

Result: 在全部评估条件下，残差控制器相比纯计算力矩控制改善了轨迹跟踪精度和扰动抑制能力，同时保留了可解释的模型基指令结构并满足了代表性评估中的可行性检查。更广泛的测试表明，跟踪改进可以在代表性案例之外持续存在，但同时也暴露了依赖于轨迹的约束限制。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory tracking in cable-driven lower-limb rehabilitation robots is challenging because model uncertainty, external disturbances, joint constraints, and pull-only cable actuation can degrade nominal control performance. Conventional model-based controllers provide an interpretable control structure but remain sensitive to model mismatch, whereas fully learning-based control can reduce transparency and complicate constraint-aware operation. This study proposes a residual deep reinforcement learning-enhanced computed torque control framework in which computed torque control generates the nominal command and a bounded Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient policy supplies only an additional compensating torque. The approach is evaluated in simulation under nominal, uncertain, disturbed, combined, and generalization conditions, together with trajectory-tracking, joint-limit, cable-demand, workspace-feasibility, and cable-Jacobian diagnostics. Across the evaluated conditions, the residual controller improves tracking and disturbance rejection relative to computed torque control while preserving the interpretable model-based command structure and satisfying the reported feasibility checks in the representative evaluation. Broader tests indicate that tracking improvements can persist beyond the representative case while also exposing trajectory-dependent constraint limitations. These results support bounded residual learning as a practical robustness-enhancement strategy for simulation-based rehabilitation robot control and motivate further constraint-aware and experimental validation.

TL;DR: 本文提出MeshPriorDiT，一种层次化布料动力学预测模型，将未来布料运动分解为结构化的网格先验和生成式残差。通过动作条件网格GNN预测多步顶点位移作为先验轨迹，再用残差DiT（基于条件流匹配）生成残差运动，最后通过材质邻接关系对残差进行重缩放和解码。在三个布料操作任务的15步自回归预测中，MeshPriorDiT比GNN-Only基线平均Global MSE降低43.42%，比DiT-DDPM基线降低75.03%，同时保持与GNN-Only相当的Edge-strain MSE。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布料动力学预测方法存在两类范式各自的局限：基于网格的GNN能捕获局部物理响应，但消息传递范围有限，难以协调拓扑远距离区域，且自回归累积误差严重；基于Transformer的动力学模型虽能通过全局注意力捕获长程交互，但缺乏显式材质连接信息，需要从数据中学习局部拓扑响应。两种范式均无法同时兼顾局部变形合理性与长程协调能力，因此需要一种融合两者优势的层次化方法。

Method: MeshPriorDiT采用两阶段层次化结构：(1) 动作条件网格GNN预测多步顶点位移，生成尊重材质拓扑和抓取约束的参考轨迹（网格先验）；(2) 条件于历史状态、规划动作和网格先验，Residual DiT通过条件流匹配联合生成先验未捕获的残差运动；(3) 生成的残差通过材质邻接关系进行重缩放和解码，以协调相邻顶点间的校正。

Result: 在三个布料操作任务的15步自回归预测中，MeshPriorDiT相比GNN-Only基线平均Global MSE降低43.42%，相比DiT-DDPM基线降低75.03%，同时Edge-strain MSE与GNN-Only相当，说明其在提升全局精度（长程协调）的同时保持了局部物理响应的质量。

Conclusion: MeshPriorDiT通过将GNN的材质拓扑先验与DiT的全局生成能力有机结合，有效解决了单一范式在布料动力学预测中的局限性，实现了局部变形合理性与长程协调能力的兼顾，验证了"网格先验+生成式残差"层次化架构在布料操作动力学预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Action-conditioned cloth dynamics prediction requires both locally plausible deformation and long-range coordination. Existing approaches largely follow two paradigms. Mesh-based GNNs capture local physical responses through material connectivity. However, their finite message-passing range limits coordination between topologically distant regions, while autoregressive rollouts tend to accumulate prediction errors. Transformer-based dynamics models capture long-range interactions through global attention, but often operate without explicit material connectivity and must learn local topological responses directly from data. We propose MeshPriorDiT, a hierarchical dynamics model that decomposes future cloth motion into a structured mesh prior and a generative residual. An action-conditioned mesh GNN first predicts multi-step vertex displacements, yielding a reference trajectory that respects material topology and grasp constraints. Conditioned on historical states, planned actions, and the mesh prior, a Residual DiT then uses conditional flow matching to jointly generate the residual motion not captured by the prior. The generated residual is further rescaled and decoded using material adjacency to coordinate corrections across neighboring vertices. We evaluate MeshPriorDiT on 15-step autoregressive rollouts across three cloth manipulation tasks. Averaged over the three tasks, MeshPriorDiT reduces average Global MSE by 43.42% relative to the GNN-Only baseline and by 75.03% relative to the DiT-DDPM baseline, while maintaining a favorable Edge-strain MSE comparable to that of GNN-Only.

</details>

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，校正转向偏移后横向跟踪误差（CTE）得到大幅降低，验证了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: EKF联合在线估计策略可以替代传统手动标定流程，有效减少WMR路径跟踪中的失准问题，提升安全关键环境下的可靠性与跟踪精度。

Abstract: Accurate steering sensing and LiDAR-to-vehicle extrinsics are crucial for reliable path tracking in warehouse mobile robots (WMRs); miscalibration often leads to snaking, weaving, and elevated cross-track error (CTE). In practice, steering ``zero'' is commonly set manually (e.g., eyeballing straightness via a PS4 joystick), while LiDAR extrinsics are assumed from CAD and may drift after maintenance. Such static, manual procedures frequently cause miscalibration in safety-critical environments. This paper presents an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF)--based method for online estimation of steering offset and planar LiDAR extrinsics within a bicycle-kinematics model, providing a principled alternative to manual calibration. Experiments on real datasets show that correcting steering offset reduces CTE substantially, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线学习框架，使双臂机器人能在数分钟内直接在真实硬件上学会多种杂耍模式，即使存在显著的sim2real差距。核心思想是学习应建立在机器人现有知识之上而非替代之，通过正则化记忆学习结合全局先验模型与局部经验模型，并构建互可达集合保障实时学习的安全性，最终在不到5分钟的真实交互中习得五种经典三球杂耍模式。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 1) 正则化记忆学习：从累积经验中学习局部模型，同时保留全局先验模型用于经验稀疏区域的推断，实现对新经验的稳定高效在线学习。2) 安全机制：构建互可达集合（mutually reachable set），确保连续投掷与接球之间的转换安全，不会使任一机械臂进入违反关节或执行器限制的状态。3) 整体框架：将上述组件集成，使配备多指灵巧手与机载视觉的双臂机器人能够安全地在真实硬件上反复练习和改进。

Conclusion: 本文证明了不完美的先验模型在实际任务中具有极大价值，学习应该是在现有知识基础上逐步精炼而非推倒重建。这一工作为机器人利用先验知识进行安全、高效的持续真实世界学习提供了范例，指向未来机器人通过自身经验不断自我完善的通用路径。

Abstract: We present an online learning framework that enables a bimanual robot to acquire diverse juggling patterns directly on physical hardware within minutes, even with a significant sim2real gap. One of the most important lessons from this work is that a model, even when far from reality, can be extremely useful for learning. This motivates a central philosophy of our approach: learning should build upon the robot's current knowledge rather than replace it. Our regularized memory-based learning puts this principle into practice by learning a local model from accumulated experience while retaining the global prior model to extrapolate where experience is sparse. This enables efficient and stable online learning from each new experience without resorting to uninformed exploration over a vast space of possible behaviors. Equally important to continual on-robot learning is safety, allowing the robot to repeatedly practice and improve in the real world. We construct a mutually reachable set that allows safe transitions between successive throws and catches, without driving either arm into a state from which its next action would require violating the robot's joint or actuator limits. Together, these ideas enable a bimanual robot with multi-fingered hands and onboard vision to safely learn and compose five canonical three-ball juggling patterns, including cascade, tennis, half-shower, shower, and box, within less than 5 minutes of real-world interaction. More broadly, this work points toward robots that build upon imperfect prior knowledge and continually refine their behavior through their own real-world experience.

Result: 在LIBERO基准上达到97.63±0.26%的平均成功率，其中LIBERO Long为96.60±0.87%；在12个RoboTwin任务中Clean/Randomized成功率分别为85.5%和84.2%。在长时程多阶段操控任务上优势最为明显。控制实验表明动作预测同时依赖历史内容和时序顺序。

Conclusion: TemporalFlow-VLA提供了一种紧凑、物理grounded的执行历史利用接口，无需在部署时进行显式运动估计或几何处理，即可有效利用有序执行历史提升多阶段操控性能。

This is especially problematic in multi-stage manipulation, where visually similar states may require different actions depending on prior execution. To address this challenge, we present TemporalFlow-VLA, which learns compact execution history through physically grounded temporal supervision. Using recorded robot states, robot geometry, and calibrated cameras, we construct robot-surface temporal flow as a training-only target and supervise two execution-aligned temporal queries that provide structured history to the action expert. The geometric supervision path is not evaluated at deployment. TemporalFlow-VLA achieves 97.63 +/- 0.26% average success on LIBERO, including 96.60 +/- 0.87% on LIBERO Long, and 85.5%/84.2% Clean/Randomized success across 12 RoboTwin tasks. It shows its clearest advantage over prior methods on longer-horizon, multi-stage manipulation. Controlled history interventions show that action prediction depends on both historical content and temporal order. With asynchronous feature caching, temporal conditioning maintains single-frame-level server-side sampling latency without additional historical-encoding overhead. Overall, TemporalFlow-VLA provides a compact, physically grounded interface for exploiting ordered execution history without explicit motion estimation or geometric processing at deployment.

Abstract: Robotic grippers face substantial challenges in grasping and manipulating thin objects. Most existing grippers rely on highly precise approach and grasp motions, which limits robustness and reduces applicability. This paper explores thin-object grasping using books as a representative example. Here, we propose a novel solution that integrates an active surface with underactuated compliance to achieve stable grasping of thin objects without complex control. First, an underactuated gripper with an active surface is designed. The active-surface thumb performs in-hand repositioning of the target book without requiring adjustments of the robot arm or the other fingers, while the underactuated fingers establish compliant contact conditions with the environment, and the reconfigurable structure enables reliable grasping of books under different configurations. Second, we establish a kinematic model of the gripper, and determine the initial grasp postures for two representative scenarios (books lying flat on a desktop and books vertically packed in a shelf). Third, by analyzing the physical model of a book lying on a table and its interaction with the gripper and the environment, we systematically optimize the structural parameters and grasping strategy. Finally, extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed gripper and strategy. The results demonstrate strong robustness and adaptability when grasping thin objects placed flat (including books, paper, fabric, plastic film, and mouse pad), as well as a high success rate when grasping vertically packed books. Moreover, the proposed gripper can reliably complete long sequential "grasp-place" tasks.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在Stonefish模拟器基础上进行物理与传感器层面的扩展：添加随机IMU和DVL漂移、磁力计扰动、高阶流体动力学、地形力学（terramechanics）、压力驱动的环境变异以及基于物理机理的水下光学模型，所有模块均设计为支持实时仿真。

Result: 该框架在保持实时仿真的前提下，能够更真实地模拟深海AUV、ROV、着陆器、ASV和水下滑翔机所处的受力与传感器测量环境，为深海条件下的导航、感知和自主研究提供了实用基础。但复杂流固耦合和完全的环境随机性建模仍存在挑战。

Conclusion: 本文推动水下仿真向更具代表性的深海作业条件发展，虽然仍面临复杂流体-结构相互作用和完全环境随机性等残余挑战，但所提出的框架为深海条件下的导航、感知与自主性研究提供了实际可行的工作基础。

Abstract: Many recent underwater simulators emphasize visual realism at the expense of physical fidelity, focusing on shallow-water effects with limited relevance in deep-water environments and high computational cost. In this work, we shift the focus toward deep-sea physical and sensor realism. We present a physics- and sensor-grounded extension of the Stonefish simulator that augments its hydrodynamic models with stochastic IMU and DVL drift, magnetometer disturbances, higher-order hydrodynamics, terramechanics, pressure-driven environmental variability, and physically based underwater optics. These additions are designed to better capture the forces and measurements shaping the behavior of deep-ocean AUVs, ROVs, landers, ASVs, and gliders, while remaining compatible with real-time simulation. This work advances underwater simulation toward more representative deep-sea operating conditions, which is particularly relevant for long-duration navigation and learning-based autonomy, where inaccurate sensor and environmental models introduce non-physical artifacts and overly optimistic performance. While challenges remain, including complex fluid-structure interactions and full environmental stochasticity, the proposed framework provides a practical foundation for navigation, perception, and autonomy research under deep-sea conditions.

</details>


### [47] [Contact-Aided Factor-Graph Localization for Underwater Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26932)
*Michele Grimaldi,Yosaku Maeda,Hitoshi Kakami,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot,Tomoya Inoue*
