TL;DR: 提出一种接触辅助因子图定位框架，将水下机器人进行近底采样时的物理接触事件建模为高置信度几何约束，与视觉里程计、目标检测和惯性-DVL数据紧耦合，在感知退化环境下显著降低轨迹漂移并提升目标重访精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下机器人在近底采样时的精确状态估计面临挑战：低空作业下朝下相机面对无纹理平底海床会造成尺度模糊、侧向退化及特征跟踪不一致；惯性-DVL融合缺乏结构性漂移校正机制，导致在感知退化环境中定位精度不足。

Method: 提出Contact-Aided Factor-Graph Localization框架：1) 将吸盘式机械臂接触事件建模为高置信度因子，引入隐式回环闭合；2) 视觉里程计相对位姿因子与地标方位-距离因子根据内点统计进行不确定性缩放，防止弱视觉帧破坏估计器稳定性；3) 系统支持在线完全初始化，实现自适应视觉里程计、学习式目标检测与机载传感器的紧耦合。

Result: 在水池、港口及仿真环境中的实验表明，与传统滤波导航及无接触图优化方法相比，接触诱导约束显著降低轨迹漂移并提升目标重访精度。

Conclusion: 物理交互可作为感知退化水下环境中的一种定位原语，接触事件能够提供无需外观匹配的隐式回环约束，弥补视觉感知不足和惯性导航漂移问题，为近底采样作业提供可靠的定位支撑。

Abstract: Accurate state estimation for autonomous underwater vehicles performing close-range seafloor sampling remains challenging. In low-altitude operation, down-looking cameras over featureless planar seabeds produce scale ambiguity, lateral degeneracy, and inconsistent feature tracking. Meanwhile, inertial-Doppler Velocity Log (DVL) fusion alone provides no mechanism for structural drift correction. We propose a Contact-Aided Factor-Graph Localization framework that treats physical interaction as an informative geometric constraint within a smoothing-based localization formulation. The method tightly fuses suction-based manipulator contact events with adaptive visual odometry, learned object detections, and on-board sensors. Visual odometry relative-pose factors and landmark bearing-range factors are uncertainty-scaled according to inlier statistics to prevent visually weak frames from destabilizing the estimator, while contact events are modeled as high-confidence factors that induce implicit loop closures without appearance-based place recognition. Furthermore, the system can fully initialize online during motion. Experimental evaluation in tanks, harbor, and simulation environments demonstrates that contact-induced constraints significantly reduce trajectory drift and improve object revisit accuracy compared to filtering-based navigation and contact-free graph formulations. These results highlight the role of embodied physical interaction as a localization primitive in perception-degraded underwater environments

</details>


### [48] [4DSynth: Controllable Procedural World Synthesis for Dynamic Embodied Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26947)
*Zehao Qi,Haochen Luo,Jia-Wang Bian,Zeyu Ma,Shuyang Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 4DSynth是一个可控的程序化系统，能将自然语言描述、蓝图掩膜或单张照片转化为可编辑的4D环境（含显式几何、动画角色、无碰撞轨迹和物理就绪状态），并基于此构建了4DSynth-Nav交互式导航基准。实验表明两个VLM模型在三个难度层级上大多数任务失败且早期即停滞，但程序化可控性保证了每个失败可复现、每个难度轴可独立调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需要视觉多样、物理交互且随时间变化的环境。程序化模拟器能生成大规模交互场景，4D生成器能产生动态视觉效果，但将二者结合仍需要大量人工劳动，且结果难以编辑和控制，无法规模化复用。

Method: 4DSynth通过统一的几何接地表示来共享多条场景生成路线（自然语言、蓝图掩膜、单张照片），同一管线处理动画、相机规划、渲染和任务生成。其程序化可控性使环境可编辑、物理仿真就绪。在此基础上构建了4DSynth-Nav导航基准，包含三个难度层级。

Result: 在4DSynth-Nav基准上，两个视觉语言模型在三个难度层级中大多数任务失败并在早期子任务后停滞。程序化可控性保证了每个失败场景可复现，且每个难度轴可独立调节。

Conclusion: 4DSynth同时提供了可控的生成管线与可扩展的基准，为开发和评估具身智能体提供了实践基础。

Abstract: Embodied agents need environments that are visually diverse, physically interactive, and changing over time. Procedural simulators can generate large interactive scene collections, and recent 4D generators produce compelling visual dynamics. Combining these properties in one environment, however, still demands extensive manual effort, and the result is rarely editable or controllable enough to reuse at scale.
  We present 4DSynth, a controllable procedural system that turns a natural-language description, a blueprint mask, or a single photograph into an editable 4D environment with explicit geometry, animated actors, collision-free trajectories, and physics-ready simulation state. Multiple scene routes share one geometry-grounded representation, so the same pipeline handles animation, camera planning, rendering, and task generation.
  To validate the full pipeline, we construct 4DSynth-Nav, an interactive navigation benchmark generated entirely from 4DSynth's procedural scenes. Two vision-language models evaluated across three difficulty tiers both fail the majority of tasks and stall after early subtasks. The same procedural controllability that produces these environments also makes each failure reproducible and each difficulty axis independently tunable. This paper presents both a controllable generation pipeline and the scalable benchmark it enables, offering a practical foundation for developing and evaluating embodied agents.

</details>


### [49] [Arbitrary-Order Hermite Interpolation of Rigid-Motion Jets via Hyper-Multidual Quaternions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27000)
*Daniel Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究有限阶刚体运动喷流的双线性插值问题，使用单位对偶四元数的超多对偶（HMD）扩展。作者证明了时间变换与相对描述子的幺正性，推导了递归系数约束及局部可实现性的逆命题；将螺旋线性插值（ScLERP）代数扩展到单位HMD四元数，指出直接HMD-ScLERP插值存在非完整性缺陷，并提出通过对数坐标Hermite多项式插值加指数映射的完整性替代方案。数值实验验证了二阶及三阶情况下的端点和接触缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体运动的高阶插值（包括位姿及其导数）在机器人学、计算机动画和运动规划等领域有广泛应用。传统的ScLERP仅能插值端点位姿，无法处理端点携带的速度、加速度等高阶运动信息。将单位对偶四元数扩展到超多对偶（HMD）四元数，可在统一代数框架内同时编码位姿及其导数，但需解决完整性问题——即HMD曲线是否真正对应其位姿投影的时间喷流。

Method: 论文首先引入阶n多对偶（MD）代数与超多对偶四元数，建立时间变换与相对描述子的幺正性并推导递归系数约束，给出对数图可容许条件下的局部可实现性逆定理。然后将ScLERP代数扩展到单位HMD四元数（直接HMD-ScLERP），推导系数准则及接触缺陷表达式。最后提出完整性替代方案：将端点变换映射到对数对偶四元数坐标，应用匹配到n阶导数的(2n+1)次Hermite多项式，再通过指数映射提升回流形。同时利用HMD算术恢复高阶加速度场而无需显式微分dexp。

Result: 直接HMD-ScLERP在任意端点喷流下一般非完整，论文给出了精确的系数判据及端点和一阶内点接触缺陷的显式表达式。对数坐标Hermite插值方案可保证完整性。旋转与完整SE(3)二阶测试（外加三阶多项式检查）均复现了所述缺陷与端点喷流，验证了理论分析的正确性。

Conclusion: 论文建立了HMD四元数框架下刚体运动高阶插值的完整理论：直接代数扩展ScLERP虽能匹配端点变换但无法保证曲线的时间完整性；通过对数坐标Hermite插值加指数映射可实现完整的有限阶刚体运动喷流插值。HMD算术为高阶运动学量的计算提供了无需微分dexp的高效途径。

Abstract: We study bilateral interpolation of finite-order rigid-motion jets represented by unit dual quaternions. An order-$n$ multidual (MD) algebra is the truncated polynomial algebra $\mathbb{R}[\varepsilon]/(\varepsilon^{n+1})$; hyper-multidual (HMD) quaternions are dual quaternions with coefficients in this algebra. Temporal HMD transforms encode a pose and its derivatives, whereas a generic HMD curve need not be the temporal jet of its pose projection; we call this requirement holonomicity. We show that a temporal transform and its relative descriptor are unitary and derive recursive coefficient constraints, together with a local realizability converse in an admissible logarithm chart. We then extend screw linear interpolation (ScLERP) algebraically to unit HMD quaternions. Although it matches complete endpoint transforms, direct HMD--ScLERP is generically non-holonomic for arbitrary endpoint jets. We give a coefficient criterion and explicit endpoint and first-order interior contact defects. A holonomic alternative is obtained by mapping endpoint transforms to logarithmic dual-quaternion coordinates, applying the degree-$(2n+1)$ Hermite polynomial that matches derivatives through order $n$, and lifting by the exponential. HMD arithmetic also recovers higher-order rigid-motion acceleration fields without explicit differentiation of $\mathrm{dexp}$. Rotation and full $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ tests through second order, with an additional third-order polynomial check, reproduce the stated defects and endpoint jets.

</details>


### [50] [Riemann-1.0: An Embodied World Action Model for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27033)
*Haofeng Sun,Jiangbo Pei,Fei Kang,Zexiang Liu,Yaokun Li,Boyi Jiang,Hua Xue,Cindy Zhou,Wei Li,Yichen Wei,Mengyin An,Fanliang Zhao,Biao Jiang,Zile Wang,Yang Liu,Yangguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Riemann-1.0，一个完全因果自回归的世界动作模型（WAM），统一建模多视角视觉观测、机器人状态和具身动作，同时作为可执行的机器人策略和多具身视觉世界模拟器，通过渐进式具身预训练在20万小时交互数据上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）存在多种范式局限：联合生成范式、视频优先预测范式、解耦建模范式，均未将机器人策略执行与世界模拟统一在单一模型中。作者希望用一个统一的因果自回归模型同时实现在线策略执行和动作条件世界模拟，并解决跨异构数据源（人类视频、手持夹爪演示、异构机器人轨迹）的具身经验规模化问题。

Method: Riemann-1.0将多视角视觉观测、机器人状态和具身特定动作建模为统一的因果自回归序列，将机器人动作和世界演化表示为因果状态转移。训练上提出渐进式具身预训练框架，在共享的世界动作建模目标下统一学习来自人类第一人称视频、手持夹爪演示和异构机器人轨迹的数据，逐步将大规模具身经验迁移为可执行的机器人操作能力。

Result: 在RoboTwin2.0上成功率94.3%，LIBERO上99.0%，长时域组合基准RoboCasa-365上62.6%，较此前最优方法提升8.4%。在真实世界长时域操作任务中SR达85.0%，PSR达94.4%，比最强开源基线在SR上超出15%。

Conclusion: 统一的世界动作建模结合渐进式具身预训练，能够有效将大规模异构具身经验转化为可泛化的机器人操作能力，证明了单一模型同时承担策略执行与世界模拟的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce Riemann-1.0, a fully causal autoregressive World Action Model for embodied intelligence. Riemann-1.0 jointly models multi-view visual observations, robot states, and embodiment-specific actions within a unified causal autoregressive sequence, representing robot actions and world evolution as causal state transitions. Unlike existing WAMs based on joint generation, video-first prediction, or decoupled modeling paradigms, Riemann-1.0 unifies online robot policy execution and action-conditioned world simulation within a single model, enabling it to function as both an executable robot policy and a multi-embodiment visual world simulator. To scale embodied experience across heterogeneous data sources, we further develop a progressive embodied pretraining framework that unifies learning from egocentric human videos, handheld-gripper demonstrations, and heterogeneous robot trajectories under a shared World Action Modeling objective. Built upon 200K+ hours of interaction data, Riemann-1.0 progressively transfers large-scale embodied experience into executable robot manipulation capabilities. Riemann-1.0 achieves state-of-the-art performance across both simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks. It achieves success rates of 94.3% on RoboTwin2.0, 99.0% on LIBERO, and 62.6% on the long-horizon compositional benchmark RoboCasa-365, outperforming the previous best method by 8.4% On long-horizon real-world manipulation tasks, Riemann-1.0 achieves a Success Rate (SR) of 85.0% and a Progress Success Rate (PSR) of 94.4%, exceeding the strongest open-source baseline by 15% in SR. These results demonstrate that unified World Action Modeling together with progressive embodied pretraining effectively transforms large-scale embodied experience into generalizable robot manipulation capabilities.

</details>


### [51] [GRAFT: Grounded and Efficient Online Reinforcement Adaptation for Fine-Grained Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27079)
*Yibo Qiu,Haoliang Ye,Shu'ang Sun,Zan Huang,Ronald X Xu,Mingzhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRAFT是一个高效在线VLA适配框架，通过区域级监督学习视图特定的视觉锚点，聚焦任务相关的局部线索，并结合单步动作生成与缓存视觉-语言前缀复用加速在线学习，在四个生物医学操作任务中成功率提升25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略为机器人操作提供了强先验，但将其在线适配到精细生物医学任务仍然具有挑战性。任务成功往往依赖细微、视图依赖的视觉线索，而任务级奖励对哪些区域重要提供很少的指导，使得从有限的真实机器人交互中学习任务相关的视觉基础变得困难。此外，VLA推理和基于回放的更新的计算成本进一步约束了在线适配。

Method: GRAFT使用区域级监督学习视图特定的视觉锚点，将感知聚焦于任务相关的局部线索，且部署时无需区域提议。它进一步结合单步动作生成和缓存视觉-语言前缀复用来加速在线学习，从而在有限的交互预算内实现高效的策略适配。

Result: 在四个生物医学操作任务中，GRAFT在匹配的适配预算下将成功率提升了25个百分点，同时降低了在线策略更新的计算开销。

Conclusion: GRAFT通过接地感知（grounded perception）实现了高效的在线VLA适配，证明了区域级监督和计算优化手段能够有效解决生物医学精细操作中视觉基础学习与在线更新效率的难题。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies provide strong priors for robot manipulation, yet adapting them online to fine-grained biomedical tasks remains challenging. Task success often hinges on subtle, view-dependent visual cues, while task-level rewards provide little guidance about which regions matter, making it difficult to learn task-relevant visual grounding from limited real-robot interaction. Online adaptation is further constrained by the computational cost of VLA inference and replay-based updates. We introduce GRAFT (Grounded Reinforcement Adaptation for Fast Task Learning), a framework for efficient online VLA adaptation through grounded perception. GRAFT uses region-level supervision to learn view-specific visual anchors that focus perception on task-relevant local cues without requiring region proposals at deployment. It further combines single-step action generation with cached visual-language prefix reuse to accelerate online learning. Across four biomedical manipulation tasks, GRAFT improves success rates by 25 percentage points under matched adaptation budgets, while reducing the computational overhead of online policy updates.

</details>


### [52] [Pass the Bucket: Efficient, Robust, Local Load Balancing for Teams of Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27085)
*Tobias Wallner,Dominik Krupke,Arne Schmidt,Sándor P. Fekete*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究异构机器人群体在无中央控制的一维受限空间中通过"桶队"机制实现去中心化任务共享与负载均衡，提出基于"令牌"的局部稳定机制来消除混沌振荡，并通过事件驱动仿真验证了系统的收敛性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人群体在协作运输等任务中需要去中心化、自组织的负载均衡机制，但简单的"桶队"机制可能导致混沌的系统行为（持续振荡），缺乏收敛保证，因此需要一种仅依赖局部传感（碰撞墙面或邻居）的稳定化方案。

Method: 提出了基于"令牌"的局部辅助稳定机制：在一次相遇后临时使机器人减速，从而消除持续振荡。比较了单边界令牌与双向多令牌两种策略，并优化了减速因子以加速系统收敛。采用事件驱动仿真评估收敛时间与鲁棒性。

Result: 令牌机制纯局部的改动即可消除持续振荡并实现系统向稳定状态的收敛；通过比较单令牌与双向令牌策略并优化减速因子可加速收敛；在机器人删除、位置或速度抖动等多种扰动下系统能够可靠地重新收敛。

Conclusion: 该局部、实用的令牌机制为异构机器人团队的鲁棒负载均衡提供了一种有效工具，可作为更复杂场景（如协调运动规划）的基础机制。

Abstract: We study the problem of decentralized, self-organized task sharing for a swarm of heterogeneous robots that collaborate in transportation or other objectives that require coordinated motion planning. To this end, we present theoretical and practical results for the simple but effective mechanism of \emph{bucket brigades} for load balancing, in which a team of heterogenous robots share a spatial task in a confined, one-dimensional space, while only being able to sense collisions with neighbors or walls. The goal is to optimize throughput of the overall system, without central control or information, aiming at an interval partition proportional to robot velocities. We address possible chaotic system behavior by developing a stabilization mechanism based on simple local aid, a ``token'', that temporarily decelerates robots after an encounter. This purely local change eliminates persistent oscillations, resulting in convergence towards a stable system state. We accelerate system convergence by comparing a single boundary token to ubiquitous two-directional tokens and optimizing the deceleration factor. Event-driven simulations report convergence times and robustness: For a large variety of perturbations (such as robot deletion, position or velocity jittering), the system reliably re-converges. The results suggest a local, practical mechanism for robust load balancing for heterogeneous teams of robots that promises an effective tool as basis for more complex scenarios.

</details>


### [53] [Active sensing to characterize the heterogeneity of plant stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27088)
*Ayman Laaroussi,Peter Hanappe,David Colliaux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自主机器人平台，通过结合3D植物重建、几何分析与运动规划，实现对植物叶片的靶向叶绿素荧光测量，超越传统被动成像的表型分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数表型平台仅依赖基于图像的测量，但高级植物表征需要整合主动生理传感模态（如叶绿素荧光）。现有系统缺乏能自动定位合适的叶面测量点并规划无碰撞机器人轨迹的能力。

Method: 系统从多视角数据重建植物密集3D模型，基于方向、可达性和传感约束提取候选叶表面，再将目标集成到任务级规划框架中，引导机械臂末端执行器到达精确接触或近接触配置以进行点式荧光采集。

Result: 该平台实现了自动化、可重复且空间分辨的生理测量，超越了被动成像的局限，通过紧密耦合感知、几何推理和操作，提供了机器人驱动的植物表型分析新方法。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了将主动生理传感与机器人操作相结合在植物表型分析中的可行性，为自主农业检测和植物感知式操作开辟了新方向。

Abstract: While most phenotyping platforms rely primarily on image-based measurements, advanced plant characterization requires the integration of active physiological sensing modali- ties such as chlorophyll fluorescence. We present an autonomous robotic platform designed to perform targeted fluorescence measurements on plant leaves. The system combines 3D plant reconstruction, geometric analysis, and motion planning to localize suitable measurement points and generate collision-free trajectories for a robotic manipulator.
  A dense 3D model of the plant is reconstructed from multi-view data and used to extract candidate leaf surfaces based on orientation, accessibility, and sensing constraints. These targets are then integrated into a task-level planning framework that guides the end-effector to precise contact or near-contact configurations required for point-based fluorescence acquisition. The platform enables automated, repeatable, and spatially resolved physiological measurements that go beyond passive imaging. By tightly coupling perception, geometric reasoning, and manipulation, the proposed system provides a robotics-driven approach to high-resolution plant phenotyping and opens new directions for autonomous agricultural inspection and plant-aware manipulation.

</details>


### [54] [Planning a Shared Modular Fixture Layout Across Robotic Disassembly Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27151)
*Haohui Pan,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块化真空夹具系统，为整个拆解序列规划统一的支撑布局，通过扩散模型生成物理信息初始配置并用贝叶斯优化细化，使螺丝刀和剃须刀的机器人拆解无需夹具重配置即可稳定进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则形状产品的机器人拆解中，随着组件逐步移除，支撑面、质量分布和任务载荷持续变化，为单一工件状态设计的夹具布局在后期的拆解阶段可能失效，因此需要在整个拆解序列上进行统一的支撑规划。

Method: 提出模块化真空夹具系统，在混合连续-离散布局空间中搜索统一支撑配置；采用去噪扩散概率模型生成物理信息驱动的初始配置，再通过贝叶斯优化进行细化，以满足跨阶段的重复评估需求。

Result: 机器人螺丝与组件移除实验验证了规划布局的拆解可行性；11项定向载荷测试量化了稳定性，实测操作载荷与方向响应对比得到螺丝刀平均经验稳定裕度66.9%，剃须刀为81.6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，产品特定的共享布局能够在整个测试的机器人拆解序列中提供稳定支撑，无需夹具重配置。

Abstract: Stable support remains challenging in robotic disassembly of irregularly shaped products. As components are progressively removed, the available support surfaces, mass distribution, and task loads change throughout the process. A fixture layout designed for one workpiece state may therefore become infeasible at later stages, motivating unified support planning over the complete disassembly sequence. This paper presents a modular vacuum-based fixturing system that plans one shared support configuration for the complete disassembly sequence of a screwdriver or shaver, allowing each sequence to proceed without fixture reconfiguration. To search the mixed continuous--discrete layout space under repeated cross-stage evaluation, a denoising diffusion probabilistic model generates physics-informed initial configurations that are refined through Bayesian optimization. Robotic screw and component-removal experiments verified the disassembly feasibility of the planned layouts, while 11 directional-load tests quantified their stability. Comparisons between the measured operational loads and directional responses yielded mean empirical stability margins of 66.9% for the screwdriver and 81.6% for the shaver. These results demonstrate that a product-specific shared layout can provide stable support throughout the tested robotic disassembly sequence.

</details>


### [55] [Task-space model-based control of pneumatic soft actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27186)
*Nithin S. Kumar,Joshua Gaston,D. Caleb Rucker,Eric J. Barth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非最小坐标离散弹性杆模型的实时动态任务空间控制与估计框架，用于平面气动软体执行器。该框架结合准静态前馈逆模型、任务空间PI控制器和动态观测器（将测量残差作为虚拟力），在高达10段离散杆的稀疏系统矩阵下实现实时控制。实验在三种不同几何形状的平面气动软执行器上验证，精度运动RMSE为1.5-2.3 mm，周期运动RMSE为5.5-12.4 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体执行器具有灵活性和柔顺交互能力，但其强非线性、分布变形和动力学不确定性使得闭环任务空间控制极具挑战。现有方法难以在保持分布式力学特性的同时实现实时控制，因此需要一种既能捕捉结构动力学又具备计算效率的控制框架。

Method: 采用绝对坐标下带完整约束的非最小坐标离散弹性杆模型（discrete elastic rod model），通过稀疏系统矩阵实现计算效率，支持多达10段离散杆的实时控制。控制架构由三部分组成：准静态前馈逆模型用于提供参考轨迹的标称输入、任务空间PI控制器用于反馈校正、以及动态观测器将测量残差融合为虚拟力以实现从稀疏传感中的全状态估计。

Result: 在三种不同几何构型的平面气动软体执行器上完成五项任务验证，包括工作空间内数字0-9书写（尖端速度3-18 mm/s）、周期运动跟踪（最高37 cm/s）、跨平台泛化、降低传感密度以及实时用户自定义参考。精度运动RMSE为1.5-2.3 mm，1-2 Hz周期运动RMSE为5.5-12.4 mm。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结构化的非最小动态模型能够实现平面软体气动执行器在自由空间中的实时、高精度、中等带宽任务空间控制，为软体机器人的基于模型实时控制提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Soft actuators enable dexterous and compliant interaction, but closed-loop task-space control remains challenging due to strong nonlinearities, distributed deformation, and uncertainty in their dynamics. This paper presents a real-time dynamic-model-based task-space feedback and estimation framework based on a non-minimal coordinate discrete elastic rod model formulated in absolute coordinates with holonomic constraints. The resulting structure preserves distributed mechanics while maintaining computational efficiency through sparse system matrices, enabling real-time control with up to 10 discretized rods. A quasi-static feedforward inverse model is combined with a task-space PI controller and a dynamic observer that fuses measurement residuals as virtual forces, enabling full-state estimation from sparse sensing. The approach is experimentally validated on three planar pneumatic soft actuators with varying geometries. Across five tasks, including drawing the digits 0-9 across the workspace (3-18 mm/s tip speed), tracking periodic motion (up to 37 cm/s), cross-platform generalization, reduced sensing conditions, and real-time user-defined references, our method achieves 1.5-2.3 mm root mean square error (RMSE) for precision motions and 5.5-12.4 mm RMSE at 1-2 Hz. Results demonstrate that structured, non-minimal dynamic models can enable real-time, high-precision, moderate-bandwidth task-space control of planar soft pneumatic actuators in free space.

</details>


### [56] [Tensegrity Continuum Robots Enable Task-Adaptive Morphologies for Cooperative Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27221)
*Mahmud Hasan Saikot,Sydney Spiegel,Sudheera Akalanka Kariyawasam,Andrew Stefka,Josh Chrisler,Jianguo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合张拉整体（tensegrity）柔性躯干与爪式连接机制的可重构模块化机器人（MRR），该机器人既能独立操作与运动，又能自我重组成链、环、分支等多种形态，实现协同操作与多模态运动，在真实场景中验证了其适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模块化可重构机器人大多依赖刚性模块，缺乏结构柔顺性，能力受限；而连续体机器人虽具有柔性骨架但无法自我重组成任务自适应的多机器人形态。作者旨在统一两者的优势，构建兼顾柔顺性与可重构性的机器人系统。

Method: 设计了一种将张拉整体结构（作为柔顺主体）与爪式连接机构相结合的模块化可重构机器人。每个单元可独立进行操控和移动，多个单元可通过爪式机构自我重组成链、环、分支等不同形态，用于协同操作和运动。

Result: 在多样任务和真实场景中验证了该机器人的能力，包括协调物体操作与搬运、多模态运动以及运动-操作（loco-manipulation）等。

Conclusion: 该工作为适应性强、多功能的机器人集体系统奠定了基础，在制造业、太空探索和搜救等领域具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Robots that can change their morphologies and behaviors for different tasks and environments hold great promise for adaptable, multifunctional systems. Modular reconfigurable robots (MRRs) can achieve such functionalities by docking and rearranging individual units, but most rely on rigid modules that lack structural compliance, resulting in limited capabilities. Continuum robots offer compliance through flexible backbones, yet they cannot self-reconfigure into task-adaptive multi-robot configurations. Here, we introduce an MRR that unifies the advantages of both architectures by combining a tensegrity-based compliant body with claw-based connection mechanisms. Each robot can manipulate and locomote independently, and multiple robots can self-reconfigure into different morphologies (e.g., chains, loops, branches) for cooperative manipulation and locomotion. We demonstrate the robots' capability across diverse tasks and environments, including coordinated object manipulation and transport, multimodal locomotion, and loco-manipulation in real-world scenarios. These results lay a foundation for adaptable and multifunctional robotic collectives, with broad potential applications in manufacturing, space exploration, and search-and-rescue operations.

</details>


### [57] [STEP: State-Aware Task Estimation and Planning with Multi-Modal LLMs for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27225)
*Maitrey Gramopadhye,Prakash Baskaran,Xiao Liu,Songpo Li,Soshi Iba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出STEP（State-aware Task Estimator and Planner），一种基于多模态大语言模型的状态感知任务规划方法。通过让MM-LLM显式估计系统状态并预测动作执行后的状态转换，解决了MM-LLM在工业人机协作场景中缺乏状态理解、容易产生幻觉动作以及自然语言生成动作计划过于高层/模糊的问题。在机器人装配任务的模拟环境中，STEP在动作可执行性上比最先进方法提升32.8%，最终状态误差降低14.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中的人机协作需要机器人理解人类意图并辅助任务规划以减少工作量。现有工作利用MM-LLM在数据稀缺场景下通过上下文学习进行长时程动作规划，但存在两大核心问题：(1) MM-LLM缺乏对系统状态的理解且不追踪状态转换，导致产生偏离目标的幻觉动作；(2) 以自然语言生成动作计划仅停留在高层描述，在动作执行时引入歧义。

Method: STEP的核心方法分为两部分：(1) 状态估计与转换预测——提示MM-LLM显式估计当前系统状态，并预测每个已执行动作引发的状态转换，通过前瞻未来状态确保规划的收敛性；(2) 动作执行增强——在预测动作的同时提供执行所需额外辅助参数，减少自然语言计划带来的执行歧义。

Result: 在模拟环境中的机器人装配任务上，STEP相比最先进方法在动作可执行性上提升32.8%，在最终状态误差上降低14.8%。

Conclusion: STEP通过引入显式的状态估计与状态转换预测机制，有效缓解了MM-LLM在任务规划中的幻觉问题，并提高了动作计划的可执行性与目标达成精度，验证了状态感知对于基于大语言模型的机器人任务规划的重要性。

Abstract: Effective human-robot collaboration in industrial settings requires robots to understand human intentions and assist with task planning, reducing workload. Recent works have explored the use of Multi-modal Large Language Models (MM-LLMs) for task planning in such data-scarce scenarios, leveraging in-context learning to interpret user actions and generate long-horizon action plans in natural language. However, MM-LLMs inherently lack an understanding of system states and do not track state transitions, often leading to hallucinated actions that deviate from the intended goal. Additionally, generating action plans in natural language tends to limit the generated plans to a high level, introducing ambiguity in action execution. To address these limitations, we propose the State-aware Task Estimator and Planner (STEP), which prompts a MM-LLM to explicitly estimate the state of the system and predict the state transitions resulting from executed actions. By forecasting future states alongside actions, STEP ensures task-convergent planning while also providing additional assistance parameters necessary for executing the predicted actions. We evaluate STEP in a simulated environment using a robot assembly task. Our approach outperforms the state-of-the-art by 32.8% in action executability and 14.8% in final-state error.

</details>


### [58] [Embodied Scene Rearrangement Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27371)
*Canzhi Chen,Zan Wang,Siqi Zhu,Qi Wu,Yixuan Li,Wei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出新型任务ESRP（具身场景重排规划），要求具身智能体仅依靠第一人称观察和俯视目标布局在3D场景中重排家具使其与目标配置一致。由于禁止全局状态访问并存在物体互相遮挡，与全局目标布局对齐局部观察具有挑战。作者基于OmniGibson构建了包含5400+场景对和8200+物体的ESRP-Bench基准，定义了三级多层级指标，并提供四个基线方法（层次化TAMP、VLM方法及IL和RL两种学习方法）。实验表明现有方法难以高效完成任务，凸显ESRP对具身智能体的长期规划能力是富有挑战的前沿任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排任务通常假设可访问全局状态，与实际机器人部署场景不符。真实环境中智能体只能获得局部第一人称观察，且物体间存在相互遮挡，这使得将局部观察与全局目标布局对齐进行长期规划极具挑战，然而这一实用性问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 作者定义了ESRP新任务，限定智能体只能使用第一人称观察和俯视目标布局。基于OmniGibson构建ESRP-Bench基准（5400+场景对、8200+物体），设计三级多层级评估指标，并提供四个基线：层次化任务与运动规划（TAMP）、基于视觉语言模型的方法、模仿学习（IL）和强化学习（RL）。

Result: 实验结果表明现有四类基线方法在ESRP任务上均难以高效完成，尤其是在长时程规划、局部-全局观察对齐和遮挡处理方面表现不足，揭示了该任务对现有方法的挑战性。

Conclusion: ESRP作为具身场景理解与长期规划的结合点，是一个具有挑战性的前沿方向，该工作为推动智能体在真实世界场景部署提供了基础性支撑。

Abstract: This paper introduces Embodied Scene Rearrangement Planning (ESRP), a novel task requiring embodied agents to rearrange furniture in 3D scenes to match a target configuration using only egocentric observations and a top-down target layout. Unlike prior rearrangement tasks, ESRP precludes global state access and introduces mutual object occlusions, reflecting the practical constraints of real-world robotic deployment. These factors make aligning partial egocentric observations with the global target layout particularly challenging for long-horizon planning. To facilitate research, we present ESRP-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark built on OmniGibson featuring over 5,400 scene pairs and 8,200 objects. We define three multi-level metrics to evaluate rearrangement quality and provide four baselines: a hierarchical task-and-motion planning method, a vision-language-model-based method, and two learning-based approaches (IL and RL). Experimental results demonstrate that current methods struggle to complete the task efficiently, highlighting ESRP as a challenging frontier for embodied agents in scene understanding and long-horizon task planning. This work serves as a stepping stone toward deploying intelligent agents in real-world scenarios. Project page: https://pie-lab.cn/ESRP/.

</details>


### [59] [FlashVLA: Streaming Action Decoding for Fast and Asynchronous VLA Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27384)
*Zekai Li,Jiaming Tang,Zhijian Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlashVLA是一种流式动作解码框架，通过维护多噪声级别的流式动作缓冲区和块级因果注意力，在每个推理步产生一个可执行的动作块，从而在保持任务性能的同时实现≥30Hz的控制频率和流畅的异步执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在真实世界机器人操作中面临高推理延迟和不稳定的异步执行两大瓶颈，尤其是基于流匹配的VLA模型需要多次迭代解码动作。现有高效推理方法仅关注控制频率，异步方法仅减少空闲时间，缺乏同时实现低延迟推理和精确、时间一致的异步执行的统一方案。

Method: FlashVLA包含两个核心设计：(1) 流式动作缓冲区，包含多个处于不同噪声水平的动作块；(2) 块级因果注意力解码机制。每个推理步骤解码并产生一个可执行的动作块，其块级自回归公式隐式保持动作连续性，无需额外的未来状态条件。

Result: 在广泛的仿真和真实世界实验中，FlashVLA显著提升了推理速度，同时保持强任务性能，在单GPU上可实现≥30Hz的控制频率，并在真实部署中实现平滑的异步推理。

Conclusion: FlashVLA通过统一的流式动作解码框架，同时解决了VLA模型低延迟推理和稳定异步执行两个挑战，为真实世界机器人操作中VLA模型的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly promising for robotic manipulation, yet their real-world deployment remains bottlenecked by high inference latency and unstable asynchronous execution. This challenge is particularly pronounced in flow-matching-based VLA models, where action decoding requires multiple iterative steps conditioned on the VLM context. While efficient inference methods improve control frequency and asynchronous methods reduce execution idle time, existing approaches often fail to jointly achieve low-latency inference and accurate, temporally consistent asynchronous execution. We introduce \textbf{FlashVLA}, a streaming action decoding framework that addresses both challenges in a unified formulation. FlashVLA maintains a streaming action buffer with multiple chunks at different noise levels and decodes them using chunk-wise causal attention. This design allows FlashVLA to produce one executable action chunk per inference step. Moreover, its chunk-wise autoregressive formulation implicitly preserves action continuity, enabling smooth asynchronous execution without extra future-state conditioning. Across extensive simulated and real-world experiments, FlashVLA substantially improves inference speed while maintaining strong task performance. It can achieve $\geq$30\,Hz control frequency on a single GPU with smooth asynchronous inference in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [60] [CLAP: Cross-Embodiment Video World Models are Zero-Shot Physical Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27406)
*Kechen Liu,Ola Shorinwa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLAP是一个跨具身（cross-embodiment）动作条件视频生成框架，通过端效应器位姿、语言指令和潜在动作三种动作表示实现异质动作空间的统一，并采用基于课程学习的训练策略，先在无标签视频上学习通用物理先验，再微调到具体动作空间，实现零样本部署。在DROID等挑战性环境上达到或超越单具身视频模型的性能，并可通过少样本适应进一步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的动作条件视频模型通常局限于单个机器人具身（single embodiment），无法利用海量异质视频数据中蕴含的丰富通用物理学习信号。由于不同机器人平台的动作表示差异巨大，且人类视频中通常缺乏动作标注，跨具身学习面临根本性挑战。

Method: CLAP包含三个核心贡献：(1) 将异质动作空间统一为三种表示——端效应器位姿（end-effector poses）、语言指令（language instructions）和潜在动作（latent actions）；(2) 提出基于课程学习的跨具身训练范式，先在无标签视频数据上利用潜在动作学习基础物理先验，再将这些先验适配到端效应器动作空间，实现真实世界任务的零样本部署；(3) 支持通过少样本适应进一步增强性能，建立训练单具身视频世界模型的新范式。

Result: CLAP在DROID等挑战性环境中达到或超越单具身视频模型的性能；通过少样本适应可获得进一步的性能增益；提供了范围最全面的动作条件视频世界模型套件，覆盖多种动作条件空间（端效应器、语言、潜在）和多种机器人形态（跨具身、DROID、Bridge、双臂YAM机器人、G1人形机器人）。

Conclusion: CLAP证明了跨具身动作条件视频生成是可行且有效的，弥合了动作表示差异与标注缺失的双重挑战，为利用互联网规模异质视频数据训练具备通用物理知识的视频世界模型开辟了新路径。所有代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: State-of-the-art action-conditioned video models are typically restricted to a single robot embodiment, preventing them from leveraging the vast corpus of heterogeneous video data that contains rich signals for learning generalizable physics. To bridge this gap, we introduce CLAP, a framework for cross-embodiment action-conditioned video generation capable of being trained on diverse, internet-scale videos across human and robotic agents. CLAP is grounded in the insight that universal physical laws govern spatiotemporal dynamics regardless of the actor. However, cross-embodiment learning is non-trivial because action representations vary sharply across robot platforms and are typically absent in human videos. CLAP addresses this fundamental challenge through the following core contributions. First, CLAP reconciles disparate action spaces using end-effector poses, language instructions, and latent actions. Second, to resolve their individual limitations, CLAP introduces a curriculum-based cross-embodiment learning recipe that first learns foundational physical priors across unlabeled video data using latent actions and subsequently grounds them in end-effector action spaces for zero-shot deployment to real-world tasks. Crucially, CLAP approaches or surpasses state-of-the-art single-embodiment video models in challenging environments like DROID. These performance advantages compound via few-shot adaptation to establish a novel paradigm for training single-embodiment video world models. Ultimately, CLAP delivers the most comprehensive suite of action-conditioned video world models to date - spanning diverse action-conditioning spaces (end-effector, language, and latent) and robot morphologies (including cross-embodiment, DROID, Bridge, bimanual YAM robots, and G1 humanoids). We open-source all code and models. Project Website at https://omni-clap.github.io .

</details>

### [61] [Efficient binned profile likelihood minimization for precision measurements with RABBIT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26376)
*David Walter,Josh Bendavid,Kenneth Long*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Rabbit（Rapid Automatic Bin-Based Inference Tool），一个基于TensorFlow 2的Python框架，利用可微编程、自动微分和JIT编译技术，在CPU和GPU上对桶状剖面最大似然拟合进行快速稳健的优化，实现近C++执行速度，并在基准测试中优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC上的精密测量日益依赖拥有数千个桶和大量束参量的桶状剖面最大似然拟合，而高亮度LHC将进一步提高这些数值。快速且稳健地最小化此类似然函数对及时的分析开发和准确的统计推断至关重要，而现有工具在挑战性场景下收敛缓慢或无法收敛。

Method: Rabbit框架利用TensorFlow 2中的可微编程，通过自动微分提供精确梯度和Hessian-向量积，供运行在Krylov子空间中的信赖域最小化器使用；同时利用即时编译（JIT）实现近C++的执行速度。框架支持灵活的统计模型与解析处理，提供对称化选项来建立高斯近似和具有确定性解的线性化似然公式，并专注于通过模型的可微变换来测量物理可观测量，包括非折叠微分截面。

Result: 在合成模型上的基准测试表明，Rabbit在桶数量和参数数量上展现出优异的扩展性，在具有挑战性的场景中性能优于现有工具，后者在合理时间内无法收敛。相比现有方法，Rabbit在CPU和GPU上均能高效运行。

Abstract: Precision measurements at the LHC increasingly rely on binned profile maximum likelihood fits with thousands of bins and nuisance parameters, and the High-Luminosity LHC will push these numbers further. Fast and robust minimization of such likelihoods is crucial for timely analysis development and accurate inference. We present Rabbit (Rapid Automatic Bin-Based Inference Tool), a Python framework that exploits differentiable programming in TensorFlow 2 to perform this task on CPUs and GPUs. Automatic differentiation provides exact gradients and Hessian-vector products for a trust-region minimizer operating in Krylov subspaces, and just-intime compilation yields near-C++ execution speed. Rabbit implements flexible statistical models with analytic treatments where possible, supports symmetrization options that establish Gaussian approximations and a linearized likelihood formulation with deterministic solutions, and focuses on measuring physical observables through differentiable transformations of the model, including unfolded differential cross sections. Benchmarks on synthetic models demonstrate excellent scaling with the number of bins and parameters, outperforming established tools in challenging regimes where these fail to converge within reasonable time.

Abstract: A search for new high-mass phenomena in events with a high-transverse-momentum photon and a jet is performed using data collected by the ATLAS experiment from proton-proton collisions at the LHC, with a centre-of-mass energy of 13 TeV. For $f=1$, masses up to 6.0 TeV ($q_{\mathrm{L}}^{*}$), 3.5 TeV ($c^{*}$), and 2.6 TeV ($b^{*}$) are excluded. These results provide the first direct constraints on $c^{*}$ production in the $γ$$-$ jet final state at the LHC. Additionally, the analysis excludes quantum black hole mass thresholds up to 5.3 (7.5) TeV in the Randall-Sundrum (Arkani-Hamed-Dimopoulos-Dvali) models at a 95% confidence level, further constraining these theoretical frameworks. Constraints on the visible cross-section are also obtained using generic Gaussian-shaped resonance production.

Abstract: Optical photon simulation is among the most computationally demanding tasks in complex and large detector geometries. In the Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE), the scale of the far-detector modules makes photon-by-photon transport with \texttt{GEANT4} prohibitively expensive on CPUs. We present the first implementation and performance evaluation of GPU-accelerated optical photon simulation at the 10~kt scale, based on \texttt{Opticks} and applied to the DUNE far-detector horizontal-drift (FD-HD) geometry. On the full FD-HD geometry, \texttt{Opticks} propagates the same photons as \texttt{GEANT4} with a speedup of $313 \pm 3$ over single-threaded and $83 \pm 1$ over four-thread \texttt{GEANT4}, and is validated against the reference \texttt{GEANT4} simulation across all metrics considered. We further integrate \texttt{Opticks} into the \texttt{LArSoft}-based DUNE software stack. Photons simulated on the GPU with \texttt{GEANT4}-equivalent physics retain full Monte Carlo fidelity at a computational cost that makes high-statistics optical studies and the generation of labeled datasets for machine learning feasible at the kiloton scale.

</details>

Method: Reservoir由一个带有真实噪声和报告伪影的随机模拟器生成，干预措施具有可配置的时间、依从性和年龄依赖效力。当前版本包含50万条疫情轨迹，覆盖10亿个模拟天，涵盖多样化的病原体特征、人口结构和干预制度。

Result: 每条模拟轨迹都携带完整的真实标签，包括无法在真实疫情中直接测量的量，如真实感染计数、时变有效再生数和反事实干预效果。该数据集能够支撑反事实实验、监测设计研究和大规模疫情模型训练。

Conclusion: Reservoir为传染病流行病学的AI方法研究提供了规模化、带完整标注的模拟数据基础，解决了真实世界数据规模不足的瓶颈，使流行病学建模能在真实数据无法提供的规模上进行训练和评估。

Abstract: Large-scale, standardized datasets have driven many advances in AI-based scientific modeling, from protein structure prediction to natural language processing. Infectious disease epidemiology is increasingly adopting AI methods for forecasting, surveillance, and outbreak analytics, but the time-series data available to train them remains orders of magnitude smaller than the corpora behind the advances seen in other fields. Because the scope of real-world epidemiological data cannot practically reach the scale needed to train truly large-scale AI methods, simulated data provides a possible alternative. Here we introduce Reservoir, a large open simulator and dataset of realistic epidemic simulations in which every trajectory carries complete ground-truth labels, including quantities that cannot be measured directly in a real outbreak, such as true infection counts, time-varying reproduction numbers, and counterfactual intervention effects. Reservoir is generated by a stochastic simulator with realistic noise and reporting artifacts, together with interventions with configurable timing, compliance, and age-dependent efficacy. The current release contains 500,000 outbreak trajectories spanning one billion simulated days across diverse pathogen characteristics, population structures, and intervention regimes. Reservoir enables counterfactual experiments, surveillance-design studies, and training of epidemic models at a scale real-world datasets cannot provide.

</details>

Result: 以德克萨斯州3,752家医院和疗养院为案例，采用气候信息热带气旋洪水情景评估。结果显示：仅最小化经济损失会系统性将更少保护分配给社会脆弱区域的设施；沿帕累托前沿适度移动能在有限额外预期成本下显著降低中断影响。精确分解求解了所有测试实例（含大规模压力测试），拉格朗日方法提供更快的高质量解。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效权衡医疗设施韧性投资的经济效率与社会公平，证明了精确分解在内存受限大规模问题中的可行性，以及拉格朗日方法在求解效率上的优势。

Abstract: Flooding can damage healthcare facilities, interrupt local care capacity, and trigger costly patient evacuations. Addressing these risks requires long-term resilience investments under uncertainty. We develop a bi-objective two-stage stochastic optimization model that jointly determines permanent facility hardening and scenario-dependent evacuation decisions. The first objective minimizes expected evacuation, physical damage, and business-interruption costs. The second minimizes a service-disruption impact index that combines the duration and scale of service loss with a place-based social-vulnerability weight. We develop an exact Benders decomposition and a scalable Lagrangian-dual method with tailored primal recovery, embedding both within an adaptive procedure for constructing informative Pareto frontiers. A case study of 3,752 hospitals and nursing homes in Texas evaluates the framework under climate-informed tropical-cyclone flood scenarios. The results show that minimizing economic losses alone can systematically allocate less protection to facilities located in socially vulnerable areas, while moderate movement along the Pareto frontier can substantially reduce disruption impacts at limited additional expected cost. The exact decomposition solves all tested instances, including larger stress tests for which the extensive formulation becomes memory-limited, while the Lagrangian method provides substantially faster high-quality solutions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于约束优化的必要最优性条件大多基于经典的非方向性方法（如度量次正则性、伪正规性等），而方向性分析能够提供更精细的基本约束条件刻画，特别是在退化点处经典条件可能失效。作者旨在将有限维方向性最优性理论推广到更一般的Asplund空间，弥补无穷维框架下方向性分析工具的缺失。

Method: 论文采用变分分析的方法，核心步骤包括：(1) 在Asplund空间中，利用方向性度量次正则性假设，通过极限次微分（limiting subdifferential）推导方向性必要最优性条件；(2) 引入方向性度量次正则性的充分条件，并建立其与方向性伪正规性（directional pseudo-normality）和准正规性（quasi-normality）之间的蕴含关系；(3) 针对具有联合约束（joint constraints）的系统，提出联合判据以验证方向性度量次正则性，并据此获得相应的必要最优性条件。

Result: 论文在Asplund空间中获得了一组方向性必要最优性条件，证明了方向性伪正规性和准正规性可作为方向性度量次正则性的充分条件，并提出了适用于联合约束系统的联合度量次正则性判据。所得定理推广了已有有限维方向性构造，同时在有限维情形下也给出了新的结论。

Conclusion: 该论文发展了Asplund空间中约束优化的方向性一阶最优性理论，系统地建立了方向性度量次正则性与伪正规性、准正规性之间的联系，并通过联合约束判据拓展了应用范围。该工作为无穷维约束优化的方向性分析提供了统一框架，兼具理论推广性与有限维新结果。

Abstract: This paper develops directional necessary optimality conditions for constrained optimization problems in Asplund spaces. Under directional metric subregularity, we first derive a directional optimality condition in terms of limiting subdifferentials. We then introduce sufficient conditions for directional metric subregularity and establish their relationships with directional pseudo-normality and quasi-normality. For systems with joint constraints, we further propose a joint criterion for directional metric subregularity and use it to obtain necessary optimality conditions. The results not only extend several finite-dimensional directional constructions to an Asplund-space setting, but also yield conclusions that are new even in finite dimensions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维空间中单位直径构型的极值问题（如Blaschke–Lebesgue面积问题）是经典几何优化难题，其非凸约束结构使直接求解极为困难。作者希望通过分析极值构型的结构性质（几何核心、可分离泛函），建立理论下界，并借助大规模数值实验揭示与正四面体包含关系的结构性猜想。

Result: 理论方面：获得加性凹泛函由正四面体达到的尖锐下界；建立了Blaschke–Lebesgue面积问题的新上确界公式。数值方面：对全部10,644个极值构型逐一验证，每个选定图的经验端点均包含正四面体；虽未对任何固定图认证全局最大值，但结果支持"四面体包含"与Blaschke–Lebesgue问题之间的结构性猜想。

Abstract: We study optimization problems for separable functionals of the Euclidean or spherical lengths of dual edge pairs in finite extremal unit-diameter configurations in three dimensions. For a fixed diameter graph, these problems lead to nonconvex constrained optimization of the vertex coordinates. We first prove that a convergent sequence of extremal configurations retains an extremal geometric core after coincident points are merged and vertices incident to at most one diameter are removed. A spherical Crofton argument then gives a sharp lower bound for additive concave functionals, attained by the regular tetrahedron. We also analyze the effect of inserting or deleting dangling vertices. For the sum of products of spherical dual-edge lengths, we obtain an exact supremal reformulation of the three-dimensional Blaschke--Lebesgue area problem. The numerical study uses all 10,644 available extremal configurations with at most 16 vertices. We combine direct evaluation with gradient-based local optimization on each fixed graph and reconstruct the intrinsic diameter graph and its dual pairs after vertex collisions. For every supplied graph, the selected verified endpoint contains a regular tetrahedron. These computations do not certify the global maximum for any fixed graph, but they motivate structural conjectures connecting tetrahedral containment with the Blaschke--Lebesgue problem.

</details>

Method: 1. 提出流基混合整数规划（MIP）模型，联合优化公交线路规划、发车频率以及按需服务覆盖的首末公里接驳，受固定运营预算约束。2. 设计新颖的列生成启发式方案，包含定制化的定价子问题，以保证城市规模网络下计算的可行性。

Result: 在波士顿和芝加哥网络上的实验表明：相较公交专用基准，波士顿和芝加哥乘客量分别最高提升20.99%和10.63%；相较按需专用基准，分别最高提升93.58%和149.84%；相较多模式解耦基准，分别提升5.42%和5.80%。

Applied to networks and demand in Boston and Chicago, the framework yields operationally feasible designs that substantially increase demand served. Relative to transit-only and on-demand-only baselines, ridership increases by up to 20.99% and 93.58% in Boston, and by up to 10.63% and 149.84% in Chicago. Compared to a multi-modal benchmark, our approach improves ridership by 5.42% and 5.80% in Boston and Chicago, respectively. These results demonstrate that (i) joint co-design of transit routes, frequencies, and on-demand legs within a unified optimization framework yields substantially greater ridership than single-mode or decoupled approaches under equivalent budget constraints; and (ii) the proposed formulation and column generation pricing scheme admit tractable, operationally feasible, high-performing solutions relative to tested baselines.

Conclusion: 该工作突破了传统李雅普诺夫函数构造的瓶颈，提供了一种无需显式构造李雅普诺夫函数、仅依赖轨迹数据的高效稳定性认证框架，并阐明了验证精度与计算成本之间的本质折中。

Abstract: Lyapunov's direct method is a cornerstone of stability and control, but it hinges on finding a Lyapunov function, a task demanding ingenuity or computation. We relax this by replacing invariance with recurrence: a set is recurrent if every trajectory starting in it returns within a finite time. This yields the notion of a Recurrent Lyapunov Function (RLF), whose sub-level sets need only be recurrent. We also give norm-based converse theorems: under the corresponding stability conditions, any norm is an RLF for their practical versions. We then develop GPU-based algorithms that certify (practical) stability from trajectory data alone, without a Lyapunov function. Certifying stability up to an $\varepsilon$-neighborhood needs only $O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ trajectory evaluations, with constants growing as the certified decay rate nears the true one, exposing an intrinsic performance-cost trade-off.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce finite-dimensional approximations to the space of convex bodies based on polytopes in vertex representation. We study the geometry of the parameter cone, redundancies in its defining inequalities, and natural projections of convex bodies onto the resulting polytope spaces. Finally, we use these approximation properties to establish the convergence of finite-dimensional approximations of constrained global optimization problems in the space of convex bodies.

We first establish convergence of the inner iterations, derive an error bound for the corresponding inner problem. Then we prove that the outer sequence generated by BPDHG approaches the optimal solution set of the LP problem we considered. Furthermore, we integrate the barrier technique into the {Primal Dual Linear Programming} (PDLP) framework to develop the corresponding Barrier PDLP (BPDLP) method. Numerical experiments show that the barrier modification can alleviate prolonged plateaus in the KKT residual on selected instances. We also investigate an empirical instance-dependent indicator for identifying LP problems on which BPDLP is more likely to outperform PDLP.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望解决Diakonikolas提出的关于一般ℓ_p几何中的参数无关一阶优化扩展问题。在严格计数的局部值-梯度模型中，仅依赖LR/ε的有限复杂度界无法在缺少非退化局部尺度观测时成立（一维构造可保持LR/ε=4而击败任意有限的查询预算），因此需要设计特定初始化条件下的参数无关方法。

Method: 本文采用确定性已知参数的复杂度上界分析方法，首先在已知参数情形下，对固定有限p>2建立维度无关的确定性上界指数p/(p+2)，匹配已发表的多项式下界。然后设计"有限局部例程"（finite local routine），复用可观测的尺度-半径过程，使得该指数在不知道L和R的情况下得以保持。分析涵盖三种情况：1<p<2时复杂度为O_p(\bar{K}^{1/2})，p=2时为O(\bar{K}^{1/2})，p>2时为O_p(\bar{K}^{p/(p+2)})，其中\bar{K}=max{1,LR/ε}。

Result: 作者建立了在非退化割线初始化条件下，值-梯度对（pair-oracle）模型的参数无关复杂度上界。初始化后的复杂度分别为：1<p<2时O_p(\bar{K}^{1/2})，p=2时O(\bar{K}^{1/2})，p>2时O_p(\bar{K}^{p/(p+2)})，并在所有情况下加上加性校准代价O_p(log(e+L/M_0))。对于p>2，得到的已知参数确定性上界指数p/(p+2)匹配了已有下界。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Diakonikolas关于一般ℓ_p范数的参数无关一阶优化扩展问题（对每个固定的1<p<∞）。结果揭示了一个重要现象：除非退化的局部尺度观测作为初始化条件，否则没有任何仅依赖LR/ε的有限复杂度界能够成立。在非退化割线初始化下，参数无关方法的复杂度指数与已知参数情形完全匹配，说明隐藏参数不会造成额外的复杂度代价（除加性的对数量级校准成本外）。

Abstract: We study the first-order oracle complexity of finding a queried point with small gradient in $\ell_p$ geometry, with particular attention to the information needed to adapt the unknown smoothness and distance scales. In the strict counted local value--gradient model, no finite complexity bound can depend only on $LR/\eps$ without a nondegenerate local scale observation: a one-dimensional construction keeps $LR/\eps=4$ while defeating every prescribed finite query budget.

Under a nondegenerate secant initialization, the method knows neither the smoothness constant $L$, the initial solution distance $R$, nor $f^*$, and returns a queried point $\widehat x$ with $\|\nabla f(\widehat x)\|_q\le\eps$. For fixed finite $p>2$, we first establish the dimension-free deterministic known-parameter upper exponent $p/(p+2)$ in $K=LR/\eps$, matching the published lower polynomial exponent under its horizon and dimension qualifications. The finite local routine fits the same observable scale--radius procedure, so this exponent is preserved without knowing $L$ or $R$. Writing $\Kbar=\max\{1,LR/\eps\}$, the post-initialization pair-oracle complexity is $O_p(\Kbar^{1/2})$ for $1<p<2$, $O(\Kbar^{1/2})$ for $p=2$, and $O_p(\Kbar^{p/(p+2)})$ for $p>2$, together with the additive calibration cost $O_p(\log(e+L/M_0))$ in every regime.

</details>

Conclusion: 给出了提升传播的精确假设条件，证明了该方法对N≥7的所有视界成立，同时明确指出并非所有秩一松弛PEP都允许这样的种子结构。

Abstract: We determine the exact worst-case value, at every horizon $N\geq7$, of the smallest queried gradient norm generated by Nesterov's fast gradient method on smooth convex functions. Let $t_0=1$ and $t_{k+1}=(1+\sqrt{1+4t_k^2})/2$, and let $x_0,\ldots,x_N$ denote the points at which the method evaluates gradients. For every such $N$ and every dimension $d\geq N-4$, we prove \[
  \sup_{\substack{f\in\F_{0,L}(\R^d),\ x_\star\in\arg\min f
  \norm{x_0-x_\star}\leq R}}
  \min_{0\leq k\leq N}\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}^2
  =\frac{L^2R^2}{\sum_{k=0}^N t_k^2}. \] The relaxed-PEP upper bound is due to Kim and Fessler, who also reported tight numerical solutions of the exact-interpolation PEP at selected horizons. What remained missing was an analytic matching family valid uniformly over the horizon. For every $N\geq7$, we construct such a family using an FGM-specific spherical polytope $K_N$ and the standard projection-envelope function \[
  f_N(x)=\max_{g\in K_N}\left\{\ip{x}{g}-\frac12\norm{g}^2\right\},
  \qquad \nabla f_N(x)=\Proj_{K_N}(x). \] Every queried gradient has the same norm, and the vertices of $K_N$ are generated from a three-dimensional seed by a one-dimensional spherical cone lift. The lift preserves all projection inequalities and raises the adversary dimension by one at each horizon. The projection/Moreau-envelope template itself is classical; the new ingredients are the FGM-specific algebraic seed, the proof that it attains the relaxed bound, and the common-latitude lift that propagates this exactness to every $N\geq7$. We state precise hypotheses for that propagation and do not claim that every rank-one relaxed PEP admits such a seed.

</details>

*Siyu Pan,Jiajin Li*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study the deterministic first-order oracle complexity of finding stationary points of the value function in smooth nonconvex-Polyak-Łojasiewicz (NC-PŁ) minimax optimization. Abstract: We classify the facial exposedness of completely positive cones over symmetric cones in terms of the rank of the associated Euclidean Jordan algebras. For the underdetermined case, we show that our methods achieve local superlinear convergence to the optimal solution. For the overdetermined case, we show that our methods achieve local condition-number-free convergence to the stationary point of the nonlinear least-square formulation of the problem. In contrast, existing quasi-Newton methods mostly focus on square systems and additionally require the initial Jacobian estimate to be sufficiently close to the exact one.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Expansion planning models usually fix storage duration before optimization, setting how much storage power to build but not for how long it can discharge. It is a rolling-horizon, two-stage stochastic linear program, each five-year stage is a full year at hourly resolution under ten coherent inflow, wind, and solar scenarios. We apply it to the Brazilian Interconnected System over 2030-2050, where demand roughly doubles to 1,716 TWh/year and variable renewable energy (VRE) supplies most new capacity. Two cases are compared: 36 pumped-storage hydropower (PSH) candidates over four subsystems and nine durations (4-144 h) alongside 4-h battery energy storage systems (BESS), and BESS alone.
  With PSH available, the model builds 31.2 GW / 755 GWh of PSH and no BESS, dominated by 12-h capacity with 4.9 GW of 72-h units. Without PSH it builds 38 GW / 152 GWh of BESS and 7.8 GW more gas-fired capacity, mostly open-cycle peakers. PSH lowers 2050 annualized system cost by US$ 5.0 billion/year (6.8%), operating cost by 23.5%, thermal generation by 34 TWh/year, and long-run marginal cost by 20%; 2035 VRE curtailment falls from 8.4% to 3.2%. The magnitudes are specific to Brazil, but the underlying mechanism is general: limiting storage candidates to a single duration understates the system's optimal energy-storage requirement and overstates its residual need for thermal capacity.

Motivation: 随着海洋科学界越来越依赖MAV（海洋自主航行器）采集关键环境数据以理解全球海洋系统，大规模自主船队的运营中，人工路径规划和调度变得越来越指数级复杂且耗时。现有方法无法高效处理大规模自主车队的优化调度问题，亟需一种自动化的优化框架来应对这一挑战。

Method: 提出一个混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型来自动化优化MAV部署调度。模型将严格的运营约束（包括电池容量和数据采集时间窗口）纳入优化，以最大化总数据采集量、最小化部署车辆数量和能耗为多目标。关键创新在于整合常规船舶航行计划，使MAV能够在任务中途通过船舶完成电池更换或加速航点间中转。

Result: 计算实验表明该模型高度可扩展：能够再数秒内求解数十辆MAV的路径规划问题，并在几分钟内扩展至数百辆MAV的规模。此外，该框架还可作为鲁棒的仿真工具，用于评估"假设"场景和分析不同参数对部署策略的影响，并生成一套可视化结果以增强可解释性。

Conclusion: 该MILP框架不仅解决了大规模MAV船队的运营调度优化问题，还提供了一个具备仿真能力和可视化支持的战略决策工具，能够帮助海洋科学界和利益相关者更高效、更可解释地规划和部署自主航行器采集海洋数据。

Abstract: The marine science community increasingly relies on Marine Autonomous Vehicles (MAVs) to collect the critical environmental data required to understand global ocean systems. However, as these operations scale, manually routing and planning large autonomous fleets becomes exponentially complex and time-consuming. To address this, we propose a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) model designed to automate and optimise MAV deployment schedules. The model accounts for strict operational constraints, including battery capacities and time windows for data collection, while aiming to maximise total data collection and minimise both the number of deployed vehicles and their energy consumption. A key novelty of this framework is integrating conventional ship itineraries, allowing MAVs to support vessels with mid-mission battery swapping or accelerated transit between waypoints. Computational experiments demonstrate that the model is highly scalable, solving routing problems for fleets of dozens of MAVs in seconds, and scaling to hundreds of vehicles in only a few minutes. Beyond operational scheduling, the framework serves as a robust simulation tool for evaluating 'what-if' scenarios and analysing the impact of varying parameters on deployment strategies. Finally, the solution generates a suite of visualisations designed to enhance explainability and support strategic decision-making for stakeholders.

</details>


### [84] [When MILP Beats QP: Piecewise-Linear Reformulations of Sequentially Coupled Bilinear Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27312)
*Quentin Ploussard,Maris Usis,Oluwabunmi Iwakin,Matija Pavičević*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对包含双线性项的优化问题，提出基于连续分段线性（CPWL）逼近的多种混合整数线性规划（MILP）公式，并系统比较其与二次规划（QP）求解器的计算性能。作者利用单位双线性函数的对称性构造高精度CPWL逼近，解析推导最大和平均逼近误差，提出"Triangle"、"Square"和"DC"三种MILP公式，并定义了"顺序耦合双线性规划"（SCBP）问题类别。数值实验表明，紧凑的"Square"MILP公式在长序列SCBP实例上性能优越，使开源MILP求解器可超越QP求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双线性项在数学建模中普遍存在，导致非凸二次规划（QP）难以全局最优求解。虽然连续分段线性（CPWL）逼近可将非线性问题转化为混合整数线性规划（MILP），但域划分的几何构造方式严重制约求解效率，亟需更高效的MILP公式。

Method: 本文首先利用单位双线性函数的对称性构造任意精度的CPWL逼近；其次解析推导CPWL逼近的精确最大和平均逼近误差；再次构建并比较三种MILP公式（"Triangle"、"Square"和差分凸"DC"公式）；随后定义顺序耦合双线性规划（SCBP）模型类别；最后通过大规模数值实验评估各公式的计算性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的紧凑"Square"MILP公式在长序列SCBP实例上具有显著的计算优势，成熟的开放源码MILP求解器在求解这些实例时能够有效超越传统QP求解器。

Conclusion: 通过利用双线性函数的对称性并设计紧凑的MILP公式，可以有效提升非凸二次规划问题的全局求解效率，使MILP方法成为QP求解在长序列问题上更有竞争力的替代方案。

Abstract: The presence of bilinear terms in mathematical modeling generally yields nonconvex quadratic programs (QPs) that remain computationally challenging to solve to global optimality. While continuous piecewise linear (CPWL) approximations can reformulate these nonlinearities into mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs), the geometric construction of the domain partition heavily dictates the resulting solver efficiency. In this paper, we present efficient MILP formulations for approximating bilinear terms and rigorously evaluate their computational merits against direct QP solvers. First, we introduce CPWL approximations with arbitrary high degrees of accuracy that explicitly account for and exploit the inherent symmetries of the unit bilinear function. Second, we analytically establish the exact maximum and average approximation errors of the CPWL approximations. Third, we construct and compare three distinct MILP formulations (a ``Triangle'', a ``Square'', and a difference-of-convex ``DC'' formulation) of the CPWL functions. Fourth, we formalize a highly relevant class of optimization models, Sequentially Coupled Bilinear Programs (SCBP), where variables represent system states and state changes. Finally, through extensive numerical experiments, we demonstrate that our compact ``Square'' MILP formulation achieves superior computational performance on SCBP instances with long sequence horizons, allowing mature open-source MILP solvers to effectively outperform QP solvers.

</details>


### [85] [UGM: A Unified Framework and New Perspectives for Accelerated Gradient Methods in Smooth and Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27368)
*Danqing Zhou,Shiqian Ma,Junfeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出统一加速梯度方法框架UGM，涵盖多种加速和常规梯度方法；将其迭代更新解释为重球法和朴素梯度下降的混合，并利用Lyapunov函数建立统一收敛分析，据此开发增强型加速梯度算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的加速梯度方法种类繁多（如Nesterov加速法、重球法等），各自的设计原理和收敛分析相对独立，缺乏统一的框架来理解其本质联系与迭代更新的内在机制。作者希望揭示经典加速方法的共同本质，从而指导新算法的设计。

Method: 提出统一框架UGM，证明其迭代更新可分解为重球法动力学与朴素梯度下降项的混合组合。利用Lyapunov函数建立统一收敛性分析框架，并基于梯度信息与迭代变量之间的内积关系指导更新策略的优化，开发出增强型加速梯度算法族。

Result: 在无约束二次优化和逻辑回归上的大量数值实验表明，所提算法在典型结构条件下相比现有基线方法取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: 统一框架揭示了经典加速梯度方法的本质——在快速但欠稳定的重球动力学中融入保守的梯度下降步骤，以平衡加速与稳定性；该理解可用于开发性能更优的增强算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a unified framework for accelerated gradient methods, dubbed UGM, which subsumes a wide range of accelerated and conventional gradient-type methods designed for minimizing $L$-smooth and $μ$-strongly convex functions. We demonstrate that the iteration update of the proposed framework can be intrinsically interpreted as a hybrid combination of the heavy-ball method and vanilla gradient descent. This interpretation reveals that classical accelerated gradient methods essentially integrate a conservative gradient descent step into the fast yet unstable heavy-ball dynamics, which achieves a favorable trade-off between acceleration and stability. We further establish a unified convergence analysis using Lyapunov functions. Guided by our analysis, we develop a family of enhanced accelerated gradient algorithms that leverage the inner product relationship between gradient information and iterative variables to optimize iterative updates. Extensive numerical experiments on unconstrained quadratic optimization and logistic regression validate that the proposed algorithms achieve superior performance compared with existing baseline methods under typical structural conditions.

</details>

### [86] [Beyond Participant-Level Cross-Validation: Reliable Inference for Longitudinal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26205)
*Shahran Rahman Alve*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对纵向传感研究中"记录数据量大但独立受试者少"导致的伪重复问题，提出了参与者级别（participant-level）的估计目标，并通过置换参与者标签、重跑完整分析流程来构建与分析流程匹配的零分布。模拟实验表明，窗口级推断在无效应时以70-80%的比率错误拒绝，而所提出的方法将一类错误控制在0.025-0.100之间。在实际腕动计（n=55）和智能手机传感（n=38）两个公开队列中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向传感研究常从少数参与者收集数千个窗口数据，记录数量多但科学研究单位（参与者）数量少。当结果按参与者定义时，这一不匹配使得看似精确的发现容易受到伪重复（pseudo-replication）、分区选择以及比较多个分析管线后仅报告一者的普通分析灵活性影响。按参与者分割数据虽然能防止一个人的记录跨分割，但并不能校准产生报告数值的标签相关的工作流（折叠构造、预处理、调参、校准和候选选择）。因此需要一种参与者级别的推断方法。

Method: 作者定义了参与者级别的估计目标，通过置换参与者标签并重跑整个分析工作流来获得与分析匹配的零分布。验证中，作者在受控模拟中比较了窗口级推断、窗口bootstrap、参与者bootstrap和所提出的分析匹配检验，并考察了冻结选定管线与重复搜索两种策略下的一类错误控制情况。实际应用中使用腕动计（n=55）和智能手机传感（n=38）两个公开队列，并采用尺度鲁棒的秩池化统计量及排除住院参与者后的匹配置换零分布进行敏感性验证。

Result: 模拟中，窗口级推断在无效应时以70-80%比率的重复实验错误拒绝，窗口bootstrap同样如此；参与者bootstrap在10-17%比率下错误拒绝；所提分析匹配检验在20到80名参与者的队列中三类错误控制在0.025-0.100。冻结选定管线而非重复搜索使一类错误从0.040膨胀至0.240（8个候选）。实际应用：腕动计n=55得到参与者AUROC 0.928且p=0.0050，在尺度鲁棒统计量下和排除住院参与者后结论仍然成立（p=0.0089）；智能手机传感n=38（7个阳性）AUROC 0.636，p=0.1724未达统计显著，敏感性0.143。

Conclusion: 实用的规则是狭义的：每个读取标签的步骤都应纳入被置换的分析之中，重复记录并不会创造额外的独立参与者。分析匹配的置换检验是纵向传感研究中处理伪重复和管线灵活性问题的有效方法。

Abstract: Longitudinal sensing studies routinely collect thousands of windows from a few dozen participants. The records are numerous; the independent scientific units are not. When the outcome is defined per participant, this mismatch makes apparently precise findings vulnerable to pseudo-replication, to partition choice, and to the ordinary analytic flexibility of comparing several pipelines before reporting one. Splitting on participants prevents a person's records from straddling a split, but it does not calibrate the label-dependent workflow fold construction, preprocessing, tuning, calibration, and candidate selection that produced the reported number. We define a participant-level estimand and obtain an analysis-matched null by permuting participant labels and rerunning that entire workflow. In controlled simulation, window-level inference rejects in 70-80% of replicates when no effect exists and a window bootstrap rejects at the same rate; a participant bootstrap still rejects at 10-17%; the analysis-matched test holds 0.025-0.100 across cohorts of 20 to 80 participants. Freezing the selected pipeline instead of repeating the search inflates Type-I error to 0.240 with eight candidates, where repeating it holds 0.040. Applied to two public cohorts, wrist actigraphy (n=55) yields participant AUROC 0.928 with p=0.0050, a conclusion that persists under a scale-robust rank-pooled statistic and under a matched permutation null computed after excluding hospitalized participants (p=0.0089). Smartphone sensing (n=38, 7 positives) yields 0.636 and does not reject (p=0.1724) despite sufficient resolution, with sensitivity 0.143. The practical rule is narrow: every step that reads labels belongs inside the permuted analysis, and repeated records do not create additional independent participants.

</details>


### [87] [A causal graph-informed temporal convolution architecture for interpretable retail electricity price forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26234)
*Yufan Ji,Abdollah Shafieezadeh,Noah Dormady*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果图引导的时间卷积网络（CG-TCN），用于提高零售电力市场价格预测的准确性和可解释性。该方法通过多分辨率分解、因果图发现及图神经嵌入，将学习到的因果结构编码到TCN的卷积和注意力机制中。基于俄亥俄州放松管制市场十年日度数据的实验表明，批发电价主要决定长期零售价格趋势，合同属性影响短期波动，CG-TCN在一步、十步和十五步预测中分别实现了3.08%、3.82%和5.43%的平均绝对百分比误差，优于所有基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放松管制的零售电力市场价格波动显著，且与远期/期货产品存在复杂交互，给运营决策带来挑战。现有预测模型缺乏对零售电价动态中因果结构的显式建模，既难以提升准确性，也无法提供可解释的决策支持。因此，需要一种能同时兼顾预测精度和可解释性的方法。

Method: CG-TCN框架包含三个关键步骤：(1) 多分辨率分解——将零售电价信号分解为半年、季度和月度趋势与高频波动分量；(2) 因果图发现——在分解分量与关键协变量（如批发远期价格、合同特征如提前终止费用）上学习因果结构，并通过领域约束确保因果方向性和外生性；(3) 图神经嵌入——将学习到的因果结构编码为邻接嵌入，条件化TCN的卷积和注意力机制，使表示学习与因果路径对齐。

Result: 在俄亥俄州放松管制市场十年日度12个月固定价格住宅合同数据上，CG-TCN在一步、十步和十五步预测中分别达到3.08%、3.82%和5.43%的MAPE，持续优于所有基准模型。因果分析揭示：批发远期价格主要决定长期零售价格趋势，而合同属性影响短期波动。

Conclusion: CG-TCN通过将因果结构显式嵌入深度时序预测架构，有效结合了预测性能与可解释性，为竞争性电力市场中的市场分析、消费者保护、监管监督、风险评估和采购规划提供了透明且具政策相关性的洞察。

Abstract: Retail electricity markets in deregulated systems face significant price volatility and complex interactions with forward and futures products, posing challenges for effective operational decision-making. This study introduces a Causal Graph-Informed Temporal Convolutional Network (CG-TCN), a forecasting architecture that integrates a learned causal graph into a temporal convolutional network via a graph-neural embedding to enhance both forecasting accuracy and interpretability of retail electricity price dynamics. It first applies a multi-resolution decomposition to isolate semiannual, quarterly, and monthly trends from high-frequency fluctuations. A causal graph is then discovered over these components and key covariates, including wholesale forward prices and retail contract attributes such as early termination fees, with domain constraints that preserve causal directionality and exogeneity. The learned causal structure is encoded as an adjacency embedding that conditions the TCN's convolutions and attention, aligning representation learning with causal pathways. Using ten years of daily 12-month fixed-price residential contracts from Ohio's deregulated market, we find that wholesale forward prices primarily determine long-term retail price trends, whereas contract attributes influence short-term fluctuations. CG-TCN consistently outperforms benchmark models, achieving mean absolute percentage errors of 3.08%, 3.82%, and 5.43% for one-, ten-, and fifteen-step-ahead forecasts of daily retail electricity median prices, respectively. By combining predictive performance with interpretability, CG-TCN provides transparent, policy-relevant insight to support market analytics, consumer protection, regulatory oversight, risk assessment and procurement planning in competitive electricity markets.

</details>


### [88] [FLEX-CP-DT: A Flexible Conditional Power Framework for Interim Futility Analysis in Clinical Trials with Count Endpoints and Temporal Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26338)
*Yanzhao Wang,Dateng Li,Ningya Wang,Haitao Gao,Chenguang Wang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出了FLEX-CP-DT方法，一种分段负二项模型框架，用于在复发事件终点III期试验的中期分析中处理治疗效应随时间变化（延迟起效或效应衰减）的情况，通过公式化的条件功率计算替代标准负二项模型的恒定率假设，改善无效性决策的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准负二项模型假设事件率和治疗效果在研究期间保持恒定，但当中期分析时大量患者随访不完整且治疗效应延迟起效或随时间减弱时，恒定率假设会引入系统性偏倚，导致错误的无效性决策。需要一种能够捕捉时间趋势而不依赖恒定率假设的方法。

Method: 提出FLEX-CP-DT框架，采用分段负二项模型（piecewise negative binomial）捕捉事件率和治疗效应的时间趋势，无需恒定率假设。推导出基于公式的条件功率计算方法，在中期阶段无需重采样或试验模拟即可计算条件功率。

Result: 通过覆盖恒定效应和延迟起效场景的大量模拟，FLEX-CP-DT在恒定率假设成立时表现与标准方法相当，在假设被违反时改善了中期无效性决策。一个以已发表的III期支气管扩张试验为校准的案例研究进一步显示，该方法能降低在延迟起效情况下错误终止有效药物的概率。

Conclusion: FLEX-CP-DT为复发事件终点试验的中期无效性分析提供了一个更稳健的替代方案，尤其是当治疗效果具有延迟起效或随时间衰减特征时，能够减少因恒定率假设偏差而导致的错误终止有效药物的风险。

Abstract: Many large-scale phase III trials with recurrent event endpoints include a pre-planned interim analysis to evaluate early futility. Conditional power (CP), which quantifies the probability of achieving statistical significance at the final analysis given the interim data, is a commonly used tool to support such decisions. The standard negative binomial model with an offset term, widely adopted for analyzing recurrent events, implicitly assumes that event rates and treatment effects remain constant over the study period. At the interim analysis, however, a substantial proportion of patients have incomplete follow-up, and when the treatment effect is delayed in onset or diminishes over time, the constant-rate assumption introduces systematic bias into the interim estimate and can lead to incorrect futility decisions. In this paper, we propose FLEX-CP-DT, a piecewise negative binomial framework that captures temporal trends in both event rates and treatment effects without imposing the constant-rate assumption. The framework yields a formula-based conditional power calculation that does not require resampling or trial simulation at the interim stage. Through extensive simulations spanning constant-effect and delayed-onset scenarios, we demonstrate that FLEX-CP-DT performs comparably to the standard approach when the constant-rate assumption holds and improves interim futility decision-making when it is violated. A case study calibrated to a published phase 3 bronchiectasis trial further illustrates the practical advantage of the proposed method in reducing the probability of falsely terminating an efficacious drug with delayed treatment onset.

</details>


### [89] [Integrating Network Psychometrics and LLMs: The Ising-Embeddings-Model applied to Reliability Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26790)
*Matthias von Davier*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成框架，将网络心理测量学与Linguistic-Integrated Reliability Audit (LiRA)通过改进的Ising模型结合，用于大规模评估中构答题（constructed-response）的评分一致性估计。该模型基于句子嵌入的余弦相似性定义二元正确性标签的联合分布，并利用LiRA的加权多数投票近似Ising模型的条件逻辑分布，支持基准评分生成、不确定性量化和缺失标签插补，无需大量双重评分即可实现可靠的评分一致性审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的大规模评估中构答题评分一致性通常通过双重评分（double-scoring）来估计，但该方法依赖小样本且假设响应之间相互独立，无法充分捕捉语义依赖关系，也难以在全数据集范围内进行可靠的一致性评估。

Method: 提出一个改进的Ising模型，定义二元正确性标签的联合分布，其中成对交互项设为句子嵌入的余弦相似度，全局偏置参数表示题目难度。通过最大伪似然估计参数，并将LiRA的加权多数投票机制作为Ising模型条件逻辑分布的近似；该框架支持基准评分生成、不确定性量化及缺失标签插补。

Result: 该框架利用全数据集而不依赖大量双重评分，能够解释语义依赖关系，并提供评分员不一致性的诊断。LiRA的可扩展方法与概率图模型的集成，为PIRLS、PISA和TIMSS等国际评估提供了全面的可靠性审计工具。

Conclusion: 该集成框架通过结合网络心理测量学与LiRA，克服了传统双重评分方法的小样本和独立性假设局限，为大规模国际评估中构答题的评分一致性估计提供了更全面、可扩展且语义感知的解决方案。

Abstract: Scoring consistency for constructed-response items in large-scale assessments is typically estimated through double-scoring, which uses small samples and assumes independence among responses. We present an integrated framework combining network psychometrics with the Linguistic-Integrated Reliability Audit (LiRA) via a modified Ising model. The model defines a joint distribution over binary correctness labels with pairwise interactions set to the cosine similarity of sentence embeddings and a global bias parameter for item difficulty. LiRA's weighted majority voting over semantic neighborhoods is shown to approximate the conditional logistic distributions of this Ising model. The parametric framework supports benchmark score generation, uncertainty quantification, and missing label imputation; parameters are estimated by maximum pseudo-likelihood. The approach uses the full dataset without requiring extensive double-scoring, accounts for semantic dependencies, and provides diagnostics for rater inconsistencies. The integration of LiRA's scalable methodology with a probabilistic graphical model offers a comprehensive tool for reliability assessment in international assessments such as PIRLS, PISA, and TIMSS.

</details>


### [90] [Climate Physics Dynamic Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26907)
*Gurjeet Sangra Singh,Frantzeska Lavda,Alexandros Kalousis*
