<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型（流匹配、扩散模型）在学习复杂动力系统时通常作为黑盒，忽略了底层物理结构；而基于偏微分方程的物理模型往往因缺失源项或参数化不确定而不完整。需要一种同时结合物理先验与数据驱动能力的方法来提升天气预测的准确性与稳定性。

Method: 提出ClimPhyDM框架，将平流型（advection-type）物理先验与数据驱动组件在变分框架中结合，采用仿真自由（simulation-free）的训练范式，能够捕捉未解析大气动力学的随机性和多模态性。

Result: 在ERA5基准上，ClimPhyDM在小时级（42小时）和月级（5个月）分辨率下均优于ClimODE和GB-DM，在扩展时域上保持更低误差，表明其具有更好的时间稳定性和抗误差累积能力；同时其仿真自由范式仅需单张12GB消费级GPU即可训练。

Conclusion: ClimPhyDM通过将物理先验与数据驱动组件在变分框架中有效融合，在保持物理一致性的同时提升了天气预测的长期稳定性与计算效率，为物理信息驱动的生成式天气预报提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Deep generative models such as flow matching and diffusion models have shown potential for learning complex dynamical systems, but typically act as black boxes that neglect underlying physical structure, while physics-based models governed by partial differential equations are often incomplete due to missing source terms, or uncertain parametrisations. We present Climate Physics Dynamic Matching (ClimPhyDM), a variational simulation-free dynamics informed framework for weather forecasting that combines an advection-type physics prior with data-driven components in a variational framework. % to capture the stochasticity and multi-modality of unresolved atmospheric dynamics. On the ERA5 benchmark at hourly (42-hour) and monthly (5-month) resolutions, ClimPhyDM outperforms ClimODE, and GB-DM, keeping the lower error at extended horizon, indicating improved temporal stability and resistance to error accumulation, while its simulation-free paradigm also enables training on a single modest 12 GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [91] [Modeling Spatially Obfuscated Street-Crime Data using Log-Gaussian Cox Processes on Metric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27117)
*Lulu Jiang,David Bolin*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于街道网络上的犯罪数据建模的log-Gaussian Cox过程框架，专门处理空间混淆（隐私保护代理位置）导致的支撑不匹配问题，通过度量图上的Whittle-Matérn高斯场SPDE表示，实现了沿街道连续变化的犯罪强度建模。模拟研究和伦敦金融城应用均表明该度量图模型优于平面替代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国警方公开的街道级犯罪数据中，发布坐标是匿名的代理位置而非真实案发地点，导致观测支撑与真实街道网络之间不匹配。现有平面点模型和平面聚合模型均未充分考虑街道网络约束，无法准确刻画犯罪强度的空间分布。

Method: 构建log-Gaussian Cox过程框架，将每个观测表示为街道网络上的聚合支撑以解决支撑不匹配问题。潜在对数强度通过度量图上的Whittle-Matérn高斯场（SPDE表示）建模，使犯罪强度沿街道连续变化同时尊重路网几何结构。与平面点模型和平面聚合模型进行比较。

Result: 在街道网络生成的模拟数据中，度量图模型在参数恢复准确性和整体拟合度上均优于平面替代模型。在伦敦金融城应用中，度量图模型在盗窃、抢劫、毒品和自行车盗窃等多种犯罪类型上均取得最佳拟合。环境设施与犯罪风险的关系因犯罪类型而异，超市呈现最一致的正向关联。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析受隐私保护和位置不精确的网络约束空间事件数据提供了有原则的方法，为犯罪学研究和警务策略制定提供了更可靠的建模工具。

Abstract: We develop a log-Gaussian Cox process framework for modelling street-level crime data observed on a road network when the released event locations are spatially obfuscated. Motivated by UK Police street-level crime data, where published coordinates are anonymised proxy locations rather than exact event locations, we address the resulting support mismatch by representing each observation through an aggregated support on the street network. The latent log-intensity is modelled as a Whittle--Matérn Gaussian field defined on a metric graph through an SPDE representation, allowing the crime intensity to vary continuously along streets while respecting the geometry of the road network. We compare the proposed metric-graph aggregated model with two alternatives: a planar point model that treats the released locations as exact points, and a planar aggregated model that accounts for spatial aggregation but ignores the network support. In a simulation study where data are generated on a street network, the metric-graph model provides more accurate parameter recovery and better overall fit than the planar alternative. In the City of London application, the metric-graph model also achieves the best fit across several crime types, including theft from the person, robbery, drugs, and bicycle theft. The results further suggest that the relationship between environmental amenities and crime risk varies by crime type, with supermarkets showing the most consistent positive associations. The proposed framework provides a principled approach for analysing network-constrained spatial event data with privacy-protected and imprecise locations.

</details>


### [92] [The Wasserstein Distance for Mortality Comparisons: Absolute versus Net Differences in Survival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27120)
*Markus Sauerberg*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Wasserstein距离（基于两个生存函数绝对差异的分布性不平等度量）与出生时预期寿命差距之间的关系。当生存函数不交叉时，二者完全对应；当生存函数交叉时（由年龄别死亡率的累积差异驱动），两者产生偏离。基于人类死亡率数据库1990-2020年69,188对人口比较，59.8%的情况下生存函数发生交叉，但逆转幅度通常很小，因此在大多数情况下，预期寿命差距不仅是均值的比较，也量化了两个死亡率制度的整体差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，出生时预期寿命差距被看作两个生存函数的净差异，而Wasserstein距离则衡量绝对差异。当生存函数发生交叉时，净差异可能很小，掩盖了两个人口在死亡率模式上的显著不同。作者旨在厘清这两种度量之间的关系，并通过实证数据识别交叉发生的场景及其幅度。

Method: 理论上，作者将Wasserstein距离与预期寿命差距进行形式化关联，指出不交叉时两者相等，交叉由年龄别死亡率的累积差异驱动。实证上，使用人类死亡率数据库（HMD）1990-2020年的生命表数据，对69,188对人口组合进行生存函数交叉的检测与幅度评估。

Result: 69,188对人口中59.8%的生存函数存在交叉，但逆转幅度通常非常小。这表明尽管交叉现象普遍存在，其实际影响有限。

Conclusion: 在大多数情况下，出生时预期寿命差距不仅是一种均值的比较，而且能够有效量化两个人口死亡率制度的整体差异；Wasserstein距离与预期寿命差距在绝大多数场景下可以提供一致的信息。

Abstract: It is well known that the gap in life expectancy at birth can be seen as the net difference between two survivorship functions. When calculating the absolute difference between the two survivorship functions instead, we derive a distributional inequality measure which is called the Wasserstein distance. The measure quantifies how far apart two probability distributions are by the minimal cost of transporting one into the other. This paper relates the Wasserstein distance to the difference in life expectancy at birth. Both measures correspond to each other, whenever the survivorship functions do not cross, i.e., the net difference equals the absolute difference in the no crossing case. Whether the curves cross is driven by the accumulated difference in age-specific death rates. Crossing occurs in situations where an accumulated survival advantage for given populations is reversed because elevated death rates at later ages outweigh the earlier survival advantage. These cases are particularly interesting because the net difference in survival (or the life expectancy gap) may be small even though the two populations show very different mortality schedules. In our empirical analysis, we search for these cases using life table data from Human Mortality Database life tables for the period 1990 to 2020. Across 69 188 population pairs, survivorship functions cross in 59.8 % of comparisons. Yet, the magnitude of the reversal in survivorship is usually very small. We therefore conclude that in most cases, the gap in life expectancy at birth is not only a comparison of means but quantifies the overall difference between the two mortality regimes

</details>


### [93] [A spatio-temporal block aggregation model for latent log Gaussian outcomes: application on modelling wastewater virus concentration in Wales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27207)
*Stephen Jun Villejo,Peter Diggle,Guangquan Li,Ella White,Matthew Wade,Christopher Williams,Davey L. Jones,Alisha Davies,Marta Blangiardo*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜对数高斯过程的时空模型，用于将空间连续的废水监测信号与不规则空间单元上的聚合观测数据（如废水病毒基因拷贝数）相链接，并在威尔士47个集水区的SARS-CoV-2数据上验证了其预测性能及其与临床阳性率的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 废水流行病学是监测社区传染病动态的重要工具，但废水测量值往往是在不规则空间单元（如集水区）上聚合得到的，这种空间错配使得难以将连续的环境信号与健康结果在行政区域层面进行有效关联。现有方法通常忽略或简化这种块状聚合结构，导致推断结果存在偏差。

Method: 构建一个时空潜对数高斯过程的贝叶斯分层模型，将底层连续空间过程通过积分方式聚合到任意空间配置上，使用线性化集成嵌套拉普拉斯近似（INLA）进行推断和预测，并建立聚合信号与健康结局（如COVID-19核酸检测阳性率）之间的关联模型。

Result: 在2022年8月至2023年7月威尔士47个集水区的SARS-CoV-2 N1基因拷贝数数据上，模型能够较好地捕捉空间和时空动态模式，预测性能良好；估计的病毒基因拷贝数与地方当局层面的COVID-19 PCR阳性率呈现强关联。

Conclusion: 显式地建模块状聚合（block aggregation）对于废水监测数据的分析至关重要，所提出的框架为将环境监测数据整合到公共卫生监测系统中提供了一种灵活且原则性的方法。

Abstract: Wastewater-based epidemiology has emerged as a valuable tool for monitoring community-level infectious disease dynamics, providing population-wide signals that complement clinical surveillance. However, wastewater measurements are often observed as aggregated values over irregular spatial units. This work develops an approach to link an underlying spatially continuous processes and an aggregated outcome. We propose a spatio-temporal model for latent log-Gaussian outcomes that provides a coherent framework for inference and prediction, allowing the process to be integrated over arbitrary spatial configurations. This framework can also be used for subsequent analyses, such as linking wastewater signal to health outcomes at administrative areas. We use a Bayesian framework for inference via the linearised integrated nested Laplace approximation (INLA) approach. We apply the proposed methodology to model SARS-CoV-2 N1 gene copies in wastewater across 47 catchment areas in Wales from the beginning of August 2022 to the end of July 2023. The results demonstrate that the model captures spatial and temporal patterns and has good predictive performance. Results also show that estimated viral gene copies are strongly linked to positivity rates from COVID-19 PCR tests at the local authority level. Our findings highlight the importance of explicitly modelling block aggregation when analysing wastewater surveillance data. The proposed framework provides a flexible and principled approach for integrating environmental surveillance data into public health monitoring systems.

</details>


### [94] [How exceptional was the Big Three era? Extremes and persistence in men's professional tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27362)
*Manuele Leonelli*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯动态Bradley-Terry状态空间模型分析1968-2025年间197,926场比赛，量化网球选手的统治力。结果显示仅德约科维奇在峰值强度上显著超越同代选手，费德勒和纳达尔与博格、麦肯罗和伦德尔难以区分。近年时代的独特性不在于顶尖选手的高度，而在于三位超级选手同时维持顶尖水平的时间长度：在巅峰期，三人在至少五分之四的周数中占据前三，而过去最强的可比十年中前三是四人分享。更显著的异常在于1990至2002年的十二年里，顶尖水平不仅空缺而且被压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了客观评估2003-2023年间网球"三巨头"时代是否如普遍认为的那样是史上最占主导地位的时代，且这些顶尖选手从未直接交锋，需要一种能够通过共同对手跨国比较的量化方法来衡量统治力。

Method: 使用贝叶斯动态Bradley-Terry状态空间模型从1968-2025年间197,926场比赛数据中估计选手实力，通过高阈值的超越数（exceedances）来刻画统治力，用固定窗口平均而非不连续的连胜来衡量顶尖选手持续占据高位的时间，并比较不同时期的巅峰强度和多位顶尖选手并存程度。

Result: 仅德约科维奇在峰值实力上能从同代选手中显著区分出来；费德勒和纳达尔在统计上与博格、麦肯罗、伦德尔无法区分。近年时代的独特之处在于三位顶尖选手同时巅峰的持续时间之长——巅峰期三人占据前三的周数比例达到至少五分之四，而过去最强可比十年前的三年被四人分享；此外1990年起长达十二年的时期内，顶尖分布不仅无人占据高位而且被压缩。

Conclusion: "三巨头"时代的统治力并非来自个体高度（除德约科维奇外），而是来自三位顶尖选手同时处于巅峰的罕见持续性；这提示将"最占统治力时代"的评判从"最高者有多高"转向"多高者有多少、持续多久"的多维框架。

Abstract: Three players won 66 of the 81 Grand Slam titles contested between 2003 and 2023, and their era is widely held to be the most dominant in the history of tennis. Assessing it means comparing players who never met, so that every comparison passes through the opponents each did face. We measure dominance by how far a player stands above the field of his own day, how many stand there at once, and how long they remain, the last through averages over fixed windows rather than through unbroken runs, which are unstable when the strengths beneath them are themselves estimated. Strengths come from a Bayesian dynamic Bradley-Terry state space model, and dominance is characterized through exceedances of a high threshold. Applied to 197,926 matches played between 1968 and 2025, only Djokovic separates from the field on peak strength, Federer and Nadal being indistinguishable from Borg, McEnroe and Lendl. What distinguishes the recent period is not how high its best players stood but how long three of them stood there together. At its height three players held a top-three position in at least four fifths of all weeks, where in the strongest comparable decade of the past those positions were shared among four. The sharper anomaly lies between: for twelve years from 1990 the upper tail was not merely unoccupied but compressed.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了正则系综中非相互作用玻色气体的凝聚体粒子数大偏差估计，发现左右尾概率以不同的指数速率衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚的研究中，理解凝聚体粒子数的涨落行为是核心问题之一。正则系综下粒子数守恒，其大偏差行为（特别是左右尾的不对称性）对理解凝聚过程的统计力学性质具有重要意义，但目前缺乏一般单粒子哈密顿量下的系统性大偏差结果。

Method: 考虑由一般单粒子哈密顿量描述的非相互作用玻色气体，在正则系综框架下，通过大偏差理论对凝聚体中的粒子数进行估计，分别分析左尾和右尾概率的指数衰减速率的渐近行为。

Result: 证明了凝聚体粒子数的左尾和右尾概率随着粒子数趋于无穷而分别以不同的指数速率衰减，即左右尾呈现出不对称的大偏差行为。

Conclusion: 正则系综下非相互作用玻色气体的凝聚体粒子数涨落表现出左右尾不对称的大偏差特性，不同方向上指数速率的差异揭示了正则系综中粒子数涨落的非高斯本质。

Abstract: We consider a non-interacting Bose gas governed by a general one-particle Hamiltonian in the canonical ensemble. Our main results are large-deviation estimates for the number of particles in the Bose--Einstein condensate. We show that the left- and right-tail probabilities decay at different exponential rates as the number of particles tends to infinity.

</details>


### [96] [A closure criterion for electromagnetic curves on the sphere in a uniform ambient field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26412)
*C S López-Monsalvo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究均匀磁场中圆球面上带电测试粒子的运动，通过推广Noether定理将问题化为求积，并给出了轨迹闭合的充要条件（旋转数为整周的有理倍数），同时计算了Mañé严格临界值等几何量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解带电粒子在球面上磁场中的运动结构，特别是闭合轨迹的判据与计数，并建立与磁系统几何（Noether定理、Reeb轨道、临界值）之间的联系。

Method: 利用带磁力系统的Noether定理推广将问题约化为求积；通过完备第一类、第三类Legendre椭圆积分刻画旋转数；对旋转数关于半回旋频率求导并判定符号；引入无量纲比值（半回旋频率/速度）分析闭合轨迹；计算Mañé严格临界值并将高于临界值的闭合轨迹识别为显式接触形式的闭合Reeb轨道。

Result: 旋转数为第三类Legendre完整椭圆积分；轨迹闭合当且仅当该进动是有理数的整周倍数，且该判据在每个两级分汇合的层面上成立；闭合轨迹固定了半回旋频率与速度的单个无量纲比值；给出了携带给定有理缠绕所对应的比值个数；极点在单一level set上可达且运动退化为摆；求得了Mañé严格临界值。

Conclusion: 该问题在磁Noether框架下可完全约化，闭合轨道与有理旋转数、无量纲参数值一一对应，并揭示了其与接触几何中Reeb轨道、Mañé临界值之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We consider the motion of a charged test particle confined to the round unit sphere in a uniform ambient magnetic field. We use an extension of Noether's theorem for systems with magnetic forces to reduce the problem to a quadrature. The rotation number of a trajectory, the azimuth it gains over one oscillation in latitude, is a complete elliptic integral of the third kind in Legendre form. A trajectory closes if and only if that advance is a rational multiple of a full turn. The criterion holds on every level set of the two first integrals. Then, we differentiate the rotation number with respect to the half-cyclotron frequency and sign that derivative on each of the two branches into which a level set divides. The rotation number sees the charge, the mass, the field and the speed through a single dimensionless ratio, that of the half-cyclotron frequency to the speed. A closed trajectory therefore fixes a value of that ratio. We say how many values carry a given rational winding. The poles are attainable on a single level set, where the motion reduces to a pendulum. The field has no flux through the sphere, so it is globally exact. We compute the Mañé strict critical value of the resulting exact magnetic flow. Above that value the closed trajectories are closed Reeb orbits of an explicit contact form.

</details>


### [97] [Quantum cellular automata and invertible phases of matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26456)
*Corey Jones,Nikita Sopenko,Ryan Thorngren*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究均匀局部有限度量空间上的可逆准局部代数（费米子和玻色子），证明了Brauer等价类群与可逆变分相群及量子蜂窝自动机稳定等价类群同构，并通过K-理论提出Kitaev所猜想的可逆变分相Ω-谱的定义，同时给出c=1/2手性Majorana费米子网和(E8)₁共形网的Brauer非平凡显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态物理中拓扑有序相和可逆变分相（invertible phases）的分类是重要问题，Kitaev曾猜想可逆变分相构成Ω-谱。该工作旨在建立可逆准局部代数、可逆变分态与量子蜂窝自动机之间的严格数学对应，并提供高维（如Z²上）非平凡可逆变分态的显式构造。

Method: （1）引入并研究无限维局部von Neumann代数的可逆准局部代数；（2）证明Brauer等价类群与可逆变分态相群及量子蜂窝自动机稳定等价类群三者同构；（3）利用可逆准局部代数与有界扩展同构的对称幺半范畴的K-理论，提出KitaevΩ-谱的定义；（4）通过c=1/2手性Majorana费米子网和(E8)₁共形网构造Brauer非平凡的显式例子；（5）证明有理对角共形场论的时间切片网具有格点自由度，进而推出任何全纯共形网的离散化是可逆的。

Result: （1）建立了Brauer等价类群≅可逆变分相群≅量子蜂窝自动机稳定等价类群的同构关系；（2）给出了Kitaev所猜想Ω-谱的定义；（3）c=1/2手性Majorana网和(E8)₁共形网是Brauer非平凡的，提供Z²上非平凡可逆变分态与量子蜂窝自动机的显式构造；（4）全纯共形网的离散化是可逆的，并揭示了有理对角CFT时间切片网的格点自由度。

Conclusion: 本文建立了可逆准局部代数、可逆变分态与量子蜂窝自动机三者之间的深层数学联系，为Kitaev的Ω-谱猜想提供了具体方案，并通过共形场论给出了Brauer非平凡的高维显式例子，推进了对称保护拓扑相的分类研究。

Abstract: We introduce and study (fermionic and bosonic) invertible quasi-local algebras over uniformly locally finite metric spaces $X$ with infinite-dimensional local von Neumann algebras. We show that the group of Brauer equivalence classes of such algebras is isomorphic to both the group of phases of invertible states and the group of stable equivalence classes of quantum cellular automata over $X\times \mathbb{Z}$. Using K-theory of the symmetric monoidal category of invertible quasi-local algebras and bounded spread isomorphisms, we propose a definition of an $Ω$-spectrum of invertible phases as conjectured by Kitaev. We then show that the $c=\frac{1}{2}$ chiral Majorana fermion net and the $(E_{8})_{1}$ conformal net provide Brauer non-trivial invertible quasi-local algebras, thus providing explicit constructions of non-trivial invertible states and quantum cellular automata on $\mathbb{Z}^{2}$. In addition, we show that the time-slice nets of rational diagonal conformal field theories admit lattice degrees of freedom, which implies the discretization of any holomorphic conformal net is invertible.

</details>


### [98] [Dynamical classical-field limit of Bosonic Gibbs states: Renormalized Hartree NLS correlations in 2D and 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26477)
*Phan Thành Nam,Rongchan Zhu,Xiangchan Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从玻色多体Gibbs动力学推导了环面T^d（d=2,3）上重整化Hartree NLS方程的时间依赖关联函数。与一维情形不同，高维场景下量子粒子数不再一致有界，经典场需要Wick重整化。证明结合了加权Hilbert空间中量子产生子的收敛性，以及由Gibbs测度控制的极限Liouville方程正解的唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多体量子系统在高温/大N极限下约化为经典非线性场方程（如Hartree NLS）是数学物理的核心问题。在低维（d=1）情形已由Fröhlich等人解决，但高维情形由于紫外发散和粒子数无界，需要新的数学工具来处理重整化问题。

Method: 证明方法包含两个关键步骤：(1) 在加权Hilbert空间中证明量子动力学产生子向经典Liouville算子的收敛性；(2) 建立由Gibbs测度控制的极限Liouville方程正解的唯一性。这需要对经典场进行Wick重整化以抵消紫外发散。

Result: 在d=2,3维环面上，验证了从Bosonic多体Gibbs动力学导出的重整化Hartree NLS方程关联函数与该NLS方程的动力学一致，推广了先前一维情形的结果。

Conclusion: 该结果表明即使在需要Wick重整化的高维情形下，平均场极限仍可从Gibbs热初态出发严格建立，为理解高维系统中量子-经典对应提供了严格的数学框架。

Abstract: In this paper we derive the time-dependent correlation functions of the renormalized Hartree NLS equation on the torus $\mathbb T^d$, $d=2,3$, from the corresponding bosonic many-body Gibbs dynamics. In contrast with the 1D problem studied earlier by Fröhlich, Knowles, Schlein and Sohinger, in higher dimensions the scaled quantum particle number is not uniformly bounded and the relevant classical fields require Wick renormalization. Our proof combines convergence of the quantum generators in weighted Hilbert spaces with uniqueness for positive solutions of the limiting Liouville equation that are dominated by the Gibbs measure.

</details>


### [99] [The Sharp Spectral Transition for Almost Mathieu Operators via Alternating Resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26590)
*Jiawei He,Xueyin Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于任意给定的频率共振指数和相位共振指数，存在特定的频率和相位，使得Almost Mathieu算子在此条件下表现出Anderson局域化（当ln|λ|>max{β(α),δ(α,θ)}时），解决了文献[MR4686650]中提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Almost Mathieu算子在临界指数条件下Anderson局域化的猜想。已有研究（如文献MR4686650）提出了关于频率共振指数β(α)和相位共振指数δ(α,θ)的猜想，本文旨在给出严格的数学证明。

Method: 通过构造特定的频率α和相位θ，使其精确实现给定的频率共振指数β(α)和相位共振指数δ(α,θ)，进而证明在这些参数条件下Almost Mathieu算子满足Anderson局域化的充分条件ln|λ|>max{β(α),δ(α,θ)}。

Result: 证明了对于任意给定的频率共振指数和相位共振指数，都能找到对应的频率和相位来实现这些值，从而在这些条件下Almost Mathieu算子展现出Anderson局域化，验证了猜想。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了文献MR4686650中的猜想，为Almost Mathieu算子的Anderson局域化理论补充了重要的完备性结果，揭示了对频率和相位选取的充分自由度。

Abstract: We prove that for any given frequency resonance exponent and phase resonance exponent, there exist a frequency and a phase exactly realizing these values such that the Almost Mathieu operator exhibits Anderson localization for $\ln|λ|>\max\{β(α),δ(α,θ)\}$. This resolves the conjecture in \cite{MR4686650}.

</details>


### [100] [Coupled stochastic variational principles for multiscale surface gravity waves -- Part I: theoretical framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27074)
*Etienne Mémin,Arnaud Debussche*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于表面重力波的完整随机变分框架，将速度势分解为大规模确定性分量和正则化随机噪声项，通过路径变分原理得到拉普拉斯方程、伯努利方程和运动边界条件的随机对应形式，并通过期望意义上的第二变分原理来闭合系统，给出噪声相关函数的演化方程。该耦合系统保持了Zakharov-Craig-Sulem公式的哈密顿结构，并通过WKB近似和Hamilton-Jacobi方程分析显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋波浪建模中未解析的小尺度（亚网格尺度）过程对大规模波浪演化具有显著影响，需要一种系统的随机化框架来捕获这些未解析尺度效应，同时保持变分原理和哈密顿结构的严谨性。

Method: (1) 基于Luke变分原理，引入速度势的两尺度分解（确定性大尺度分量 + 正则化随机噪声项）；(2) 通过路径变分原理推导随机化的Laplace、Bernoulli和运动边界条件；(3) 通过期望意义的第二变分原理闭合系统，得到噪声相关函数的演化方程；(4) 利用WKB近似分析显式解，证明噪声相关函数满足Hamilton-Jacobi方程并具有射线追踪动力学。

Result: 成功构建了耦合的随机表面重力波系统，该体系保持Zakharov-Craig-Sulem公式的哈密顿结构，噪声相关函数的演化通过Hamilton-Jacobi方程与射线追踪动力学描述。

Conclusion: 该框架为海洋波的降阶随机模型和同伴论文中的动理学理论提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a comprehensive stochastic variational framework for surface gravity waves. Starting from Luke's variational principle for irrotational, incompressible free-surface flow, we introduce a decomposition of the velocity potential into a large-scale deterministic component and a regularized stochastic noise term representing unresolved scales. A path-wise variational principle yields the stochastic counterparts of the Laplace, Bernoulli, and kinematic boundary conditions. To close the system, a second variational principle in expectation is formulated, providing evolution equations for the noise correlation functions. The resulting coupled system preserves the Hamiltonian structure of the Zakharov--Craig--Sulem formulation. We further analyze explicit solutions via WKB approximation and show that the noise correlation functions satisfy a Hamilton-Jacobi equation with ray-tracing dynamics. This framework provides a rigorous foundation for reduced stochastic models of ocean waves and for the kinetic theory developed in the companion paper.

</details>


### [101] [Quasi-polynomiality and $N$-point functions of single connected leaky completed Hurwitz numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27109)
*Chongyu Wang,Chenglang Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究单连通k-渗漏(r+1)-完备Hurwitz数的结构，首先证明了在固定亏格下，提取显式的Pochhammer型因子后，稳定的连通渗漏Hurwitz数是关于商[μ_i]模k+r的多项式；随后给出了单连通渗漏Hurwitz数生成函数的闭式公式，可用于研究其亏格依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渗漏Hurwitz数（leaky Hurwitz numbers）是经典Hurwitz数的推广，与分块组合、模空间和数学物理中的各种枚举问题密切相关。研究其代数结构和亏格依赖性有助于深入理解这类数的组合与几何性质。

Method: 本文采用两种主要方法：(1) 通过提取显式的Pochhammer型因子，证明稳定的连通渗漏Hurwitz数在固定亏格下是关于[μ_i]模k+r的多项式函数；(2) 推导单连通渗漏Hurwitz数生成函数的闭式表达式，从而建立其亏格依赖关系。

Result: 证明了固定亏格下，去掉Pochhammer型乘积因子后，稳定连通渗漏Hurwitz数是[μ_i]模k+r的多项式；给出了单连通渗漏Hurwitz数生成函数的闭式公式，该公式可用于分析数的亏格依赖性。

Conclusion: 本文为渗漏Hurwitz数提供了新的多项式结构描述和生成函数的闭式表达，为后续研究渗漏Hurwitz数的亏格依赖关系及更深层组合与几何性质奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the structures of single connected $k$-leaky $(r+1)$-completed Hurwitz numbers. We first prove that, for fixed genus, after extracting an explicit product of Pochhammer type factors, the stable connected leaky Hurwitz numbers for partition $μ$ are polynomials in the quotients $[μ_i]$ modulo $k+r$. We then give a closed formula for the generating function of single connected leaky Hurwitz numbers, which can be used to study the genus dependence of leaky Hurwitz numbers.

</details>


### [102] [Multiscale Loop Vertex Expansion for Cumulants, the $φ^4_2$ Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27188)
*Vincent Rivasseau*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了φ^4_2模型（最简单的非平凡超可重整化模型），使用多尺度圈顶点展开（一种改进的构造性场论方法），证明了累积量在有限阶内的解析性和Borel可和性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: φ^4_2模型是最简单的非平凡超可重整化模型，对其进行严格的构造性场论分析具有重要意义。经典方法在处理该模型时存在局限，需要引入更好的技术手段来证明其解析性和Borel可和性。

Method: 采用多尺度圈顶点展开（multiscale loop vertex expansion），这是对构造性场论方法的改进。该方法结合了多尺度分析和圈顶点展开技术，能够有效地处理超可重整化模型中的紫外和红外问题。

Result: 成功证明了φ^4_2模型的累积量在有限阶内具有解析性，并且满足Borel可和性。

Conclusion: 多尺度圈顶点展开是分析超可重整化模型（如φ^4_2）的有效工具，能够严格证明其解析性和Borel可和性等关键性质，为进一步研究更复杂的量子场论模型提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: We consider the $φ^4_2$ model. It has been called the simplest non- trivial super-renormalisable model. The method we use is the multiscale loop vertex expansion, an improvement of constructive field theory. We prove analyticity and Borel summability of the cumulants up to a finite order.

</details>


### [103] [2-Morita Theory of $E_2$-Algebras and Module Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27228)
*Rongge Xu,Holiverse Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于作者先前关于$E_2$-代数2-Morita等价的工作，通过$n$-Morita范畴$\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})$建立了不同维度拓扑序的Morita等价系统框架，统一了各种$n$-Morita等价概念，并证明了$\mathrm{Mod}_n$函子对于$n=1,2$是等价关系，揭示了双双模在拓扑序缺陷研究中的自然角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在统一和系统化不同维度下拓扑序的Morita等价理论。此前关于$E_2$-代数的2-Morita等价工作为这一方向提供了基础，但仍缺乏一个统一的框架来涵盖各维度的Morita等价概念，同时也需要厘清不同构造（Haugseng与Gwilliam-Scheimbauer）之间的关系。

Method: 作者引入了$n$-Morita范畴$\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})$作为统一框架，将各种$n$-Morita等价概念表述为该范畴中对象的等价。通过定义函子$\mathrm{Mod}_n:\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})\to \mathrm{Mrt}_{E_{n-1}}(\mathrm{LMod}^{\mathrm{rep}}(\mathcal{C}))$，并证明其对$n=1,2$时是范畴等价，将高阶Morita理论的代数描述与其模范畴实现联系起来。在此框架中，双双模（bi-bimodule）自然涌现，为局域模和受限模提供统一描述。

Result: 建立了统一的$n$-Morita范畴框架，证明了对$n=1,2$，$\mathrm{Mod}_n$函子是范畴等价，建立了高阶Morita理论的代数描述与模范畴实现之间的对应关系。同时，双双模的自然涌现及其显式orientation数据和融合规则，澄清了拓扑序中由凝聚产生的缺陷之间的关系。

Conclusion: 该框架为不同维度拓扑序的Morita等价提供了统一且系统的数学描述，双双模概念为局域模与受限模提供了共同的语言，有助于深入理解拓扑序中缺陷的结构与关系。

Abstract: Building on our previous work on 2-Morita equivalence for $E_2$-algebras using topological pictures, we develop a systematic framework for Morita equivalence of topological orders in different dimensions in terms of $n$-Morita categories $\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})$. In this framework, various notions of $n$-Morita equivalence are unified as equivalences of objects in $\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})$. We also compare the constructions of higher Morita categories due to Haugseng and to Gwilliam--Scheimbauer.
  For $n=1,2$, we prove that the functor $\mathrm{Mod}_n:\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})\to \mathrm{Mrt}_{E_{n-1}}(\mathrm{LMod}^{\mathrm{rep}}(\mathcal{C}))$ is an equivalence, relating the algebraic description of higher Morita theory to its realization in terms of module categories. In this formulation, the notion of a bi-bimodule arises naturally and provides a common framework for local and confined modules. Its explicit orientation data, together with the corresponding fusion rules, clarifies the relations among defects arising from condensation in topological orders.

</details>

### [104] [A $σ$-McKay theorem for $π$-separable groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26373)
*David Cabrera-Berenguer*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个Hall σ-子群版本的McKay猜想，针对π-可解群提出，该结果同时推广了经典McKay猜想及其π-可解版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: G. Navarro提出了一个关于Hall σ-子群的McKay猜想类比。此前经典McKay猜想及其π-可解版本分别在不同框架下被研究，本文旨在将两者统一为一个更一般的结论，即通过选取σ=π∪{p}，同时涵盖p-局部和π-结构的信息。

Method: 针对π-可解群G，取素数p并令σ=π∪{p}，利用Hall σ-子群H的存在性，考虑σ'-次数不可约特征标集合Irr_{σ'}(G)，证明其基数等于正规化子N_G(H)中对应的不可约特征标集合Irr_{σ'}(N_G(H))的基数。

Result: 证明了对于任意π-可解群G，|Irr_{σ'}(G)|=|Irr_{σ'}(N_G(H))|，即G与其Hall σ-子群的正规化子在σ'-次数不可约特征标数量上相等。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了经典McKay猜想（当π为空集时退化为p-情形）及其π-可解版本，为不可约特征标计数问题提供了统一的Hall σ-子群框架下的证明。

Abstract: We prove a Hall $σ$-subgroup analogue of the McKay conjecture for $π$-separable groups proposed by G. Navarro. This result simultaneously generalizes the classical McKay conjecture and its $π$-separable version. More precisely, if $G$ is $π$-separable, $p$ is a prime and $σ=π\cup\{p\}$, then it is known that there exists $H$ a Hall $σ$-subgroup of $G$. In this case, we prove that $|{\rm Irr}_{σ'}(G)|=|{\rm Irr}_{σ'}(\mathbf{N}_{G}(H))|$.

</details>


### [105] [Auslander-Reiten (n+2)-angles and local finiteness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26653)
*Jian He,Yu-Zhe Liu,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对偶数n的(n+2)-角化范畴，给出了Zhou定理（奇数n情形）的一个部分肯定回答，通过建立一个充分条件使得Auslander-Reiten (n+2)-角生成的Grothendieck群关系可以推导出范畴局部有限性，并证明该充分条件被一大类例子满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhou证明了当n为奇数时，如果Auslander-Reiten (n+2)-角生成Grothendieck群的关系，则该(n+2)-角化范畴是局部有限的。但该结论对偶数n是否成立仍是开放问题。本文旨在部分回答这一开放问题，即寻找一个充分条件使得偶数n情形下同样的蕴含关系成立。

Method: 本文通过建立一个新的充分条件来证明偶数n情形下Auslander-Reiten (n+2)-角生成Grothendieck群关系可推出范畴的局部有限性，并进一步构造和验证一大类满足该充分条件的例子，以说明该结果具有广泛的适用性。

Result: 本文给出了上述开放问题的一个部分肯定回答：建立了一个充分条件，在该条件下，偶数n的(n+2)-角化范畴满足Zhou定理的相应结论；并且证明了该充分条件被一大类广泛的例子所满足，表明结果不仅仅适用于孤立的特例。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了对(n+2)-角化范畴Grothendieck群与其局部有限性之间关系的理解，特别是在偶数n情形下取得了进展，但仍未完全解决原始开放问题，且充分条件的具体形态及适用范围可在后续工作中进一步探索。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal C$ be an $(n+2)$-angulated category. Zhou proved that, when $n$ is odd, if the Auslander-Reiten $(n+2)$-angles generate the relations for the Grothendieck group of $\mathcal C$, then $\mathcal C$ is locally finite. Whether the corresponding statement remains valid for even $n$ is still open. In this paper, we give a partial affirmative answer to this problem by establishing a sufficient condition under which the same implication holds for even $n$. We further show that our sufficient condition is satisfied by a broad class of examples, thereby demonstrating that the result extends well beyond isolated cases.

</details>


### [106] [An enhanced Helgason-Johnson bound for $Sp(p, q)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26664)
*Zhan Ying,Chao-Ping Dong*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在不可约酉表示π为无穷维的假设下，改进了1969年Helgason-Johnson关于辛群Sp(p,q)的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Helgason-Johnson于1969年给出了Sp(p,q)上的某个界，本文希望在无穷维不可约酉表示的假设下对该界进行改进，以获得更精确的结果。

Method: 利用无穷维不可约酉表示π的假设条件，对Helgason-Johnson在1969年为Sp(p,q)建立的界进行理论上的改进。

Result: 在无穷维不可约酉表示的假设下，成功改进了Helgason-Johnson界。

Conclusion: 通过添加无穷维不可约酉表示的约束条件，可以在Sp(p,q)上获得比1969年Helgason-Johnson界更优的结果。

Abstract: Under the assumption that the irreducible unitary representation $π$ is infinite dimensional, this paper improves the Helgason-Johnson bound in 1969 for $Sp(p, q)$.

</details>


### [107] [Unitary Shimura Correspondence for Complex Classical Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26795)
*Wan-Yu Tsai,Kayue Daniel Wong,Hongfeng Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个从复正交群SO_2n(C)或辛群Sp_2n(C)的容许Harish-Chandra模的Grothendieck群到其Spin覆盖的genuine表示的Grothendieck群的提升算子，并显式确定了与任何特殊幂等轨道相关的特殊幂等表示之和的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复Spin群的genuine表示，特别是genuine幂等表示的结构与幺正性，并建立它们与经典群（SO和Sp）表示之间的联系。

Method: 构造从Grothendieck群到Grothendieck群的提升算子，利用特殊幂等轨道（特殊unipotent表示）的对应关系，显式计算出这些表示之和的提升。

Result: 确定了与任何特殊幂等轨道相关的特殊unipotent表示之和的提升，证明了这些提升中出现的表示（若非零）是复Spin群的genuine幂等表示且是幺正的。

Conclusion: 该提升算子在大量幺正表示类上保持幺正性，为复Spin群genuine表示理论提供了重要工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct a lifting operator from the Grothendieck group of admissible Harish-Chandra modules of $G =\mathrm{SO}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$ (resp. $\mathrm{Sp}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$) to that of genuine representations of $\mathrm{Spin}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$ (resp. $\mathrm{Spin}_{2n+1}(\mathbb C)$). We determine the lift of the sum of special unipotent representations attached to any ${}^{\vee}\mathcal O \subseteq {}^{\vee}\mathfrak g$ explicitly. In particular, the representations occurring in these lifts, if nonzero, are genuine unipotent representations of complex Spin groups and are unitary. As a consequence, the lifting operator preserves unitarity on a large class of unitary representations.

</details>


### [108] [The Saito determinant for extended affine Weyl discriminant strata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26817)
*Andrea Brini,Karoline van Gemst*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展仿射Weyl群的反射表示在判别式任意层上的商空间中的Saito度量的形式，计算了所有层和Dynkin类型的Saito行列式，并证明其可被相关超平面排列的限制根的q-模拟乘积整除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将Saito度量的因子分解性质从低余维数情形推广到判别式的任意余维数层，同时为Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan关于Coxeter群的结果提供q-模拟细化，并推广Weyl分母公式中的因子分解。

Method: 通过对扩展仿射Weyl群的反射表示在判别式各层上的限制，系统计算Saito行列式，并利用限制根的q-模拟建立整除关系，覆盖所有Dynkin类型和判别式层。

Result: 对所有层和Dynkin类型计算出了Saito行列式，证明其可被一组q-模拟的限制根的乘积整除，同时将Weyl分母公式中的因子分解推广到更高余维数的层，并给出了Coxeter群相关结果的q-模拟细化。

Conclusion: Saito度量的行列式在判别式任意层上具有统一的q-模拟因子分解结构，这同时推广了Weyl分母公式和Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan的结果。

Abstract: We investigate the form of the Saito metric on quotients of the reflection representation of an extended affine Weyl group, restricted to an arbitrary stratum of its discriminant. We compute the Saito determinant for all strata and Dynkin types and show that it is divisible by a product of q-analogues of restricted roots for the associated hyperplane arrangement. Our results simultaneously generalise the factorisation in the Weyl denominator formula to higher codimension strata, and provide q-analogue refinements of results of Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan for Coxeter groups.

</details>


### [109] [Noncommutative Cluster Varieties and Moduli Spaces of Local Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27284)
*Zachary Greenberg,Dani Kaufman,Merik Niemeyer,Anna Wienhard*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了非交换簇变量A_{R,S}（同时涵盖Fock-Goncharov、Li、Goncharov-Shen等人的簇变量），并定义了Jordan分裂群这一大类代数群。通过统一Tits-Kantor-Koecher构造与分裂李代数构造，利用根系统R的grading来构建此类群的李代数，并分类了R-graded对(G,Θ)。进一步定义了Jordan代数点的A_{R,S}，用于参数化G-局部系统，给出非交换簇结构于R-Bruhat胞腔上，推广了Berenstein-Fomin-Zelevinsky的簇代数。最后讨论了正结构、正半群及其与Guichard-Wienhard正结构的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在统一和推广多种已有的簇变量构造（如Fock-Goncharov、Goncharov-Shen、Berenstein-Retakh等），同时引入Jordan分裂群这一新概念，建立簇变量与代数群、局部系统、正结构之间的深层联系，为理解正配置和正表示提供代数框架。

Method: 1) 对每个约化根系统R和标记曲面S构造非交换簇变量A_{R,S}，统一推广多个已知构造；2) 定义Jordan分裂群G，通过统一Tits-Kantor-Koecher构造与分裂李代数构造构建其李代数；3) 利用根系统grading，分类满足条件（仅依赖于简单根子集Θ）的R-graded对(G,Θ)；4) 定义Jordan代数点参数化G-局部系统，在圆盘情形给出非交换簇结构于双R-Bruhat胞腔；5) 对形式实Jordan代数定义正结构、正半群，并与Guichard-Wienhard的正结构建立等价关系。

Result: 成功构造了统一框架下的非交换簇变量A_{R,S}，定义了Jordan分裂群并完成R-graded对的分类；建立非交换簇结构与双R-Bruhat胞腔的推广关系；证明形式实Jordan代数情形下(G,Θ)的正结构恰好等价于Guichard-Wienhard定义的正结构，并给出正配置与正表示性质的代数证明。

Conclusion: 本文通过构造非交换簇变量和Jordan分裂群，在簇代数、李理论与正表示之间建立了统一的代数框架，并通过正结构的代数刻画完善了对正配置和正表示的理解。

Abstract: In this article, we construct noncommutative cluster varieties, $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$, for each reduced root system $R$ and marked surface $S$ simultaneously generalizing the cluster varieties of Fock-Goncharov, Li, Goncharov-Shen, Berenstein-Retakh, Goncharov-Kontsevich, and our previously introduced polygonal cluster algebras. Additionally, we define a large class of algebraic groups, we call Jordan split groups. Given a reduced root system $R$ and a family of Jordan algebras, the Lie algebra for $G$ is constructed by unifying the Tits-Kantor-Koecher construction for a single Jordan algebra with the construction of a split Lie algebra.
  The notion of Jordan split groups is closely related to a grading of its Lie algebra by the root system $R$. We show that these gradings are usually induced by a choice of standard parabolic subalgebra $\mathfrak{p}_Θ$ and we classify $R$-graded pairs $(G,Θ)$ via a condition depending only on the subset $Θ\subset Δ$ of the set of simple roots. Next, we define Jordan algebra points of $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$ which parameterize $G$-local systems on $S$ with boundary decoration related to cosets $G/U_Θ$ when $G$ is Jordan split of type $R$. When $S$ is a disk, points of $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$ parameterize configurations of decorated flags. We use this to give noncommutative cluster structures on the double $R$-Bruhat cells of $G$, generalizing the cluster algebras of Berenstein-Fomin-Zelevinsky. When each Jordan algebra is formally real, we say that $G$ has a positive structure with respect to $Θ$. This defines a positive semigroup in $G$. For real algebraic groups, the pairs $(G,Θ)$ which have positive structures are exactly those which admit a positive structure as defined by Guichard-Wienhard and we give algebraic proofs of many of the properties of positive configurations of flags and of positive representations.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Coordinate-Deletion Bundles from Composition Algebras: Hopf Defects, $KO$-Classes, and Real Projective Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26300)
*Marina Palaisti*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文通过删除实合成除代数D（维度d=2,4,8）在每个标准仿射坐标卡上的一个齐次坐标，利用左乘矩阵作为转移数据，在RP^d上构造了一个显式的秩d实向量丛E_D。该丛在去掉d+1个坐标点后有一个由矩阵确定的平凡化，每个删除点对应的局部衔接映射为S^{d-1}→SO(d)，即复/四元/八元Hopf衔接映射。文章证明此构造提供了γ^{⊕d}的显式带框架缺陷实现，特别给出了RP^8上γ^{⊕8}的半代数八元实现，并讨论了任意域上的Pfister二次丛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望在实射影空间RP^d上为熟知向量丛γ^{⊕d}（平凡丛上秩d的稳定等价类）提供显式的带框架缺陷构造，且该构造应自然体现复、四元数、八元数三种经典的Hopf衔接映射结构，从而统一并显式化这些向量丛的几何实现。

Method: 令D为实合成除代数（复数C、四元数H或八元数O，维度d分别为2,4,8）。在每个标准仿射坐标卡上删除一个齐次坐标，将剩余坐标解释为D中的元素，并用对应的左乘矩阵作为转移数据构造秩d实向量丛E_D→RP^d。利用这些矩阵在去掉d+1个坐标点处给出确定的无平凡化，每个被删除点的局部衔接映射恰为u↦L_u，即对应的Hopf衔接映射。通过同一矩阵导出的截面，其非退化零点恰为坐标点，据此计算Stiefel-Whitney类和Euler类，结合K-理论与Euler类消去论证E_D≅γ^{⊕d}。

Result: 构造的向量丛满足E_D≅γ^{⊕d}，其Stiefel-Whitney类为w(E_D)=1+x^d。特别地，该构造给出了RP^8上γ^{⊕8}的半代数八元实现，具有九个指定的局部Hopf缺陷。此外还描述了在任意域上的Pfister二次丛推广。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一族统一的显式向量丛构造，将复、四元数和八元数Hopf衔接映射作为RP^d上γ^{⊕d}的局部缺陷严格实现，兼具代数与几何的显式性，并推广到任意域上的Pfister二次丛。

Abstract: Let \(D\in\{\C,\Hh,\Oct\}\) be a real composition division algebra of dimension \(d\in\{2,4,8\}\). We construct an explicit rank-\(d\) real vector bundle $ E_D\longrightarrow\RP^d$ by deleting one homogeneous coordinate on each standard affine chart, interpreting the remaining coordinates as an element of \(D\), and using the corresponding left-multiplication matrices as transition data. The bundle admits a matrix-determined trivialization away from the \(d+1\) coordinate points. Relative to this trivialization, each deleted point has local clutching map $S^{d-1}\longrightarrow\SO(d),\; u\longmapsto L_u$, which is respectively the complex, quaternionic, or octonionic Hopf clutching map.
  A section arising from the same matrices has exactly the coordinate points as nondegenerate zeros. Consequently, $w(E_D)=1+x^d$, and real \(K\)-theory together with Euler-class cancellation gives $E_D\congγ^{\oplus d}$. Thus the construction supplies explicit framed-defect realizations of these familiar bundles. In particular, it gives a semialgebraic octonionic realization of \(γ^{\oplus8}\) on \(\RP^8\) with nine specified local Hopf defects. We also describe the associated Pfister quadratic bundle over arbitrary fields.

</details>


### [111] [Local Bousfield classes via homological support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26876)
*Tobias Barthel,Natalia Castellana,Drew Heard,Beren Sanders,Changhan Zou*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究大张量三角范畴中对象A的局部化范畴的同调与上同调Bousfield类。同调支撑类恰好分类同调Bousfield类当且仅当A-相对同调检测性质成立，且这等价于A与同调残域余积Bousfield等价。上同调Bousfield类由同调余支撑分类则更强，等价于A-相对同调分层。这在绝对情形也是新结果。将结果应用于色同伦理论，分类了Morava K-理论余积谱局部化的同调Bousfield类，并完全刻画了此类色局部化的相对同调分层，回答了Wolcott关于调和谱范畴的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解张量三角范畴中局部化范畴的Bousfield类结构，建立同调检测、分层与Bousfield类分类之间的精确关系，并将其应用于色同伦理论以回答开放问题。

Method: 利用A-相对形式的同调检测性质与同调分层条件，建立同调Bousfield类与同调支撑、上同调Bousfield类与同调余支撑之间的分类等价关系，并通过构造具有空同调余支撑的局部谱来产生非同调的上同调Bousfield类例子。

Result: 证明了同调Bousfield类的分类等价于A-相对同调检测，亦等价于A与同调残域余积的Bousfield等价；上同调Bousfield类的分类等价于A-相对同调分层；分层还等价于同调Bousfield类分类加上所有上同调Bousfield类都是同调的。应用于色同伦理论，分类了Morava K-理论余积局部化的同调Bousfield类，并完全刻画了相对同调分层，回答了Wolcott关于调和谱的问题。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Bousfield类分类与同调检测/分层之间的精确等价关系，并将其成功应用于色同伦理论，解决了长期悬而未决的问题，构造了新的非同调上同调Bousfield类例子。

Abstract: Given an object $A$ in a big tensor-triangulated category, we study the homological and cohomological Bousfield classes of the associated localization: the tensor-triangulated category of $A$-local objects. We show that the homological support classifies the homological Bousfield classes of the $A$-local category precisely when an $A$-relative form of the homological detection property holds. Moreover, we prove that this holds if and only if $A$ is Bousfield equivalent to a coproduct of homological residue fields. The analogous classification of cohomological Bousfield classes by homological cosupport is strictly stronger: it is equivalent to an $A$-relative form of homological stratification. This equivalence between stratification and the classification of cohomological Bousfield classes is new even in the absolute case. A further surprise is that stratification is also equivalent to the classification of homological Bousfield classes together with the statement that every cohomological Bousfield class is homological. Applied to chromatic homotopy theory, these results classify the homological Bousfield classes of any localization of spectra with respect to a coproduct of Morava $K$-theories. This covers many localizations of interest. We also completely characterize when such chromatic localizations are relatively homologically stratified. This yields new examples of cohomological Bousfield classes that are not homological. In particular, it answers a question of Wolcott concerning the category of harmonic spectra. Our examples are produced by exhibiting local spectra with empty homological cosupport.

</details>


### [112] [On orientability, Poincaré duality, and connectivity of GKM graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26999)
*Oliver Goertsches,Panagiotis Konstantis,Leopold Zoller*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究抽象GKM图的可定向性组合概念及其与Guillemin-Zara意义下图同调的联系，证明GKM图的可定向性等价于有理（非等变）图同调代数的Poincaré对偶性，并由此推出可定向GKM图在移除任意单个顶点后仍保持连通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索抽象GKM图的可定向性这一组合性质与图同调代数结构（特别是Poincaré对偶性）之间的内在联系，以建立组合性质与代数拓扑性质之间的桥梁。

Method: 通过严格证明建立"GKM图可定向"与"有理图同调代数满足Poincaré对偶"这两个性质的等价性，并利用这一等价性作为工具推导可定向GKM图的顶点连通性（移除单顶点后仍连通）这一应用结论。

Result: 证明了GKM图可定向性等价于有理非等变图同调代数的Poincaré对偶性；作为应用，证明了可定向GKM图在移除任意单个顶点后仍然是连通的。

Conclusion: 图的可定向性这一纯组合概念与图同调的Poincaré对偶性之间存在本质等价关系，且该等价关系可进一步导出关于图结构连通性的新结果。

Abstract: We investigate a combinatorial notion of orientability for abstract GKM graphs and its connections to graph cohomology in the sense of Guillemin--Zara. In particular, we prove that orientability of the GKM graph is equivalent to Poincaré duality of the rational (non-equivariant) graph cohomology algebra. As an application, we prove that orientable GKM graphs remain connected after removing any single vertex.

</details>


### [113] [Persistence Meets Resistance: Doubling Down on Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27269)
*Benedikt Kolbe,Tim Mayr*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出在持久同调框架下，针对有限度量空间滤过的稳定不变量进行近似计算的新算法。在1-参数情形下，通过重新审视贪心排列技术，首次实现任意稳定条形码的加性ε-近似的线性时间算法；在多参数情形下，对归一化测度双滤过（如multicover和subdivision-Rips双滤过）的任何稳定不变量，可以在对n常数时间（即与n无关）内概率性近似。此外，通过细粒度复杂性视角，证明矩阵秩的计算可归约到Vietoris–Rips或Čech滤过条形码的近似问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久同调是计算拓扑中分析度量空间几何与拓扑结构的重要工具，但稳定不变量（如条形码）的精确计算在高维或大规模数据上代价高昂。特别是多参数持久同调的精确计算本质困难，因此发展近似算法以在大规模数据上高效估计稳定不变量是核心动机。

Method: (1) 在1-参数情形下，以新颖方式重新利用贪心排列（greedy permutations）技术，构造线性时间算法，对任何稳定条形码计算加性ε-近似；(2) 在多参数情形下，推导均匀采样的收敛率和近似质量界，将单参数方法推广到归一化测度双滤过（包括multicover和subdivision-Rips双滤过），实现时间与n无关的常数时间概率近似；(3) 通过细粒度复杂性归约，证明近似条形码问题至少与矩阵秩计算同样困难，给出加性和乘法（常数因子）两种归约变体。

Result: (1) 在1-参数情形下，首次获得任意稳定条形码加性ε-近似的线性时间算法；(2) 在多参数情形下，对归一化测度双滤过的稳定不变量可在常数时间（相对于n）内概率性近似，常数依赖于加倍维度、直径和成功概率；(3) 通过细粒度复杂性分析，建立矩阵秩计算到Vietoris–Rips/Čech滤过条形码近似问题的归约，表明该近似问题的下界与矩阵秩计算相当。

Conclusion: 本文在n点度量空间（加倍维度增长为o(log n)且有界直径）的设定下，系统性地推进了持久同调稳定不变量的近似计算理论。1-参数情形实现线性时间近似，多参数情形实现与n无关的常数时间近似，同时通过细粒度复杂性归约揭示了问题的固有计算难度，为该领域后续研究提供了算法设计与复杂性下界的双重贡献。

Abstract: We present results on the approximate computation of stable invariants for filtrations of finite metric spaces in the context of persistent homology. We establish novel approximation algorithms in the setting of $n$-point metric spaces where the growth of the doubling dimension is in $o(\log n)$ and the diameter is bounded.
  In the $1$-parameter case, by revisiting known techniques (greedy permutations) in a new way, we derive the first linear-time algorithms for the problem of computing additive $\varepsilon$-approximations of any stable barcode.
  By deriving bounds on the convergence rate and the approximation quality of uniform samples, we extend the approach to selected multiparameter filtrations. We show that for normalized measure bifiltrations, including the multicover and subdivision-Rips bifiltration, any stable invariant can be probabilistically approximated in time constant in $n$. The constants in the running times of our algorithms depend on the doubling dimension, the diameter and the success probability.
  We further study the problem through the lens of fine-grained complexity and show that computing the rank of a matrix reduces to that of approximating the barcode of the Vietoris--Rips or Čech filtration. We present two variants of the reduction, one for sufficiently good additive approximations and the other for any constant factor multiplicative approximations.

</details>


### [114] [Koszul duality and Morita categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27450)
*Max Blans*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了谱中operad和cooperad的$(\infty,2)$-范畴版本的Koszul对偶，即operad与双模构成的Morita范畴和cooperad与双余模构成的对偶Morita范畴之间存在等价关系，该结果涵盖了文献中各种形式的operadic Koszul对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的operadic Koszul对偶有多种形式出现在文献中，但缺乏一个统一的高阶范畴论框架来统摄和证明这些结果，本文旨在建立$(\infty,2)$-范畴层面的Koszul对偶，将其推广到谱范畴并统一已有结果。

Method: 作者通过构造operad与双模构成的Morita范畴以及cooperad与双余模构成的对偶Morita范畴，在$(\infty,2)$-范畴的框架下建立了这两个范畴之间的等价关系，从而证明了谱中operad和cooperad的Koszul对偶。

Result: 证明了在谱环境中，operad与双模的Morita范畴与cooperad与双余模的对偶Morita范畴之间存在范畴等价，该结果统一并涵盖了文献中已有的各种operadic Koszul对偶形式。

Conclusion: 本文在$(\infty,2)$-范畴的抽象框架下建立了谱中operadic Koszul对偶的统一陈述和证明，为理解operad与cooperad之间的对偶关系提供了高阶范畴论的基础性结果。

Abstract: We prove an $(\infty,2)$-categorical version of Koszul duality for operads and cooperads in spectra by showing that there is an equivalence between a Morita category of operads and bimodules and a dual Morita category of cooperads and bicomodules. This result subsumes various forms of operadic Koszul duality present in the literature.

</details>
