### [139] [A simple algorithm for the summation of alternating series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26287)
*Eleonora Denich,Paolo Novati,Alvise Sommariva*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用sinc（梯形）求积法则计算交错级数之和的方法，通过将级数改写为带Abel权重的加权积分，并分析余项与求积点数之间的关系，提供了数值实验验证误差估计的可靠性以及自动求和算法的有效性，所有Matlab代码以开源形式公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错级数求和是数值分析中的重要问题，其通项可用解析函数表达时，需要一种高效且可靠的数值计算方法。传统方法在精度控制和自动求和方面存在不足，因此需要新的求积策略。

Method: 首先将交替级数改写为带Abel权重的加权积分形式，然后采用sinc（梯形）求积法则对该积分进行离散逼近，并深入研究余项关于求积点数n的渐近误差估计。

Result: 推导出了基于sinc求积法则的余项误差界，数值实验验证了误差估计的可靠性，并展示了算法在预设精度下自动进行级数求和的能力。所有Matlab代码已作为开源软件公开在作者主页。

Conclusion: 本文提出的sinc求积方法为解析通项的交错级数求和提供了一种高效、可靠且可自动控制精度的数值方案，同时附带开源实现便于实际应用和复现。

Abstract: This paper deals with the computation of the sum of alternating series, whose general terms can be expressed by means of analytic functions. After rewriting the series as a weighted integral with the Abel weight, we employ the sinc (trapezoidal) rule and analyze the remainder term with respect to the number of quadrature points. We provide some numerical experiments to show the reliability of the derived error estimate and to test the algorithm for automatic summation with prescribed accuracy. All the Matlab codes used in the present paper can be found as open-source software at the authors' homepage.

</details>


### [140] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Biot's Model via Fixed-Stress Splitting and Energy Natural Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26303)
*Kexin Sun,Qiang Liu,Minfu Feng,Mingchao Cai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为FS-ENGD-PINN的鲁棒物理信息神经网络求解器，用于解决强耦合多物理场系统（如Biot固结模型）中标准PINN性能退化的问题。该方法通过固定应力（FS）分裂方案解耦多孔弹性系统、能量自然梯度下降（ENGD）加速优化，并结合三场混合公式解决近不可压缩状态下的体积锁死，实验表明其在精度和鲁棒性上均优于标准PINN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINN在求解强耦合多物理场系统（如Biot固结模型）时，由于病态的优化环境导致性能严重退化，需要一种更稳健的PINN求解器来应对此类挑战。

Method: 该方法包含三个核心策略：(1) 采用固定应力（FS）分裂方案将耦合的多孔弹性系统分解为收缩力学子问题和流动子问题，提高训练稳定性与收敛性；(2) 引入能量自然梯度下降（ENGD），在函数空间中近似牛顿方向，有效缓解刚性导致的收敛缓慢问题；(3) 引入带附加总压力变量的三场混合公式到PINN框架中，解决近不可压缩状态下的体积锁死问题。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，FS-ENGD-PINN在精度和鲁棒性方面一致优于标准PINN公式，在广泛材料参数范围内为多孔弹性提供统一且可靠的学习型求解器。

Conclusion: FS-ENGD-PINN通过物理解耦与几何感知优化的协同，为强耦合多物理场问题的PINN求解提供了一种统一且可靠的方案，显著提升了求解精度与稳定性。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have recently gained considerable attention as a mesh-free framework for solving partial differential equations. Nevertheless, their performance deteriorates when applied to strongly coupled multiphysics systems, such as Biot's consolidation model, due to severely ill-conditioned optimization landscapes. In this work, we propose a robust PINN-based solver, termed FS-ENGD-PINN, which synergistically integrates physics-based decoupling with geometry-aware optimization. Specifically, the Fixed-Stress (FS) splitting scheme is employed to decompose the coupled poroelastic system into contractive mechanics and flow subproblems, thereby significantly improving training stability and convergence. To further accelerate optimization, we adopt Energy Natural Gradient Descent (ENGD), which approximates the Newton direction in function space effectively mitigates stiffness-induced slow convergence. Moreover, to address volumetric locking arising in the nearly incompressible regime, we incorporate a three-field mixed formulation with an additional total pressure variable into the PINN framework. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed FS-ENGD-PINN consistently outperforms standard PINN formulations in terms of accuracy and robustness, providing a unified and reliable learning-based solver for poroelasticity across a wide range of material parameters.

</details>


### [141] [Physics-Informed Stochastic Configuration Machine: A Backpropagation-Free Neural Network with Fast Training for Nonlinear Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26549)
*Yuehao Song,Zhong Chen,Lihui Cen,Liang Wu,Kai Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息随机配置机（PI-SCM），一种无需反向传播的框架，通过解析计算非线性微分算子的局部雅可比矩阵，将物理损失线性化并映射到统一线性代数子空间，利用广义线性最小二乘求解器显式确定最优网络权重，从而克服PINNs的梯度下降瓶颈和非凸优化难题，实现前向与逆问题的高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs虽然作为求解复杂微分方程的变革性范式，但依赖基于反向传播的梯度下降和自动微分，带来显著的计算瓶颈和严重的非凸优化挑战，限制了其可扩展性和实时应用能力。

Method: 解析评估非线性微分算子的局部雅可比矩阵以实现物理损失的线性化表示，并投影到统一线性化代数子空间，从而通过广义线性最小二乘求解器序列显式确定最优网络权重；开发渐进式算法套件包含局部构造（PI-SC-I）、滑动窗口更新（PI-SC-II）和全局更新（PI-SC-III），并严格建立其通用逼近性质。

Result: 大量实验表明PI-SCM实现了高保真预测精度和鲁棒的参数识别，同时相较标准PINNs将训练过程加速了数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作为下一代实时科学机器学习应用提供了高效且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: While Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as a transformative paradigm for solving complex differential equations, their reliance on backpropagation-based gradient descent and automatic differentiation (AD) imposes significant computational bottlenecks and severe non-convex optimization challenges. To overcome these fundamental limitations, we propose the Physics-Informed Stochastic Configuration Machine (PI-SCM), a novel backpropagation-free framework for both forward and inverse problems in differential equations. The core mathematical contribution lies in the analytical evaluation of local Jacobians for nonlinear differential operators, which facilitates a linearized representation of the physical loss and projects it into a unified, linearized algebraic subspace. This reformulation allows for the explicit determination of optimal network weights via a sequence of generalized linear least squares solvers, effectively bypassing the iterative traps of traditional nonlinear optimizers. We develop a progressive algorithmic suite comprising localized construction (PI-SC-I), sliding-window updating (PI-SC-II), and global updating (PI-SC-III), and rigorously establish their universal approximation properties. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PI-SCM achieves high-fidelity predictive accuracy and robust parameter identification while accelerating the training process by orders of magnitude compared to standard PINNs. Our work provides a highly efficient and scalable foundation for next-generation, real-time Scientific Machine Learning applications.

</details>


### [142] [A stable DG-POD reduced-order method for dynamic linear viscoelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26625)
*Byeong-Chun Shin,Yongseok Jang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于本征正交分解（POD）的降阶建模框架，用于求解由广义Maxwell模型控制的动态线性粘弹性问题。通过内变量重构本构关系，采用对称内惩罚间断伽辽金方法进行空间离散，并分别使用向后欧拉或Crank-Nicolson格式进行时间离散。降阶模型通过将全离散系统投影到位移快照构建的低维空间中获得。对于全阶间断伽辽金格式，作者给出了适定性证明并推导了不使用Gronwall不等式的先验稳定性界，该界随最终时间至多线性增长而非指数增长。同时还导出了空间误差估计以及将空间离散、时间离散和POD截断效应分离的误差分解。数值实验验证了收敛行为、评估了不同内积对约化基构建的影响，并展示了在考虑快照生成和基构建成本后仍能显著节省计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态线性粘弹性问题的全阶数值模拟通常需要求解大规模离散系统，尤其是涉及内存性本构关系的长期时间演化，计算代价高昂。现有方法的时间积分稳定性界往往依赖Gronwall不等式导致指数增长，限制了长时间仿真的可靠性和效率。因此需要发展一种既能保证稳定性和精度、又能大幅降低计算成本的降阶模型方法。

Method: 采用POD降阶建模框架：首先将广义Maxwell模型的记忆性本构律用内变量重构，消除积分型历史依赖；空间上用对称内惩罚间断伽辽金（SIPG）方法离散，时间上用向后欧拉或Crank-Nicolson格式离散；然后从位移快照中构建低维POD子空间，将全离散系统投影到该子空间得到降阶模型。理论分析方面，不借助Gronwall不等式证明全阶离散格式的稳定性界（随最终时间线性增长），并推导空间误差估计和分离空间离散、时间离散与POD截断的误差分解。

Result: 证明了全阶间断伽辽金格式的适定性和先验稳定性界，该界随最终时间至多线性增长。推导了空间误差估计和将空间离散、时间离散及POD截断效应分离的误差分解。数值实验验证了理论预测的收敛阶，评估了不同内积选择对约化基质量的影响，并在考虑快照生成和基构建成本的前提下，准确恢复了瞬态振荡和粘弹性松弛行为，同时实现了显著的计算节省。

Conclusion: 提出的POD降阶框架为动态线性粘弹性问题提供了一种高效且可靠的长时间仿真方案。通过避免Gronwall不等式的指数型稳定性界，保证了长期计算的数值稳定性；误差分解为各离散环节的贡献提供了清晰的理论指导；数值实验证实该方法在精度和计算效率之间达到了良好的平衡，适用于实际工程中长期粘弹性动力学仿真。

Abstract: We present a reduced-order modeling framework based on proper orthogonal decomposition for dynamic linear viscoelasticity governed by generalized Maxwell models. The hereditary constitutive law is reformulated using internal variables, and the resulting problem is discretized in space by a symmetric interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin method and in time by either the backward Euler or Crank--Nicolson scheme. The reduced model is obtained by projecting the fully discrete system onto low-dimensional spaces constructed from displacement snapshots. For the full-order discontinuous Galerkin formulation, we show well-posedness and derive an a priori stability bound without using Gronwall's inequality. Consequently, the bound grows at most linearly, rather than exponentially, with the final time. We further derive spatial error estimates and an error decomposition that separates the effects of spatial discretization, temporal discretization, and proper orthogonal decomposition truncation. The reduced formulation replaces the large full-order systems with low-dimensional problems, enabling efficient long-time simulations. Numerical experiments verify the predicted convergence behavior, assess different inner products for constructing the reduced basis, and demonstrate accurate recovery of transient oscillations and viscoelastic relaxation with substantial computational savings, even after accounting for snapshot generation and basis construction.

</details>


### [143] [A linear mass-lumped finite element method for the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation: unconditional energy dissipation and length preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26687)
*Qiumei Huang,Binghong Li,Xiaoli Li,Cheng Wang,Jiang Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对高度非线性的Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG)方程，在拟均匀三角网格上提出了一种线性、无条件能量耗散、质量集总的有限元方法。该方法通过投影策略结合混合有限元-有限差分框架，同时保证节点单位长度约束|m|=1和无条件能量稳定性，并建立了最优阶误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLG方程是铁磁材料微磁学中的基本方程，其求解面临两大核心挑战：(1)非凸点态约束|m|=1难以在标准有限元离散中精确保持；(2)该约束与无条件能量稳定性在同一离散框架下同时满足极为困难。需要一种既能保持几何约束又能保证能量耗散的结构保持数值方法。

Method: 提出混合有限元-有限差分统一框架：(1)利用质量集总有限元方法的弱形式和节点结构；(2)结合有限差分离散中的插值算子与逐节点长度保持机制；(3)通过投影策略强制执行非凸点态约束|m|=1；(4)在该统一框架下同时完成格式构造与误差分析，利用插值基和逐节点有限差分技术控制强非线性阻尼项。

Result: 得到线性格式，能够逐节点保持单位长度约束并满足离散能量耗散律；建立了最优阶误差估计；数值实验（包括经典爆破解模拟）验证了方法的精度、能量耗散性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法为受限耗散系统提供了统一的通用设计框架，系统性地将有限元方法的几何灵活性与有限差分方法的点态约束保持性质相整合，为构造和分析结构保持离散格式提供了普适性策略。

Abstract: We develop a linear, unconditionally energy-dissipative, mass-lumped finite element method for the highly nonlinear Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert (LLG) equation on quasi-uniform triangular meshes. The method is built on a projection strategy for enforcing the nonconvex pointwise constraint $|\mathbf{m}| = 1$, whose simultaneous preservation with unconditional energy stability remains challenging for standard finite element discretizations. The key innovation is a unified hybrid finite element-finite difference framework that underlies both the design and the analysis of the proposed method. In the scheme construction, we exploit the weak formulation and nodal structure of mass-lumped finite element method, while incorporating suitable interpolation operators and a node-wise length-preserving mechanism inspired by finite difference discretizations. This combination yields a linear scheme that preserves the node-wise unit-length constraint and satisfies a discrete energy dissipation law. The same hybrid framework also plays a central role in the error analysis, where the weak formulation and quasi-uniform mesh structure of finite elements are combined with interpolation-based and nodewise finite difference method to control the strongly nonlinear damping term and to establish an optimal-order error estimate. More importantly, the proposed method provides a unified framework that systematically integrates the geometric flexibility of finite element method with the pointwise constraint-preserving property of finite difference method, and thus offers a general strategy for designing and analyzing structure-preserving discretizations of constrained dissipative systems. Numerical experiments, including a classical blow-up simulation, confirm the predicted accuracy, energy dissipation, and robustness of the method.

</details>


### [144] [A Discontinuous Galerkin discretization for the sea ice dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26810)
*Emma Lagracie,Thomas Richter*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用完全间断伽辽金（DG）方法对Hibler黏塑性海冰模型进行空间离散，并对mEVP伪时间迭代求解器的收敛性进行了理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海冰动力学数值模拟具有挑战性，因为其非线性的冰流变学导致线性动力学特征（LKFs）的出现，而这些特征对空间分辨率和速度场离散化方式非常敏感。准确表示这些特征是必要的，因为它们影响热力学和海洋-大气交换过程。

Method: 采用完全间断伽辽金（DG）方法对所有变量（包括速度场）进行空间离散，利用DG元素表示不连续性及高阶局部多项式逼近的能力来解决复杂冰变形特征。同时使用成熟的基准测试评估该全DG方法，并对mEVP公式作为VP动量方程的伪时间迭代求解器进行理论收敛性分析。

Result: 通过与现有最新离散化方法对比基准测试表明了全DG方法在表示海冰变形方面的优势；理论分析证明了连续伪时间动力系统收敛到黏塑性形式极限，为mEVP作为Hibler VP模型迭代求解器提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 全DG方法适用于海冰动力学模拟，其对不连续性的表示能力有利于准确捕捉LKFs；同时mEVP方法的理论收敛性得到了严格证明，为实际应用提供了数学依据。

Abstract: Sea ice dynamics plays a crucial role in the Earth's climate system, making it an important component of weather and climate prediction models. Its numerical simulation remains challenging, however, as it exhibits complex mechanical behaviors due to a nonlinear ice rheology and interactions with external physical forcings. In particular, sea ice presents linear kinematic features (LKFs), i.e., narrow bands of intense deformation associated with processes such as lead opening or pressure-ridge formation. Accurately representing these features is necessary as they affect thermodynamics and ocean-atmosphere exchange. Yet their number, localization, and structure are highly sensitive to spatial resolution and to the chosen discretization of the sea ice velocity. In this work, we investigate the spatial discretization of Hibler's viscous plastic sea-ice model using a fully discontinuous Galerkin (DG) representation of all variables, including the velocity field. The ability of DG elements to represent discontinuities while having high order local polynomial approximation makes them well suited for resolving the complex ice deformation features, and insuring robustness towards mesh-induced numerical artefacts. We assess the fully DG method using an established sea ice dynamics benchmark and compare the obtained sea ice deformation with state-of-the-art discretizations. Additionally, we study the theoretical convergence of the modified Elastic-Viscous-Plastic (mEVP) formulation as a pseudo-time iterative solver for the viscous-plastic (VP) momentum equations. We prove the convergence of the underlying continuous pseudo-time dynamical system towards the viscous plastic formal limit, thereby providing a theoretical foundation for the use of mEVP as an iterative solver for Hibler's VP model.

</details>


### [145] [A fast solver for many-particle elastic scattering in layered media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26875)
*Jinrui Zhang,Yixiao He,Jun lai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于层状介质中多粒子时谐弹性散射问题的快速求解器，支持Dirichlet和Neumann边界条件，通过Sommerfeld积分表示层状介质贡献，结合边界积分公式、散射矩阵和多散射理论，并利用快速多极子方法加速大规模粒子系统的计算，在二维和三维中均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状介质中的多粒子弹性散射问题在复合材料优化、无损检测和地下成像等应用中非常重要，但由于粒子间强多重散射相互作用以及层状界面的存在，计算极具挑战性，亟需高效的数值求解方法。

Method: 该方法通过Sommerfeld积分表示层状介质的贡献，并将其与良态的边界积分公式耦合以处理粒子散射问题；采用高阶积分方程离散化和散射矩阵处理任意形状的粒子；利用多重散射理论高效描述粒子间相互作用；对于大规模粒子系统，引入快速多极子方法（FMM）加速计算。公式体系在二维和三维中均有发展。

Result: 针对刚性和无牵引粒子（即Dirichlet和Neumann边界条件）的数值实验验证了公式的精度，并展示了该方法在正演散射模拟和反散射应用中的灵活性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种准确、灵活且可扩展的求解框架，能够高效处理层状介质中多粒子的弹性散射问题，适用于正演模拟和反演应用。

Abstract: This paper proposes a fast solver for time-harmonic elastic scattering by multiple particles embedded in layered media, with either Dirichlet or Neumann boundary conditions imposed on the particle surfaces. Such problems arise in many important applications, including composite material optimization, nondestructive testing, and subsurface imaging. They are computationally challenging because of strong multiple scattering interactions among the particles and the layered interface. The proposed method represents the layered medium contribution through Sommerfeld integrals and couples this representation with a well-posed boundary integral formulation for the particle scattering problem. High-order integral equation discretization and scattering matrix are used to handle particles of general shape, while multiple scattering theory provides an efficient description of particle interactions. To reduce the cost for large particle systems, the resulting multiple scattering computation is further accelerated by the fast multipole method. The main formulation is developed in both two and three dimensions. Numerical experiments for rigid and traction-free particles validate the accuracy of the formulation, and demonstrate its flexibility in both direct scattering simulations and inverse scattering applications.

</details>


### [146] [QH-GEM: Quantum-Hydrodynamic Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27216)
*Harbir Antil,Alex Kaltenbach,Sarswati Shah*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自由粒子薛定谔方程Madelung公式的确定性物理约束生成框架，通过参考Born概率密度和可控初始相位函数生成动力学样本，将随机性仅限于初始采样，后续生成完全确定，并建立了末端分布匹配的PDE约束相位识别问题，给出了高斯波包的显式动力学和可达性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成模型（如扩散模型）依赖随机动力学或独立参数化的时变速度场，导致生成过程复杂且缺乏物理可解释性。本文旨在利用量子力学中的Madelung流体力学表述，构建一个确定性的、由物理约束驱动的生成框架，使随机性仅存在于初始采样阶段，从而简化生成过程并获得理论保证。

Method: 基于自由粒子薛定谔方程的Madelung公式，将Born概率密度与相位函数通过Bohm量子势耦合，相位函数决定流体动力学速度场。当概率密度恒正且速度场产生唯一全局特征流时，沿特征流传输的样本始终服从演化的Born概率密度。将末端分布匹配形式化为PDE约束的相位识别问题，推导了哈密顿量和Fisher信息结构；对各项同性高斯波包推导显式动力学和精确可达性充要条件，并证明特征流以O(T^2)误差逼近一阶输运映射。

Result: 对各项同性高斯波包获得了精确可达性条件与对应采样映射；对光滑给定势的初始速度场，建立了特征流对一阶输运映射的O(T^2)一致误差界及1-和2-Wasserstein距离误差界；数值高斯基准验证了全离散前向求解器的有效性，并在非对称双峰目标上演示了全网格PDE约束相位识别。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个介于常微分方程输运方法和随机生成模型之间的确定性生成框架，具有明确的物理约束和理论误差保证。高斯情形下的精确可达性分析和一般情形下的O(T^2)逼近误差表明该方法在理论和实践上均是可行的生成建模工具，未来可进一步扩展至更复杂的目标分布和高维场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a deterministic, physically constrained generative framework based on the Madelung formulation of the free-particle Schrödinger equation. A reference Born probability density and a controllable initial phase function serve as initial data for the free Madelung system, which couples the Born probability density and phase function through the Bohm quantum potential, while the phase function determines the hydrodynamic velocity field. Provided that the Born probability density remains positive and the hydrodynamic velocity field generates a unique global characteristic flow, samples drawn from the reference density and transported along the characteristic flow are distributed according to the evolving Born probability density at every time.
  As a consequence, randomness enters only through the initial sampling; the subsequent generation is deterministic and involves neither stochastic dynamics nor an independently parameterized time-dependent velocity field. We formulate terminal-time distribution matching as a PDE-constrained phase-identification problem and derive the underlying Hamiltonian and Fisher-information structure. For isotropic Gaussian wave packets, we obtain explicit dynamics and a necessary and sufficient condition for exact reachability of isotropic Gaussian targets by quadratic initial phase functions, together with the corresponding sampling map. For a smooth prescribed potential initial velocity field, we further establish that the characteristic flow approximates the associated first-order transport map with an O(T^2) error, both uniformly and in the 1- and 2-Wasserstein distances. A numerical Gaussian benchmark validates the fully discrete forward solver, while full-grid PDE-constrained phase identification is demonstrated for asymmetric bimodal targets.

</details>


### [147] [Spectral Convergence of the Multipole Expansion Method for Acoustic Scattering in Three Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26884)
*Jinrui Zhang,Jun Lai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了三维多散射问题中多极展开法（MEM）的谱收敛性，通过对角预条件单层公式和球谐能量空间中的截断系统分析，建立了收敛因子的几何与物理来源，为快速多极算法（FMM）等一大类快速算法的收敛性分析铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多极展开法是声学与电磁学中多散射问题的核心方法，也是快速多极方法等快速算法的理论基础。尽管这类算法广泛应用，但三维情况下MEM因涉及无穷多次波反射而导致的收敛性问题在此前一直未被严格证明，缺乏理论保证。

Method: 采用对角预条件单层公式，在自然的球谐能量空间中分析N阶截断系统。将相互作用截断分为目标侧和源侧高次项两部分，分别采用投影格林核表示和位移球谐波族的逐阶估计进行刻画。基于Parseval恒等式和球谐加法定理构建统一分析框架，并通过首次传递分析获得更尖锐的估计。

Result: 证明了时间谐波声学散射中有限个良分离球体的MEM具有谱收敛性；通过首次传递分析获得了更尖锐的收敛估计；数值实验验证了预测的谱衰减速率和几何收敛因子。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了MEM收敛性分析的一般框架，揭示了收敛因子的几何与物理起源，为一大类基于多极展开的多散射快速算法的收敛性分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multiple scattering is a fundamental wave interaction phenomenon in acoustics and electromagnetics. The multipole expansion method (MEM) is the basis of many fast algorithms, such as the fast multipole method (FMM), for such problems. However, due to the infinitely many wave reflections involved, its convergence in three dimensions remains unexplored. In this paper, we prove spectral convergence of the MEM for time-harmonic acoustic scattering by finitely many well-separated spheres in three dimensions. Using a diagonally preconditioned single-layer formulation, we analyze the degree-$N$ truncated system in a natural spherical harmonic energy space. We split the interaction truncation into target-side and source-side high-degree parts and choose a different representation for each: a projected Green-kernel representation for the former and degree-wise estimates of a translated spherical wave family for the latter. The resulting argument, based on Parseval's identity and the spherical harmonic addition theorem, provides a general framework for the convergence analysis of MEM and reveals the geometric and physical origins of the convergence factors. We also obtain a sharper estimate through the first-transfer analysis. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted spectral decay and the geometric convergence factor. This paves the way for the convergence analysis of a large class of fast algorithms for multiple scattering.

</details>


### [148] [Waveguiding in systems of high contrast resonators: Theory and fast computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26906)
*Habib Ammari,Bowen Li,Borui Miao,Jiayu Qiu,Lara Vrabac*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究高对比度谐振子系统中非零内部Neumann频率附近的导波模式（超越亚波长区域）。通过压缩外部Helmholtz Dirichlet-to-Neumann算子到内部谐振Neumann特征空间的迹，构造了无限维频率依赖的电容算子，证明了对比度趋于零时连续问题到离散有效算子的范数-预解收敛，并给出了第一阶渐近公式。利用Combes-Thomas论证建立了电容系数的指数远对角衰减，结合复数吸收与Hermitian对称化，实现了快速计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越亚波长区域研究高对比度谐振子中的导波模式，现有方法在内部Neumann频率附近面临外部问题不适定的挑战。作者旨在建立严格有效的离散模型，既能精确逼近连续问题，又能实现快速数值计算，同时克服内部Neumann频率处的外部Dirichlet问题的不适定性。

Method: 1) 在规则外部区域（参考波数处外部Dirichlet问题适定）引入无限维频率依赖电容算子，通过压缩外部Helmholtz Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射至内部谐振Neumann特征空间；2) 证明范数-预解收敛性并推导第一阶渐近公式；3) 用Combes-Thomas论证证明电容系数的指数远对角衰减；4) 引入复数吸收与Hermitian对称化消除稳定性假设；5) 选取吸收参数O(√δ)及截断/修补半径O(|logδ|)保持O(δ²)精度。

Result: 建立了指数精度的离散有效算子截断，其保留系数可由局部Helmholtz问题计算。在物理频率下，均匀稳定性假设下局部近似指数收敛。通过复数吸收和Hermitian对称化可移除稳定性假设，吸收参数O(√δ)、截断与修补半径O(|logδ|)足以保持第一阶高对比度展开的O(δ²)精度。数值实验表明该离散模型对偶极和四极谐振的精度、指数局域性及对直/弯曲波导的适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作将高对比度谐振子的离散有效模型从亚波长区域推广到非零Neumann频率附近，提供了严格的收敛性证明和快速计算方法。通过指数局域性的揭示和复数吸收技巧，实现了对缺陷频率与线缺陷带函数的高精度、高效数值求解，为材料或几何失谐产生的波导设计提供了理论保障。

Abstract: In this work, we study guided modes in systems of high-contrast resonators near nonzero interior Neumann frequencies, beyond the subwavelength regime. In the regular exterior regime, where the exterior Dirichlet problem is well-posed at the reference wavenumber, we introduce an infinite-dimensional frequency-dependent capacitance operator obtained by compressing the exterior Helmholtz Dirichlet-to-Neumann map to the traces of the interior resonant Neumann eigenspaces. We prove the norm-resolvent convergence of the continuous problem to this discrete effective operator as the contrast $δ\to0$, and derive first-order asymptotic formulas for compact-defect frequencies and line-defect band functions. We then establish exponential off-diagonal decay of the capacitance coefficients by a Combes--Thomas argument, yielding an exponentially accurate truncation of the discrete operator, and show that its retained coefficients can be computed from local Helmholtz problems. At the physical frequency, this local approximation converges exponentially under a uniform stability assumption for the growing finite-cluster problems. The stability assumption can be removed by introducing a vanishing complex absorption together with a Hermitian symmetrization. In particular, an absorption parameter of order $\sqrtδ$, together with interaction truncation and patch radii of order $|\logδ|$, suffices to preserve the $O(δ^2)$ accuracy of the first-order high-contrast expansion of the defect eigenfrequencies, yielding a fast computational method. Numerical experiments for dipole and quadrupole resonances illustrate the accuracy, exponential locality, and applicability of the discrete model to straight and bent waveguides generated by material or geometric detuning.

</details>


### [149] [Stiff Norm Estimates for Variable-Stepsize Peer Two-Step Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26913)
*Jens Lang,Bernhard A. Schmitt*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对刚性半线性系统，发展了变步长对角隐式L(α)-稳定Peer两步法的传播算子严格范数估计。通过构造具有网格无关左特征向量的Peer方法族，推导出统一的稳定性界，并构造了高达5阶且阶段阶达到s的方法，突破了经典不可约s级对角隐式Runge-Kutta方法q≤2的阶段阶壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Peer两步法结合了Runge-Kutta方法与多步法的优点，包括高阶精度、强稳定性以及避免阶数下降。然而，对于刚性半线性系统在变步长网格下的传播算子缺乏严格的范数估计，这限制了其理论分析深度和在最优控制、边值问题等领域的进一步应用。因此需要发展一个严格的稳定性分析框架。

Method: 论文提出一类具有网格无关左特征向量的Peer方法族，利用该性质为整个系数族构造合适的矩阵范数。在此基础上，对变步长对角隐式L(α)-稳定Peer方法针对刚性半线性系统的传播算子建立严格范数估计，并构造具有阶段阶q=s且具有s+1阶超收敛性质的s级方法（最高到5阶）。

Result: 证明了该方法类包含具有阶段阶q=s和超收敛阶s+1的L(α)-稳定方法，突破了经典Dirk方法阶段阶上限q≤2的壁垒，并实际构造了高达5阶的方法。对三个经典刚性基准问题进行了比较数值实验，包括3个新Peer两步方法（3、4、5阶）和4个带首次显式步的SDIRK方法（阶段阶2，整体阶3、4、5、6），验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该理论为刚性半线性问题在变网格上提供了统一的稳定性界，建立了Peer方法传播算子范数估计的严格基础，为后续在最优控制和相关边值问题中的进一步应用奠定了基础。高阶段阶的Peer方法在刚性微分方程中有效缓解阶数下降问题，展示了其在实践中的优势。

Abstract: Peer two-step methods are attractive for the numerical solution of stiff initial value problems because they combine favorable features of Runge-Kutta and multi-step methods, including high-order accuracy, strong stability properties, and the avoidance of order reduction. This paper develops rigorous norm estimates for the propagation operators of variable-stepsize diagonally-implicit $L(α)$-stable Peer methods applied to stiff semi-linear systems. The analysis relies on a class of Peer methods whose propagation matrices possess a grid-independent left eigenvector, enabling the construction of suitable matrix norms for entire coefficient families. We show that this class contains $L(α)$-stable methods of stage order $q=s$ and super-convergence of order $s+1$ with $s$ stages, exceeding the classical stage-order barrier $q\le 2$ of irreducible $s$-stage diagonally-implicit Runge-Kutta methods, and construct such methods up to order 5. Achieving high stage order is one of the principal advantages of Peer methods, especially for stiff differential equations where it helps mitigate order reduction. The resulting theory provides uniform stability bounds for stiff semi-linear problems on variable grids and establishes a foundation for further applications to optimal control and related boundary value problems. Comparative numerical results are presented for three classical stiff benchmark problems, including three novel Peer two-step methods of order 3, 4, 5, and four singly diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta methods with a first explicit step of stage order 2 and order 3, 4, 5, and 6.

</details>


### [150] [Scale-uniform inverse inequalities for scaled kernel spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26945)
*Davoud Mirzaei*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了尺度一致的逆不等式，用于由缩放正定核生成的空间（其原生空间为Sobolev空间）。在核尺度δ→0时保持常数受控，首先在有界域上建立了尺度依赖Sobolev空间中的逆估计，进而得到以L²为较弱范数的标准Sobolev范数下的尺度一致逆不等式；随后在整空间上建立了更一般的尺度一致Bernstein不等式。证明直接使用核空间中函数的Fourier表示，结合低-高频分解和分离指数多项式的框架估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆不等式是核逼近方法稳定性与收敛性分析的重要工具。在多尺度设置下，试验空间随核尺度δ变化，固定核的逆估计不再适用，常数必须在δ→0时保持受控。因此需要建立尺度一致的逆不等式。

Method: 首先在尺度依赖的Sobolev空间中建立逆估计；在有界域上导出了以L²为较弱范数的标准Sobolev范数下的尺度一致逆不等式。然后在整空间上建立了更一般的尺度一致Bernstein不等式，覆盖更广泛的Sobolev指标范围。证明直接基于核空间中函数的Fourier表示，采用低-高频分解与分离指数多项式的框架估计相结合，避免了分别比较缩放与标准Sobolev范数时产生的额外尺度因子。

Result: 建立了尺度一致逆不等式，其要求分离距离上界为核尺度的固定倍数，允许核尺度比分离距离下降得更慢（即多尺度细化场景）。在整空间上建立了更一般的尺度一致Bernstein不等式，覆盖广泛的强弱Sobolev指标组合。

Conclusion: 该工作为多尺度核逼近方法提供了新的分析工具，通过Fourier表示的直证方法消除了额外尺度因子，使得逆/ Bernstein不等式在多尺度细化过程中保持常数受控，适用于核尺度下降慢于分离距离的实际场景。

Abstract: Inverse inequalities are an important tool in the stability and convergence analysis of kernel approximation methods. In a multiscale setting, however, the trial space changes with the kernel scale $δ$, and inverse estimates for a fixed kernel are not sufficient. The constants must remain controlled as $δ\to0$. In this paper, we study scale-uniform inverse inequalities for spaces generated by scaled positive definite kernels whose native spaces are Sobolev spaces. We first establish inverse estimates in scale-dependent Sobolev spaces. On bounded domains, this leads to a scale-uniform inverse inequality in standard Sobolev norms with $L^2$ as the weaker norm. The estimate requires only that the separation distance of the centers be bounded above by a fixed multiple of the kernel scale. This allows the kernel scale to decrease more slowly than the separation distance, as is relevant in multiscale refinement. We then establish more general scale-uniform Bernstein inequalities on the whole space. Our result covers a broad range of weaker and stronger Sobolev indices under the same relation between the separation distance and the kernel scale. The proof works directly with the Fourier representation of functions in the kernel space and combines a low-high frequency decomposition with frame estimates for separated exponential polynomials. This avoids the additional scale factors that arise from separate comparisons of scaled and standard Sobolev norms.

</details>


### [151] [A quantum-assisted framework for PDE-based Bayesian inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27028)
*Dong An,Yinan Li,Pucheng Tang,Yunfeng Xiong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个量子-经典混合框架用于贝叶斯偏微分方程（PDE）反演问题。量子处理器演化PDE并用Hadamard测试评估归一化量子态损失函数（仅需有限数量的量子态拷贝即可提取经典信息），经典计算机通过高斯过程回归调参探索下一候选。实验表明该数据失配函数在量子测量下优于传统L2损失，并在1D/2D线性对流扩散方程和非线性受迫粘性Burgers方程上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有量子PDE求解器专注于制备解的量子态，但从这些量子态中高效恢复经典信息（即读出限制）的研究仍较少。作者受这一读出瓶颈的驱动，旨在解决量子PDE求解器在反演问题中如何以有限的量子态拷贝有效提取经典信息的问题。

Method: 提出量子-经典混合框架：量子处理器负责演化PDE并通过Hadamard测试评估归一化量子态损失作为数据失配函数（针对线性和半线性自治演化PDE），无需重建完整解向量，仅需有限量子态拷贝；经典计算机使用高斯过程回归（GPR）调整超参数以探索下一个试验候选。同时分析了误差传播与给定精度下的损失评估总体复杂度。

Result: 误差传播和复杂度分析表明，新提出的数据失配函数在量子测量下优于传统L2损失。量子电路模拟（1D和2D线性对流扩散方程，含近似和有限采样损失评估）以及经典数值实验（非线性受迫粘性Burgers方程）证明即使在损失评估受采样噪声影响的情况下，该框架仍能实现参数反演。

Conclusion: 该框架为PDE反演问题提供了一种可行的量子辅助方案，并阐明了量子PDE算法在端到端（量子到经典）完整优化栈中的潜力。

Abstract: Quantum computing offers potential advantages for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, most existing quantum PDE solvers primarily focus on preparing quantum states for solutions, while the efficient recovery of classical information from these states remains less explored. Motivated by the readout limitation, we propose a quantum-classical hybrid framework for Bayesian PDE inversion problems: The quantum processor evolves the PDE and evaluate the loss function with sampling noises, while the classical computer tunes the hyper-parameters in the Gaussian Process Regression to explore the next trial candidate. To match the quantum solvers for linear and semi-linear autonomous evolution PDEs, we suggest to use a normalized quantum-state loss as the data-misfit function and evaluate the new misfit by combining quantum PDE solvers with the Hadamard test, thereby allowing us to extract useful classical information using only a limited number of quantum state copies without reconstructing the full solution vector. The analysis of error propagation and overall complexity of loss evaluation under a prescribed accuracy shows that the new data-misfit function outperforms the conventional L2-loss under quantum measurements. Quantum circuit simulations of 1D and 2D linear convection diffusion equations under approximate and finite sampling loss evaluations, together with classical numerical experiments on a nonlinear forced viscous Burgers equation, demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach for parameter inversion even when the loss evaluations are affected by sampling noise. This framework may provide a viable quantum-assisted scheme for PDE-based inverse problems and elucidate the potential of quantum PDE algorithms in addressing a complete quantum-to-end optimization stack.

</details>


### [152] [Primal-dual methods and acceleration for Morozov and equality constrained regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27106)
*Diana-Elena Mirciu,Martin Benning,Elena Resmerita*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对求解含噪线性逆问题的非加速与加速原始-对偶方法进行了正则化分析，在Hilbert空间中分别对Condat-Vũ算法和加速原始-对偶混合梯度法推导了Bregman距离和范数意义下的误差估计，并将非加速方法推广至Banach空间（含非自反情形）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性逆问题在实际中常伴随噪声数据，现有正则化分析大多局限于经典框架，缺乏对原始-对偶算法的系统正则化理论。作者希望量化数据扰动对重构误差的影响，并将分析推广到更一般的凸数据保真项、非欧几里得几何和非自反空间等更广泛的设定中。

Method: 对Condat-Vũ算法在Hilbert空间中利用Bregman距离建立误差估计；对加速原始-对偶混合梯度法在范数意义下建立误差估计。研究适用于满足特定扰动条件的一般凸数据保真项（显式验证了等式约束和Morozov正则化情形）。非加速方法的分析进一步扩展至Banach空间，考虑非欧几里得几何并包含一个针对非负解重构的非自反特定设定。

Result: 在经典设定下恢复已知行为，并将正则化分析推广至更一般的框架。数值实验采用稀疏性和熵等代表性正则子，验证了理论结论并展示了噪声条件下的实际性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为原始-对偶方法在含噪线性逆问题中的正则化行为提供了统一的定量刻画，涵盖Hilbert与Banach空间、非加速与加速算法，并通过数值实验验证了理论的实用性与有效性。

Abstract: This work develops a regularization analysis of non-accelerated and accelerated primal-dual methods for solving linear inverse problems in the presence of noisy data. We investigate a Condat-Vũ algorithm and an accelerated primal-dual hybrid gradient method in Hilbert spaces, with focus on quantifying the effect of data perturbations on the reconstruction error. For the non-accelerated scheme, we derive error estimates in terms of Bregman distances, whereas for the accelerated scheme we establish error estimates in norm. The study accommodates a general class of convex data fidelities satisfying suitable perturbation conditions, which are verified explicitly for equality constrained and Morozov regularization. For the non-accelerated method, the analysis is further extended to Banach spaces, taking into account non-Euclidean geometries and including a particular non-reflexive setting tailored to nonnegative solution reconstruction. The results recover known behavior in classical settings while extending the regularization analysis to these more general frameworks. Numerical experiments with representative regularizers, including sparsity and entropic models, support the theoretical findings and illustrate practical performance under noise.

</details>


### [153] [A multi-class kinetic traffic flow model: discrete-velocity formulation and diffusively-corrected macroscopic limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27159)
*Carmen Mezquita-Nieto,Paola Goatin,Axel Klar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个非局部Prigogine-Herman框架下离散速度动力学交通流模型的多类扩展，推导并分析了其双曲缩放系统、离散化结构性质（双曲性与完全线性退化），采用路径守恒有限体积格式进行数值逼近，并推导了相应的扩散修正宏观多类模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展经典的Prigogine-Herman动力学交通流模型到多类车辆场景，以更真实地描述不同类型车辆（如轿车、卡车）之间的相互作用，同时保持模型良好的数学结构性质和数值可解性。

Method: 从连续动力学公式出发推导双曲缩放的多类系统；对任意数量车辆类别进行速度变量离散化；证明模型的非线性结构性质（双曲性和完全线性退化）；采用路径守恒有限体积格式处理非守恒结构；推导扩散修正的宏观多类模型并进行稳定性分析。

Result: 成功构建了多类离散速度动力学模型，证明了系统的双曲性和完全线性退化性质；路径守恒数值格式有效逼近非守恒系统；扩散修正模型满足稳定性条件；单车道数值模拟验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为多类交通流的动力学建模提供了理论与数值兼备的完整框架，兼顾了数学严谨性（双曲性、线性退化、稳定性）与实用数值模拟能力。

Abstract: This paper introduces a multi-class extension of a discrete-velocity kinetic traffic flow model based on a non-local Prigogine-Herman framework. We derive a hyperbolically scaled system of equations from a continuous kinetic formulation describing interactions between different vehicle classes through braking and relaxation terms. The model is then discretized with respect to the velocity variable for an arbitrary number of vehicle classes, and the structural properties of the resulting formulation are analyzed. In particular, we prove hyperbolicity and total linear degeneracy. Due to the non-conservative structure of the model, we employ a path-conservative finite volume scheme for the numerical approximation of the system. Finally, we derive the corresponding diffusively-corrected macroscopic multi-class model, investigate its stability and present numerical simulations on a single-lane road to illustrate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [154] [Bochner Stability for B-stable DIRK Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27210)
*Anthony E. Ramirez,Abner J. Salgado*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了二阶段和三阶段对角隐式Runge-Kutta（DIRK）格式中U-稳定性与B-（代数）稳定性之间的等价性，从而为B-稳定方法提供了合适的Bochner范数先验估计，并将其应用于线性强制发展问题和梯度流的收敛性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前，Salgado和Tomas引入了DIRK格式的U-稳定性概念，但其与其他稳定性概念（如B-代数稳定性）之间的关系尚不明确。本文旨在建立U-稳定性和B-稳定性之间的等价关系，以便利用B-稳定方法的成熟理论为U-稳定方法提供Bochner范数先验估计，进而推动其在更广泛问题中的应用。

Method: 本文首先针对二阶段和三阶段DIRK格式，严格证明了U-稳定性与B-（代数）稳定性之间的等价性。基于这一等价性，推导了B-稳定方法在Bochner范数下的先验估计。随后，利用能量估计和紧致性方法，证明了U-稳定DIRK格式在最小正则性条件下对线性强制演化问题的收敛性。最后，将这些离散格式应用于梯度流问题，推导出局部能量耗散不等式，并构造反例来阐明阶段级能量单调性的局限性。

Result: （1）建立了二阶段和三阶段DIRK格式中U-稳定性与B-代数稳定性的等价关系；（2）获得了B-稳定方法的Bochner范数先验估计；（3）在最小正则性假设下证明了U-稳定DIRK格式对线性强制演化问题的收敛性；（4）为梯度流推导了离散局部能量耗散不等式；（5）构造反例揭示了阶段级能量单调性在一般情况下不成立。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立U-稳定性与B-稳定性在低阶DIRK格式中的等价性，弥合了这两类稳定性概念之间的理论鸿沟，为B-稳定方法提供了丰富的分析工具，并将这些结果成功应用于演化问题的收敛性证明和梯度流的能量耗散分析。

Abstract: In Abner J. Salgado and Ignacio Tomas. Diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta schemes: discrete energy-balance laws and compactness properties. J. Number. Math., 31(4):313-341, 2023, the notion of $U$-stability for Diagonally Implicit Runge-Kutta (DIRK) schemes was introduced. Here we establish the equivalence between $U$- and $B$- (algebraic) stability for two- and three-stage DIRK schemes, which then} provides suitable Bochner norm a priori estimates for $B$-stable methods. As applications, we first prove the convergence of $U$-stable DIRK schemes for linear coercive evolution problems under minimal regularity via energy estimates and compactness. Second, we apply these discretizations to gradient flows, which allow us to derive discrete local energy dissipation inequalities and provide counterexamples that demonstrate the limitations of stagewise energy monotonicity.

</details>


### [155] [Enforcing Dirichlet Boundary Conditions in Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27256)
*Andrew M. Stuart,Margaret Trautner*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的神经算子架构，通过在每一层输出中强制包含在拉普拉斯算子齐次Dirichlet特征函数张成的子空间中，从而在不依赖训练数据的情况下独立满足齐次Dirichlet边界条件，同时对输出域仅要求有界且Lipschitz边界，不使用均匀网格或可分盒状域的限制。作者证明了该架构的普适逼近性质，并通过2D PDE（Darcy流和Helmholtz方程）实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经算子架构大多通过训练数据间接学习边界条件，而显式强制边界条件的修改方案存在实际限制，包括需要边界光滑、均匀网格以及可分盒状域。这些限制严重阻碍了神经算子在一般几何和任意网格数据上的应用，因此需要一种既能显式满足边界条件又不牺牲灵活性和表达力的架构。

Method: 该架构的核心思想是：在每一层的输出中强制满足输出域上Laplacian的齐次Dirichlet特征函数子集中的跨度包含性质，从而保证整个网络输出独立于训练过程满足齐次Dirichlet边界条件。该方法仅要求输出域有界且具有Lipschitz边界，对离散化选择无任何限制，适用于任意网格数据和一般几何。此外，作者证明了该架构的普适逼近性质，并且所采用的分析方法还统一了广泛核积分神经算子的现有理论。

Result: 作者在2D PDE的算子映射上验证了该方法的有效性，具体包括：方格域上的Darcy流（系数到解映射）和圆形域上的Helmholtz方程，并与替代方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种同时满足显式边界条件约束和保持核积分神经算子表达力的架构，消除了对网格均匀性和域形状的限制，并通过普适逼近定理和实验验证表明其在一般几何和任意网格上的适用性。

Abstract: Operator learning in scientific machine learning is concerned with approximation of maps between infinite-dimensional function spaces; such maps frequently arise as the solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs). Neural operators have demonstrated broad empirical success at approximating such maps from data. However, most existing neural operator architectures enforce boundary conditions indirectly through training from data even though the boundary condition is often known exactly. Furthermore, existing modifications and approaches that do enforce boundary conditions explicitly suffer from impractical restrictions, including boundary smoothness, uniform grids, and separable, box-like domains. In this work, we propose an architecture which, independently of training, satisfies homogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions, whilst simultaneously retaining the expressivity of existing kernel-integral neural operator architectures. This is achieved by enforcing the property that the output of each layer is contained in the span of a subset of the homogeneous Dirichlet eigenfunctions of the Laplacian on the output domain. The method requires only that the output domain be bounded with Lipschitz boundary and places no restriction on the choice of discretization, making it applicable to arbitrary mesh data and general geometries. We prove universal approximation for the resulting architecture; furthermore the approach we adopt in the analysis proves universality for a broad class of kernel-integral neural operators thereby uniting existing theory for a variety of operator learning methods. We validate the proposed method on maps defined by the coefficient to solution map in 2D PDEs: Darcy flow on a square domain and the Helmholtz equation on a circular domain. Comparisons are made with alternative methods.

</details>


### [156] [A Multilevel Interacting Particle System Method for the estimation of Failure Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27275)
*Rubén Aylwin,José Pinto*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将多层次分解与交互粒子系统框架相结合的新方法，用于计算给定感兴趣量超过或低于预设阈值的概率。通过序贯采样方案，该算法实现了比经典多层次蒙特卡洛更快的收敛速度，与当前最先进技术相当，且具有维度无关性。算法生成的样本点集中在感兴趣量的临界值附近。理论结果得到严格证明，并通过数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算某些感兴趣量超过或低于设定阈值的概率（即尾部概率或稀有事件概率）在许多应用中具有重要意义。经典的多层次蒙特卡洛（MLMC）方法在此类计算中收敛速度有限，需要开发更高效的算法来计算此类概率，同时保持维度无关性。

Method: 将多层次分解（multilevel decomposition）与交互粒子系统（Interacting Particle Systems）框架相结合。算法采用序贯采样方案（sequential sampling scheme），通过粒子系统在不同层次间的相互作用来高效估计感兴趣量跨越预设阈值的概率。

Result: 该算法实现了比经典多层次蒙特卡洛更快的收敛速率，与当前最先进技术相当，且具有维度无关性。生成的样本点序列集中在临界值附近，有助于提高估计效率。理论结果经严格证明并通过数值实验验证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的结合多层次分解与交互粒子系统的新方法，为计算阈值概率提供了一种高效、维度无关的算法，在收敛速度上优于经典MLMC并可与最先进技术竞争，是稀有事件概率估计领域的一项重要进展。

Abstract: We propose a novel method that combines a multilevel decomposition with the framework of Interacting Particle Systems to compute the probability that given quantities of interest take values above or below a certain pre-specified value. Through the application of a sequential sampling scheme, the algorithm is able to achieve convergence rates faster than those achieved by the classical Multilevel Monte Carlo algorithm, and competes with the current state of the art techniques, while also being dimension independent. Furthermore, the sample points generated by the algorithm form a sequence which concentrates near the critical value of the quantity of interest. Our results are rigorously established and later verified through numerical experiments.

</details>

### [157] [Quadratic Complexity of Voronoi Diagrams in $\mathbb{R}^3$ for Lines in a Single Ruling of a Regulus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27114)
*Eunku Park*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了欧氏度量下，所有n条线属于一个光滑双直纹实二次曲面同一族直纹线的最近与最远Voronoi图。证明在一般位置假设下，两类图至多有4n(n-3)个顶点和O(n²)的总组合复杂度；构造性地证明最近Voronoi图的最坏情形复杂度为Θ(n²)，最远图对任意一般位置输入均为Θ(n²)。提出O(n²)时间精确算法，可枚举所有有限顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意直线站点的最近Voronoi图组合复杂度已知仅介于Ω(n²)和O(n^{3+ε})之间，存在巨大间隙。作者希望通过限制直线站点满足特定几何结构（同属一个双直纹二次曲面的同一族直纹），来获得精确的复杂度刻画。

Method: 利用Plücker嵌入将直纹族映射为一条圆锥曲线，将直线与欧氏球相切的条件化为二元四次式。在正则顶点处，四个支撑参数恰好是其根，符号交替迫使参数圆上出现两段无站点弧，从而将可能的循环支撑类型限制为n(n-3)/2种；再利用Bézout定理将每种类型的中心数量上界限定为8。对于最坏情形构造，使用固定非旋转单叶双曲面的一族直纹构建一般位置站点集。

Result: 最近与最远Voronoi图在一般位置下至多有4n(n-3)个顶点和O(n²)总组合复杂度；对每个n≥4，存在一般位置n条线使得最近图至少有(n-2)(n-3)/2个不同的正则最近顶点，因此最近图最坏复杂度为Θ(n²)；最远图由于恰好有n(n-1)个三维胞腔，复杂度恒为Θ(n²)。同时给出O(n²)时间的精确枚举算法。

Conclusion: 在双直纹二次曲面的同一族直纹这一受限几何类别中，最近Voronoi图的最坏情形复杂度被精确确定为Θ(n²)，消除了任意直线站点场景中Ω(n²)与O(n^{3+ε})之间的间隙。该结果结合了代数几何（Plücker嵌入、Bézout定理）、符号交替论证以及精确算法设计。

Abstract: We study nearest and farthest Voronoi diagrams of lines in $\mathbb{R}^3$ under the Euclidean metric when all $n$ lines belong to one ruling of a smooth doubly ruled real quadric. For arbitrary line sites, the combinatorial complexity of the nearest Voronoi diagram is known only to lie between $Ω(n^2)$ and $O(n^{3+\varepsilon})$. Under general-position assumptions, we prove that both diagrams in the ruling class have at most $4n(n-3)$ vertices and $O(n^2)$ total combinatorial complexity. Conversely, for every $n \ge 4$, one ruling of a fixed non-rotational one-sheeted hyperboloid contains a general-position set of $n$ lines with at least $(n-2)(n-3)/2$ distinct regular nearest vertices, where regular means that exactly four lines support the vertex and their three defining bisectors meet transversely. Thus the worst-case complexity of the nearest Voronoi diagram in this class is $Θ(n^2)$, while the farthest diagram has $Θ(n^2)$ complexity for every general-position input, since it has exactly $n(n-1)$ three-dimensional cells. Under the Plücker embedding, the ruling is a conic, and the condition for a line to be tangent to a Euclidean sphere restricts to a binary quartic. At a regular vertex, the four supporting parameters exhaust its roots, and sign alternation forces two arcs of the parameter circle to be site-free. This leaves only $n(n-3)/2$ possible cyclic support types, while Bézout's theorem bounds the number of centers for each type by eight. The same reduction yields an exact $O(n^2)$-time algorithm that, after cyclically sorting the site parameters, enumerates all finite nearest and farthest vertices as constant-degree real univariate representations.

</details>


### [158] [Lunar Generalizations of the Euclidean Minimum Spanning Tree in the Plane and their Expected Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27118)
*Ondřej Draganov,Herbert Edelsbrunner,Sophie Rosenmeier,Morteza Saghafian*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文受彩色持续同调（chromatic persistent homology）的启发，将欧几里得最小生成树（EMST）推广到"lunar EMST"，用于处理s+1种颜色的点集，并证明其期望代价收敛到某个常数乘以√n（当n趋于无穷时）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的欧几里得最小生成树（EMST）已被广泛研究，但其彩色版本（即考虑点带有多种颜色）尚未被充分探索。受彩色持续同调的引入所启发，需要建立一种能够反映彩色点集几何结构的生成树概念，并分析其渐近性质。

Method: 将R²中n个点（分为s+1种颜色）的EMST推广为lunar EMST。定义"lune"为以不同颜色点为圆心、半径为r的s+1个圆盘的交集；该推广的EMST刻画了当r从0增长到∞时lune并集的历史演化过程。树的代价定义为弧与节点形成时半径之差的二倍。在[0,1]²上均匀随机选取点并随机着色，研究期望代价的渐近行为。

Result: 证明了当n趋于无穷时，期望代价收敛到某个依赖于s的常数乘以√n；但类似经典EMST的情形，这个常数的精确值仍然难以确定。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于证明了lunar EMST期望代价的渐近常数存在性，填补了彩色生成树理论中的一个关键理论空白，但其精确值的求解仍然是一个开放问题。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent introduction of chromatic persistent homology, we generalize the Euclidean minimum spanning tree (EMST) for $n$ points in $\mathbb{R}^2$ to the lunar EMST for the case in which the points come in $s+1$ colors. Calling the intersection of $s+1$ disks of radius $r$ centered at points with pairwise different colors a \emph{lune}, the generalized EMST reflects the history of the union of lunes as $r$ goes from $0$ to $\infty$, and its \emph{cost} is twice the difference between the radii when the arcs and nodes of the tree are formed. If the points are chosen uniformly at random in $[0,1]^2$ and colored randomly, the expected cost converges to some constant (that depends on $s$) times $\sqrt{n}$, as $n$ goes to infinity. The main contribution of this paper is a proof that this constant exists, however similar to the case of the classic EMST, its precise value remains elusive.

</details>
